Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.11

Chapter 3405
After entering the Immortal Painting, the creatures of the Nirvana Realm all changed greatly. This "fox" moved through the maze like a normal Nirvana Realm creature. Naturally, no one doubted her and treated her as a Nirvana Realm creature.
When Luo Zheng said this, everyone's suspicions immediately increased greatly.
"You're a Curly Beast, right?" Luo Zheng asked.
The fox stood not far away and said with disdain, "That's the name you idiots gave me."
As an intelligent creature, she was very dissatisfied with this name.
"But it doesn't matter," the fox laughed again, "You're going to die just like the thousands of idiots before you."
As she said that, she waved her hand in the painting.
The two snakes that had blocked the way out slowly wriggled in the tunnel.
The snake Xie Qi was obviously under her control …
"Tens of thousands …" Fei Yu was shocked, "You killed thousands of people?"
"Maybe, but it should be more than that. I forgot," the fox said with a faint smile.
"You used the snake Xie Qi to kill them? That's impossible. The snake Xie Qi only appeared a few times. "
Kuai Jian questioned.
According to the historical records of Ping Yi Heaven, the snake Xie Qi only appeared five times. Only one of them was completely wiped out. The other four times, they didn't succeed. Those people chose to give up, and no second Xie Qi appeared.
"To kill you idiots, must I need Xie Qi?" The fox said sarcastically, "If you have any memory, you've also been completely wiped out in the Immortal Painting many times."
The test in the Immortal Painting was not smooth sailing. Although many people would choose to give up when they felt that it was difficult, there were still many people who wanted to force their way through.
Sometimes, when the Immortal Painting opened once, 30%, 50% would die. Sometimes, it was very abnormal that they would all die in the Immortal Painting. The reason for their death was unknown.
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation The creatures of the Nirvana Realm in Pingyi Heaven were all terrified of the Great Serpent Xie Qi as well as several other powerful curly beasts. However, they only knew about these curly beasts because someone had seen them and left the Immortal Painting alive.
They recorded it.
And in the Immortal Painting, there was a Curly Beast that had never been recorded, because those who saw her were all dead.
"Then what do we do now?" A Nirvana Realm creature trembled.
"Of course we wait for death," the fox replied.
"We'll kill you first!"
One of the Nirvana Realm creatures couldn't hold back his nerves and charged towards the fox.
The moment the Nirvana Realm creature approached the fox, the fox's body suddenly became small, like a projection of an object on the ground. It moved away, disappeared, and then suddenly reappeared!
Next, the Nirvana Realm creature was covered by the fox. Without seeing how the fox attacked, the Nirvana Realm creature was cut in half.
If Luo Zheng could observe from a higher perspective, he would see that the fox had jumped out of the second page of the immortal scroll in an instant. It flipped in the air, crossed its hands left and right on the second page of the immortal scroll, and gently tore it.
The painting of the Nirvana Realm creatures below her was split into two from the middle …
"It's the ability of Insect Tapir." Luo Zheng frowned.
Fei Yu, Kuai Jian, and the others also noticed it.
They originally wanted to swarm up and subdue the fox, but the strength that the fox displayed was equally terrifying. Even if so many of them joined forces to fight the tapir, it was not something that could be suppressed in a short period of time.
"Who else wants to die?" The fox's body gradually returned to normal, completely appearing on the immortal scroll.
"You're strong yourself, and you can directly control the Ogre Snake Xie Qi. Why don't you just kill us?" Luo Zheng asked seriously.
The two Ogre Snakes controlled by the fox could easily block their escape route.
Luring them all the way from the bottom to the top right corner of the Unending Labyrinth, the goal was to kill them. It was unnecessary.
Luo Zheng's question made the others perk up their ears.
But judging from the fox's answer, the result wouldn't be good.
"It's too wasteful to let the Ogre Snake Xie Qi swallow you all. There are still many of those Curly Beasts that you speak of who are starving …"
The fox turned around as it spoke, and walked towards the top right corner along the passage.
The two Ogre Snakes flanked them from both sides. Seeing that they were getting closer, their scalps tingled.
No matter how unwilling they were, they had no other choice. Compared to the fox, the threat of the Ogre Snake Xie Qi was more realistic …
So under the escort of the two Ogre Snakes, the group had no choice but to follow the fox slowly.
At the end of the top right corner, there were indeed some smaller Curly Beasts. Those Curly Beasts looked like dogs, and some of the Nirvana Realm creatures recognized them immediately.
"Those Curly Beasts look like Iron Hound Beasts?"
"They are Iron Hounds!"
"Are they planning to feed us to the dogs?"
Iron Hound Beasts were a very common Curly Beast on the first page. Many of the Nirvana Realm creatures present had seen and killed many of them. Naturally, they could recognize them at a glance.
"Hmm? That's right. You all seem to have become smarter all of a sudden, "the fox praised.
"…"
The Nirvana Realm creatures were silent. They had always been the favored ones in Fandu Heaven, and were the top existences in the entire Nirvana Realm. Now, they finally had a taste of being on the chopping block.
The Nirvana Realm creatures all felt a chill in their hearts.
Luo Zheng walked forward while quietly observing.
Because the entire tail of the maze was in the form of a nine-square grid, there were many crisscrossing passages. But every passage was blocked by Ogre Snake's body, completely blocking the way out …
When he followed the fox through another horizontal passage, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows abruptly.
At the top right corner, there were two symmetrical Hexagonal Star Formations!
"Fei Yu," Luo Zheng greeted in a low voice.
Fei Yu, Kuai Jian, and the other Nirvana Realm creatures also saw the two Hexagonal Star Formations.
In fact, there were many Hexagonal Star Formations at the end of the maze. Almost every three passages had two Hexagonal Star Formations.
But even if they relied on the Hexagonal Star Formations to transform into their original form, they were still no match for Ogre Snake. So along the way, no one planned to hide. That was no different from seeking death.
But now, it was different …
No one was willing to be fed to the dogs.
Rather than waiting for death, it was better to fight to the death.
These Nirvana Realm creatures obviously thought of the same thing.
Just as they passed by the passage, two groups of people rushed out from both sides.
In the blink of an eye, these Nirvana Realm creatures had already stepped into the Hexagonal Star Formation. One by one, they finally returned to their original form, and their expressions were as if they were facing a great enemy.

Chapter 3406
The fox stood at the top right corner of the Unending Labyrinth, looking at the scrambling scene with a cold smile.
There were many hexagonal star array rooms at the end of the Unending Labyrinth, these people would most likely escape into the hexagonal star array rooms if they were not willing to give up.
"Do you think you can fight me just because you've returned to your original form?"
"None of your seniors were able to escape from my hands. Do you think you can do it?"
"Hehehe …"
Her laughter was very crisp, like a wind chime being blown by a gentle breeze.
But in this beautiful laughter, there was a trace of coldness, this coldness seeped into the bones of all the Nirvana Realm beings, making them shiver.
Then she slowly turned back from the top right corner …
"She's coming down!"
"She's going to enter the hexagonal star array like this?"
"She's not going to command Otaki, she's going to do it herself?"
Everyone was very nervous.
Although they had returned to their original form, they did not think they could deal with Otaki.
If this fox had commanded Xie Qi to rush into the room with the Hexagon Star Array, it would still be able to crush everyone. Even if these people had some unique techniques, it would still be hard to deal with them.
But looking at the fox's posture, she was going to go alone?
When the fox walked to the center of the crisscrossing passageway, her footsteps paused. Luo Zheng, Fei Yu, Kuai Jian and the other Nirvana Realm beings were in the hexagonal star array room on the left, while Wu Dang and another group of Nirvana Realm beings were in the hexagonal star array room on the right.
Suddenly.
After a short while, she made her choice and walked towards the hexagonal star array room on the right.
Wu Dang and the other Nirvana Realm beings saw the fox walking towards them, their eyes flashed.
They did not have a good way to deal with Otaki, but if the fox came in, maybe they would have a chance?
In a situation that was originally a dead end, a chess piece suddenly appeared, anyone would grab this opportunity with all their might!
"Everyone, spread out!"
"Since she can command Otaki, if we capture her, we should be able to make Otaki retreat …"
"At this point, there's no need to hide anymore, use whatever methods you have!"
Wu Dang and the others were originally leaning against the corner of the wall. In the face of battle, they mustered the courage in their hearts and stood in a fan-shaped formation in the not spacious room. Power gathered in their bodies …
Fox stepped into the room. The moment she stepped onto the hexagonal array, she revealed her true form.
She looked like a thirteen or fourteen year old girl, but she had two furry ears on her head. Her entire body was emitting a faint halo, as flawless as a white jade. Her light purple hair hung down, covering her.
More than half of his face was covered by baby fat.
"This form is really disgusting …" The fox looked at its own body.
As a scroll beast, she believed that the form of a scroll was a higher form, and this kind of body that contained too many details was a very low level form.
"Everyone, attack!"
As soon as the fox returned to its normal form, Wu Dang let out a roar.
His body tilted slightly, and a purple jade tablet appeared in his hand.
"Pu!"
Wu Dang clenched his fist, and the purple jade tablet was crushed into powder.
As soon as the purple jade tablet was crushed, a purple shadow emerged from it. When the shadow appeared, it released a vast aura. This aura was confined to the room, and under the powerful pressure, everyone retreated.
A few steps.
Only the fox girl stood in place, dumbfounded, not moving at all.
"It's the Heaven Bestowal All Spirit Tablet! It seems to be a top grade tablet! "
"As expected of the Purple Cloud Clan, they actually let their clansmen carry this item!"
"… …"
When the other Nirvana Realm beings saw the purple shadow, the flames of hope in their hearts ignited.
The Heaven Bestowal All Spirit Tablet came from the 33 Heavens.
Within the 33 Heavens, there was a mountain called Heaven Bestowal, and within Heaven Bestowal Mountain lived a type of life form made of pure energy. This type of life form was similar to a pure energy body, and had a gentle temperament. There were many of them.
After they grew old, fell sick, and died, they would drill into a purple jade stone within Heaven Bestowal Mountain.
The more life forms that were immersed, the higher the grade of the jade stone.
According to the color division, the Heaven Bestowal All Spirit Tablet had a total of six grades. The sixth grade was called a top grade tablet, and even within the 33 Heavens, it was a powerful weapon!
This Heaven Bestowal All Spirit Tablet was a personal keepsake of Wu Dang's grandfather. It had been hidden away for many years without being used. It was originally given to Wu Dang for self-defense after he entered the 33 Heavens.
Wu Dang never thought that he would use this item in the Immortal Painting, but this fox girl had given him a chance. Naturally, he no longer had any misgivings. Compared to the precious tablet, his own life was more important.
"Hu hu hu …"
The purple shadow containing pure energy enveloped the fox girl. In the face of this powerful energy pressure, the fox girl only gently raised her hand, and two pale pink fingernails intertwined as she lightly flicked them.
It was as if she had flicked some law, and the law spread out from her fingertips.
The surging purple shadow was instantly suppressed, shrunk, and disappeared …
While Wu Dang was still in a daze, his chest suddenly suffered a heavy blow from a sledgehammer. With a "peng", his entire body was plastered to the wall.
His muscles, bones, and flesh exploded layer by layer, and his entire body was almost flattened. However, his soul was not severely injured, but he was on his last breath.
"They don't like to eat dead things," the fox girl emphasized.
She stretched out her pink finger and gently hooked it, peeling Wu Dang from the wall. She dragged him toward the Hexagonal Array and threw him out of the room.
The moment she threw him out, Wu Dang's body broke into pieces. The broken pieces turned into paintings and scattered on the white scroll. It was a shocking sight.
The iron hounds in the upper right corner seemed to have smelled blood. They slowly crawled along the passage and began to devour Wu Dang's broken body …
The remaining Nirvana Realm creatures in the room on the right trembled like a sieve. When they thought that they were going to become food for the iron hounds, they could not suppress the fear in their hearts.
"What a strange method!"
"We're going to die anyway. Let's fight!"
"…"
The Nirvana Realm creatures gathered their remaining courage and swarmed forward.
The fox girl's expression did not change. She gently clenched her finger, and her pink fingernails gently knocked and flicked.
"Peng peng peng peng …"
Wave after wave of tremendous force crashed into their chests. Regardless of whether it was the Earth Calamity Tribe with their powerful physical bodies or the Bee Luring Tribe with their fist-sized bodies, they were unable to retaliate at all. When their bodies were pressed against the wall, they were already on the verge of death.
The fox girl gently clenched her finger again, and these half-dead Nirvana Realm creatures turned into the shape of paintings and were thrown into the passage to feed the dogs.
After getting rid of the last Nirvana Realm creature, she slowly turned around and cast her gaze across the passage to the room on the left.

Chapter 3407
The doors of the two Hexagonal Star Array rooms were facing each other.
Luo Zheng and the others were in the room on the left. Since their vision was not blocked, they could see everything clearly. The group of Nirvana Realm creatures' faces turned green.
The fox girl knew that the Nirvana Realm creatures in the room on the right were looking from a distance. She pursed her lips and showed a youthful smile. "I'm coming."
Her pleasant voice was like the promise of the Judge of Life and Death. It turned into wisps of cold air and poured into the spines of everyone.
"Retreat …"
"We can only fight with her!"
"Fight? Even the Tian Feng All Spirits Board can't do anything. Do you have stronger means? "
The Nirvana Realm creatures in the room on the left were not weak. They also planned to fight back, but what was the actual result?
What was the most terrible thing? It was being helpless in a desperate situation.
They watched helplessly as the vixen walked out of the Hexagon Star Array. They watched helplessly as she turned into a beautiful painting and slowly moved towards them on the ground. However, they could not think of any countermeasures!
"What means did she use just now? It seems to have directly dissolved the energy in the Tian Feng All Spirits Board," Fei Yu said in a low voice. He was probably one of the few who could keep calm.
Luo Zheng was also in deep thought.
The Tian Feng All Spirits Board that Wu Dang used was very powerful. Even though they were not in the same room, Luo Zheng could still feel its strong power.
The fox girl did not directly confront the Tian Feng All Spirits Board. She did use some means to dissolve it …
But the powerful energy in the Tian Feng All Spirits Board was directly dissolved?
What means is this …
"I've never seen such means. I didn't even know there was such a powerful person hidden in the Immortal Picture Scroll," Heavenly Lord Yuanshi said with a serious face.
Even the knowledgeable Heavenly Lord Yuanshi could not understand. Luo Zheng's heart sank slightly.
Just when everyone was hesitating, Fei Yu suddenly said, "I have a Spirit Trapping Rope. As long as it traps a creature, it can stop all means!"
"Spirit Trapping Rope?"
The dim eyes of the Nirvana Realm creatures in the room suddenly brightened up.
The Nirvana Realm creatures who could enter the Immortal Picture Scroll were also the main targets of cultivation. As expected, they carried many good things with them.
"I don't know if the Spirit Trapping Rope is effective against that fox girl," Fei Yu said hesitantly.
"Regardless of whether it works or not, we can still give it a try." Luo Zheng's eyes were burning, and his fighting spirit was already brewing in his chest.
According to Luo Zheng's experience of wandering around in desperate situations, if he was indecisive at this time, he would die. If he fought back, he might still have a chance to live!
"Then …"
Fei Yu's eyes flashed with determination. After taking two steps forward, he shook his right hand and a golden rope appeared on his arm. The rope coiled around his arm like a snake.
Luo Zheng's figure flashes and stands on one side of the Hexagon Star Array.
Since this fox girl was a creature living in the immortal scroll, she must be different from them. Whether ordinary methods would work was a big question mark, but he had no other choice.
As for the other Nirvana Realm creatures, they retreated far away to the sides.
"No, it's useless …"
"That is an undefeatable existence!"
"We will all die. We will definitely die!"
After seeing the scene on the right side of the room, they had already lost their fighting spirit. Even if they were to fight against Luo Zheng and Fei Yu, it was futile in their eyes.
"Brother Luo Zheng, I have a treasure that can protect all of you."
Kuai Jian stood out too.
He held a small violet gourd in his hand. As he spoke, he pointed the mouth of the gourd at Luo Zheng and Fei Yu. This gourd mouth spat out a membrane of light that flickered with a faint luster.
As this membrane of light floated, it actually gathered on Luo Zheng and Fei Yu's bodies.
"This Heavenly Gourd Feather Coat can withstand 50,000 Deity jun of strength."
Kuai Jian said.
50,000 Deity jun of strength was not a small amount …
If it was given to those Sacred Soul Realm experts in the Mother World, coupled with their Chaotic Origin Spirit, they could even resist the attacks of ordinary Undying Realm experts. This Heavenly Gourd Feather Coat was clearly not an ordinary Nirvana Realm talisman.
Luo Zheng and Fei Yu did not speak. They watched as the fox girl on the ground slowly moved closer to the Hexagon Star Array. Following that, an eye-catching figure began condensing …
"Do it!" Luo Zheng ordered in a low voice.
Fei Yu extended his hand and shook it, causing the Spirit Confining Rope to coil around the fox girl.
At the moment the Spirit Confining Rope coiled around the fox girl, the energy within Luo Zheng's body suddenly erupted. A fist imbued with immense strength smashed down on the fox girl's head.
At the moment when Luo Zheng erupted with his strength, Fei Yu, Kuai Jian, and the other Nirvana Realm experts in the room were all stunned.
The Heaven Bestowal Myriad Spirit Tablet that Wu Dang brought out was already very powerful. But Luo Zheng's fist contained half of its aura. Did this mean that Luo Zheng's strength was comparable to half of the Heaven Bestowal Myriad Spirit Tablet?
That should be the strength of an Undying Realm expert, right?
Everyone present had come here for the sake of reaching the Undying Realm. None of them had reached the Undying Realm yet!
But the current situation was extremely dangerous. Even though everyone was shocked, they were still worried about the fox girl and not Luo Zheng. Could this fist smash the fox girl to death?
The fox girl who was bound by the Spirit Confining Rope did not react in time. She only saw a huge fist smashing down on her head.
"Boom!"
The moment Luo Zheng's fist touched the top of the fox girl's head, his heart skipped a beat. There was a chance!
After the fox girl was bound by the Spirit Confining Rope, she seemed to have lost all means and abilities. The top of her head was directly smashed by Luo Zheng. The scene was bloody and horrifying.
The fox girl who had half her head smashed did not die. She raised her right hand slightly and clasped two tender fingers together. It seemed that the strange ability was about to be released.
"Crack! Crack! "
Luo Zheng suddenly reached out with both hands and grabbed the fox girl's thin arms. With a fierce twist, the fox girl's arms were fractured. Naturally, she could not release the strange nomological power released from her fingertips.
At this moment, Luo Zheng naturally did not have the slightest bit of mercy. After breaking the fox girl's arms, his other fist smashed into her chest. The terrifying strength directly pierced through her chest and sent her petite body flying backward.
Because she was facing the Hexagonal Star Formation, she had turned into a painting on the ground and rushed out of the room when she was flying backward. She crashed into the wall on the opposite side of the aisle. Her originally incomplete body was smashed into pieces.
The painting that she had turned into was no longer in the shape of a human. Instead, it was scattered on the ground like a broken gunny sack.
Fei Yu was still holding the other end of the Spirit Confining Rope. Half of his Dragon-snaring Rope was in its normal form. The other half extended outside the room and was entangled with the tattered gunny sack painting.
"Is she dead?" Fei Yu asked with concern.
Even if the fox girl was smashed into pieces, he did not dare to take back the Spirit Confining Rope.
"If she was a normal Nirvana creature, she should be dead," Luo Zheng said with a frown.
With her head smashed and her chest pierced, even a strong person in the Immortal Realm would die.
Those Nirvana creatures who had chosen to give up also stared at the tattered gunny sack painting on the ground with bright eyes. Luo Zheng's performance had completely turned the situation around. They suddenly felt that they could survive.

Chapter 3408
Seeing the movement of the "broken sack" on the ground, everyone's heart skipped a beat.
She's still alive?
When a living being reached the level of a true god, their vitality would become extremely tenacious. It wouldn't matter if they lost an arm or a leg, or even if their organs were shattered.
But even if they reached the Nirvana Realm, their most important dantian and soul couldn't be damaged. If their dantian was destroyed, their cultivation would be gone, and if their soul was destroyed, they would die.
At least, the living beings that Luo Zheng had come into contact with at this stage couldn't escape this level. Even the Blood Spirit Black Tortoise of the Ten Great Beasts was no exception.
Luo Zheng didn't show any mercy just now. He only wanted to cut off the vixen's life.
His fierce strength almost crushed her into pieces, but she still wasn't dead?
"She's … fine?"
"What kind of monster is this? Did he not hurt her soul just now?"
"It can't be …"
When everyone saw that Luo Zheng and Fei Yu had worked together to kill the vixen, their hearts rose to the sky. They thought that it would be a simple escape from death, but now …
The pile of things that had fallen to the ground moved slowly. As they moved, these black lumps continued to merge and recombine.
When the pile of things reached the door, it was almost in the shape of the vixen.
"She's back." Fei Yu's face was full of bitterness.
"Then let's do it again," Luo Zheng said expressionlessly.
In any case, they didn't have a way to punish the vixen. If they could still smash her into pieces, then they could just continue like this. She couldn't be reborn endlessly!
The vixen entered the Hexagon Star Array again. At this time, Fei Yu's Spirit Trapping Rope had already been retracted. He saw that the vixen was about to reform again, so he suddenly threw the Spirit Trapping Rope to bind her again.
At the same time, the power in Luo Zheng's body suddenly exploded, blasting towards the vixen.
But at this time, the vixen's body suddenly flashed with a glassy luster. Her snow-white skin suddenly became translucent. After becoming translucent, her body became abnormally strong every time.
"Bang!"
The moment she saw Luo Zheng's fist coming at her, she raised her hand and easily caught Luo Zheng's fist. This punch that contained 80,000 Shen Jun's strength was easily blocked by her!
"You think this rope can trap me?" The fox girl grabbed Luo Zheng's fist with one hand and grabbed the Spirit-Trapping Rope with the other.
"You can't?"
Fei Yu's pupils shrank slightly.
Actually, when he saw the vixen's body turn into a glassy color, he already knew that something was wrong. If the Spirit Trapping Rope was effective, the vixen would not have such a transformation. The only explanation was that the rope was completely ineffective!
The fox girl grabbed the Spirit Trapping Rope and pulled it gently. The end of the rope was pulled out of Fei Yu's hand and she threw it on the ground. Her big eyes turned to Luo Zheng and a smile appeared on her translucent face. "You're still here?"
He didn't give up? I think they have all given up. "
Most of the Nirvana Realm creatures in the room didn't have any fighting spirit. They were either pinning their hopes on Luo Zheng or waiting for death. Only Fei Yu and Kuai Jian had a little fighting spirit.
But the guy in front of him was completely different. There seemed to be sparks burning in his eyes. Although he was silent, his fighting spirit was boiling. He didn't seem to have any intention of giving up.
"Why should I give up?"
Luo Zheng opened his eyes. His other hand grabbed onto the shoulder of the vixen.
After the body of the vixen turned translucent, her body became slippery and extremely tough. Luo Zheng grabbed her shoulder and pulled her up from the ground before slamming her onto the ground.
"Hong!"
Energy ripples spread out in all directions. Those Nirvana Realm creatures who were leaning against the wall felt this spreading energy and were suppressed to the point of suffocation.
The Hexagonal Star Array room was constructed using the laws of the Instant Civilization. It was almost indestructible. Hence, the ground was naturally tough. This slamming force was not weak at all.
But this vixen with a glassy body didn't show any signs of cracking …
She continued slumping her body and looked at Luo Zheng with ridicule. "90,000 Divine Jun strength, is that your limit?"
In close combat, Luo Zheng could release about two hundred thousand Godly Juns!
But if he did so, Luo Zheng's Chaotic Origin Spirit would temporarily fall into a state of fatigue. Even though it would only last for a short period, it would still be dangerous.
Hence, Luo Zheng controlled his strength to below 100,000 Divine Jun.
Seeing the mocking expression of the vixen, Luo Zheng lifted her into the air with a kick. His body suddenly pressed down, causing the Divine Jun strength to surge out once again.
Another fist smashed onto the chest of the vixen.
"Hong!"
The vixen was sent flying and smashed against the wall.
A Nirvana Realm creature beside the vixen trembled. If he were to be smashed by the vixen, he would most likely die.
"This little strength isn't enough." The vixen retracted her mocking smile. She was planning to kill him.
Seeing the transparent glassy body gradually fading away, Luo Zheng lightly raised his left hand. He gave a signal with his eyes, "Fei Yu!"
"Shua!"
The Spirit Confining Rope bound her body once again.
At the moment the Spirit Confining Rope bound the vixen, her expression turned sluggish. Her slender index finger and thumb clasped together, yet she did not trigger the law energy. The Spirit Confining Rope had taken effect …
On that childish face of hers, a pair of curved eyebrows suddenly stood up. She felt humiliated as though her thoughts were seen through. "Very good. Since you want to die in a more aggrieved manner, I'll fulfill your wish!"
It turned out that the Spirit Confining Rope was not ineffective. At least, it could prevent her from using the sacred art of clasping her fingers. After being bound by the Spirit Confining Rope, she could not use any sacred art. She could only transform into her glassy form. But in this glassy form, she could not use any sacred art.
Previously when the vixen snatched the Spirit Confining Rope and threw it on the ground, Luo Zheng had secretly transmitted a message to Fei Yu, asking him to retrieve the Spirit Confining Rope.
Even though Luo Zheng did not know what other trump cards or sacred arts the vixen had, he could not resist against the strange power of clasping her fingers. With the Spirit Confining Rope, he could at least prevent her from using that technique.
The glassy color that had faded away reappeared on the surface of her body. At the same time, she reached out to grab the Spirit Confining Rope.
However, Fei Yu was very quick-witted. He did not miss the opportunity to pull back the Spirit Confining Rope. He looked at the vixen with vigilance.
In any case, Fei Yu understood that only one of them could survive today. He was not afraid of offending the vixen.
"If that's the case, then I'll just beat you to death!"
The vixen did not want to play a game of seesaw with Fei Yu. A ruthless expression flashed across her face. Her glassy hand pierced the head of the Nirvana Realm creature beside her like lightning.
"Pop!"
The Nirvana Realm creature's head exploded like a watermelon.
In the next moment, her petite body, which was not even as tall as Luo Zheng's chest, crashed into Luo Zheng.
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng was sent flying and stuck to the wall on the other side.

Chapter 3409
Not long after Luo Zheng and the others passed through the ice cave, the two Ning Bingnu came out of the ice cave and chased after them.
If it were other Five Elements Spiritual Slaves, they would not be able to move forward in this extremely low temperature. However, Ning Bingnu's body temperature was extremely low, so they could walk freely in this environment.
After the originally crystal clear Ning Bingnu rushed to the upper layer of the ice field, a layer of white frost immediately condensed on the surface of their bodies. They were originally very agile, but in this environment, they became a little slow.
"Follow them!"
"Gather the other Five Elements Spiritual Slaves and encircle them!"
Because of what happened to Huan Leinu, the two Ning Bingnu were very smart and did not choose to attack. Instead, they followed Luo Zheng and the others all the way.
Luo Zheng sped forward while looking at the movement behind him.
"Why do they keep following us like this?" Li Bianxue asked.
"This is the most troublesome thing," Phoenix shook her head.
If Luo Zheng got rid of them, he could naturally find a place to wait for the Soul Breaking Sand on Phoenix to lose its effect. Then, the three of them could quietly leave the other side and avoid this disaster. They could then think about how to enter the 31st Layer of Heaven in the future.
But if they kept following them like this, there was no chance for them to leave the other side!
"We have to find a way to get rid of them!"
Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
After speeding forward for a short distance, he abruptly stopped in his tracks.
"Sizzle —"
While stabilizing his body on the ice, he rushed toward the two Ning Bingnu in the opposite direction.
The two Ning Bingnu were also very alert.
When Luo Zheng stopped, they also stopped. Seeing Luo Zheng rushing toward them, they also raised their wings and retreated quickly.
They kept a distance of hundreds of feet from him, neither too far nor too close.
Seeing that he could not catch up with them, Luo Zheng's face showed a trace of helplessness. He could only turn back and move forward …
At the same time, another layer of ice on the upper layer of the ice field suddenly collapsed. The cold wind rushed in and a huge ice cave appeared.
"Crack!"
A huge flaming hand reached out from the ice cave and pressed down on the edge of the ice cave. A huge body leaped out from the cave. The body was covered in green flames. It was a Moon Fire Slave.
"Phew! It's cold! "
The Moon Fire Slave shivered.
Yu Sheng-tian's extremely cold environment naturally didn't welcome Moon Fire Slaves.
Therefore, if the Moon Fire Slaves wanted to climb to the second layer of the Icefield, they had to make special preparations.
The armor on its body was similar to the Yuan Spirit Divine Fire Shield.
As long as a certain amount of flame was injected, it would be able to resist the extreme cold.
Even with the protection of the armor, the low temperature still annoyed the Moon Fire Slave.
However, the order had already been given. The human brat they had been waiting for had actually fled to the Upper Ice Plains. This was a result that no one had expected.
As long as they caught the kid, their mission would be completed. They did not need to stay in this cold for too long …
Burly Moon Fire Slaves kept crawling out of the square ice cave.
After these Moon Fire Slaves determined the direction, they ran wildly in that direction. Even the ice layer, which was hundreds of feet thick, started to shake as if there was an earthquake under the running of so many Moon Fire Slaves …
On the other side of the Upper Ice Plains, a bowl-sized ice cave appeared.
"Pipa!"
Bolts of lightning, which were as thick as bowls, crawled out of the ice cave.
The Huan Leinu, which was originally like a long snake, coiled around in the air and quickly turned into strange and abstract human figures.
The Huan Leinu were all silent. After determining the direction, they ran straight in that direction.
The Huan Leinu immediately intertwined with each other like long snakes, making explosive sounds as they moved forward at an extremely high speed.
The two Ning Bingnu kept sending Luo Zheng's location to their companions. The Five Elements Spirit Slaves formed a huge circle and surrounded him from all directions.
"Pipa, pipa …"
When Luo Zheng heard the rumbling of thunder again, his face slightly darkened.
He glanced to the left and saw bursts of lightning in the distance. It must be the Huan Leinu rapidly approaching.
Boom! Boom! Boom!
At the same time, the ground began to shake rhythmically again. On the other side, the burly Moon Fire Slaves were also chasing after them …
The Five Elements Spirit Slaves became more and more powerful. Luo Zheng and the others' escape route became narrower and narrower.
After sprinting forward for a short distance, he saw the Cold Prison that was dozens of kilometers wide.
They were surrounded by the Five Elements Spirit Slaves on their left, right, and rear. In front of them was the Cold Prison, which was like a forbidden area. The three of them really had no way out.
"What should we do …" Li Bianxue bit her lips tightly.
She was still young. She did not want to die, nor did she want her Yang Soul to be trapped in Yu Sheng-tian.
Luo Zheng frowned and did not respond to Li Bianxue. He just frowned and slowly retreated with his back against the Cold Prison.
Just then, the Yuanshi Tianzun suddenly said, "Luo Zheng, go near the Cold Prison!"
"Go near the Cold Prison?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed.
The bald giant imprisoned in the largest Cold Prison was not to be trifled with. In other words, the bald giant's strength was much more terrifying than the Five Elements Spirit Slaves!
If they went near the Cold Prison without permission, they would be attacked by the bald giant. Wasn't this courting death?
"In comparison, the bald giant should hate Genryū more," the Yuanshi Tianzun replied.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly raised.
There was no way out in front of them.
Even the Yuanshi Tianzun would find it difficult to fight against so many Five Elements Spirit Slaves …
If the Yuanshi Tianzun's hypothesis was true, they could fight for it!
According to the Tianzhi Civilization, the bald giant should have been captured by the Yuanling Civilization. He must hate Genryū the most.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, then said to the green figure, "Help me activate the Tianzhi Core. I want to see the situation in the Cold Prison!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the Tianzhi Core in the distance was activated again.
Because the Tianzhi Civilization had already deciphered the light spots in the Cold Prison before, it was very easy to decipher it a second time. As two green lights flashed, the scene in the Cold Prison was instantly reflected in Luo Zheng's eyes.
He wouldn't have known if he didn't take a look, but when he did, Luo Zheng was shocked.
The bald giant was already lying on the edge of the Cold Prison, his body bent forward and his arms tensed. He looked like he was ready to pounce at any time!
The Yuanshi Tianzun had guessed one thing correctly. The bald giant hated the Yuanling Civilization to the core and wanted to tear Genryū's people to pieces at all times. Now that he saw the Five Elements Spirit Slaves, he even wanted to swallow them whole.
The Moon Fire Slaves naturally could not understand the light spots in the Cold Prison. They did not know that there was a powerful existence in the Cold Prison eyeing them covetously. They only knew that there was no way out for Luo Zheng.
"Human brat, you have no way out. Come back with us obediently, and we can still spare your life," the Moon Fire Slave at the front said with a sinister smile.
This Moon Fire Slave was already planning to drill a hole in the ice and return to the lower layer of the Ice Field. The low temperature here was really annoying.
Luo Zheng did not answer, but continued to retreat.
The bald giant was bound by chains and could only attack within a range of three hundred feet outside the Cold Prison.

Chapter 3410
The fox girl saw that she was being obstructed by the bone piece. She immediately pulled out her blood-stained hand and pierced towards Luo Zheng's Dantian once again.
Luo Zheng had already predicted this action of hers. He twisted his body once again.
The fox girl revealed a grin. She thought that Luo Zheng would still struggle futilely like before. But this time, as Luo Zheng twisted his body, he stomped on the ground and bounced up like a zombie.
At the same time, the exhausted Chaotic Origin Spirit in Luo Zheng's body had already been repaired.
"Hu!"
A green feather appeared above Luo Zheng's head. It was the Green Jade Feather brought out by the Green Jade Civilization's Tool of Civilization. At the moment this feather was brought out, Luo Zheng's agility greatly increased.
But this was far from enough!
"Senior Primordial Heavenly Sage!"
That green book of the Human race started flipping through the pages. When the green book stopped at a certain page, it corresponded to a certain location within Luo Zheng's inner world that started flashing with green light.
That was a pond constructed in the sky. It was called the Phoenix Pond.
After Primordial Heavenly Sage occupied Luo Zheng's inner world, he assigned many missions to the Human race. The Phoenix Pond was one of them.
Be it the Primordial Heavenly Sage or the true body of Nuwa, their remnant souls were extremely weak. They could only borrow the Human Race's Tool of Civilization.
The book let out a light, causing green ripples to appear on the surface of the Phoenix Pond. Those green ripples rushed out of Luo Zheng's inner world like a waterfall. Following which, they were released from the surface of Luo Zheng's body.
With the support of the two Nirvana Lifeforms, Luo Zheng's speed increased tremendously!
The fox girl did not treat Luo Zheng as a threat. She remained expressionless as she tried to grab onto Luo Zheng. In her opinion, dismembering Luo Zheng would only take three to five breaths of time.
Who would have thought that with this grab, Luo Zheng was like a slippery loach that slid behind her.
"Ka!"
He grabbed onto the back of the fox girl and threw her towards the wall by the side.
"Hong!"
The fox girl's body was about to hit the wall. Her legs gently bent and she stood firmly on the wall. She said, "You've become faster? But it's still useless. It's useless. With your strength, you're just scratching an itch.
Itchy! "
She fiercely kicked off the wall with her legs. Unexpectedly, she turned her body upside down and kicked towards Luo Zheng's head.
If this kick landed, Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to survive!
However, Luo Zheng, whose speed had skyrocketed, merely tilted his shoulder slightly and dodged the kick that had a force of more than 100,000 Shenjun. The fox girl's kick spiraled out and directly kicked another Nirvana Realm creature.
Although the Nirvana Realm creature tried his best to evade, the Hexagonal Star Formation room was only so big. By the time he realized the danger, there was no space left for him to evade.
"Boom!"
The fox girl's foot swept across the Nirvana Realm creature's head, and the head instantly exploded into blood foam. The Nirvana Realm creature immediately became a headless corpse. After swaying a few times, it laid on the ground powerlessly, with blood gushing out from the wound on its neck.
。
When the other Nirvana Realm creatures saw this scene, they were like white rabbits meeting a tiger. They hid in a corner and trembled. They did not dare to resist and could only pray that Luo Zheng could eliminate this monster …
They could not keep up with his speed at all. Even if they hid in a corner, they would become collateral damage if they were careless. This feeling was worse than fighting with their lives on the line!
Instead, there were two Nirvana Realm creatures who used their unique techniques. One of them turned his body into a grayish-brown poisonous fog. He did not think that his poisonous fog could defeat the fox girl. He did it to protect himself.
The other Nirvana Realm creature shrunk himself to the size of a fist and hid in a corner. This way, he would be much safer …
After Luo Zheng's speed increased, he finally had a chance to catch his breath.
Before the Chaotic Origin Spirit recovered, he was not willing to fight against the fox girl head-on. He could only rely on his nimble body to dodge. At the same time, Luo Zheng was analyzing the situation at high speed.
The fox girl had increased the strength in her hands almost every time she attacked. Currently, her strength was close to 200,000 Shenjun!
This meant that her fleshy body could withstand a strength of 200,000 Shenjun.
Luo Zheng could only exert strength of 200,000 Shenjun with his fleshy body. It was unlikely that he could shatter the Colored Glass Body of the fox girl …
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
Luo Zheng's figure moved swiftly. He barely dodged two attacks from the fox girl. Her strength had increased to 220,000 Shenjun.
Perhaps it was because of her powerful physical body, but the fox girl's attacks became more casual. Every smash was earth-shattering.
"Dodging like this is meaningless." The fox girl continued chasing and smashing as she sighed: "Even if I give you a chance to counterattack, what can you do? The limit of my God Crystal is not something you can imagine. "
In her opinion, even if she stood at her original spot and allowed Luo Zheng to smash her, Luo Zheng would not move at all.
After the Chaotic Origin Spirit recovered, Luo Zheng seized the opportunity to send out a few punches on the fox girl. He controlled his strength to around 90,000 Shenjun and could not injure her at all.
"Peng!"
As the two figures crossed each other, Luo Zheng circled behind her and sent out a kick at her back. This kick that was close to 100,000 Shenjun was easily blocked by her.
Seeing this scene, despair appeared on the faces of Fei Yu and the others.
If Luo Zheng had displayed his full strength, it meant that they were doomed. After all, Luo Zheng could not injure his opponent at all.
And in this narrow room, Luo Zheng would fail sooner or later. Failure meant death.
"You should be able to control strength of more than 100,000 Shenjun. Why don't you dare to exceed 100,000 Shenjun?"
After blocking Luo Zheng's kick, the fox girl giggled as she turned around to grab at him. But Luo Zheng had already floated away and barely avoided this calamity.
In this extremely difficult battle, Luo Zheng's performance was unusually silent.
It was not that he did not have a chance. Currently, the only thing he could rely on was that Celestial Spear.
At the 31st Layer of Heaven, Luo Zheng had given the Celestial Spear to Li Bianxue for safekeeping. Before entering the Immortal Painting Scroll, Luo Zheng used his Yang Soul Body to take it back from Li Bianxue.
By relying on the Three Clear Stone, Luo Zheng had already fused his soul and body as one. Hence, Luo Zheng could similarly open the Sumeru Space in his Yang Soul.
"Or do you think you have some trump cards that allow you to counterattack?"
The fox girl suddenly felt reluctant to kill this plaything of hers.
In the Immortal Painting Scroll, she had hunted and killed many of such low-leveled lifeforms. These lifeforms that lacked a string were so weak in her eyes. They could not even withstand a single blow.
This human in front of her was most likely the only one who had played with her for so long.
Even though there were some special reasons — most likely because they were in the Hexagonal Star Array room.
But no matter what, it was not bad for him to be able to withstand her attacks.
"Sou!"
"If you have this kind of trick, use it quickly. Otherwise, you won't have a chance!"
As she spoke, the fox girl jumped directly onto the roof of the room more than ten Zhang high. Then, with a kick of her legs, she swooped down toward Luo Zheng.
This was purely using her own body as a weapon. It was so ruthless and unreasonable.
She had used the same trick several times. Every time, it was extremely threatening. Luo Zheng had no choice but to flee in panic.

Chapter 3411
This time, the Celestial Spear had recovered to its peak state. It could amplify Luo Zheng's strength by nine times.
If he were to go all out, Luo Zheng would be able to unleash a power of one million and two hundred thousand Shen Jun, then the power of this attack would be as high as one million and eight hundred thousand Shen Jun.
But there was only one chance. Hence, Luo Zheng was exceptionally careful.
When he was dealing with the fox girl, he was extremely patient. He was waiting for this chance!
"Weng!"
200,000 Deity Monarchs of strength surged along his arms like a tide.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit could only withstand 100,000 Deity Monarchs. The remaining 100,000 Deity Monarchs of strength had to be resisted by Luo Zheng's fleshy body. And this 100,000 Deity Monarchs of strength was already the limit of Luo Zheng's body.
"Ka, ka, ka …"
When Luo Zheng wielded the Celestial Spear, fine cracking sounds could be heard coming from his body.
When his body reached its limit, Luo Zheng's bones could no longer withstand the strength, causing fine cracks to appear on them.
If Luo Zheng remained in this state for a period of time, his body would most likely disintegrate.
But the Celestial Spear had already lashed out at the fox girl.
After infusing 200,000 Deity Monarchs of strength into it, the Celestial Stone embedded at the front emitted an astonishing light. An indescribable weight pressed down on the tip of the spear.
When Luo Zheng brandished the spear, the spear body was pressed into a full moon shape!
"Hu!"
Everyone could only feel a gust of wind rushing at them. This gust of wind was like a spear or a blade cutting at their faces. The intense pain forced them to dodge instinctively. But there was nowhere to run in the room.
The fox girl had never treated Luo Zheng as an opponent. She had never thought that Luo Zheng would bring out such a weapon.
When she felt the strength released by the Celestial Spear, that glazed face revealed a look of shock.
She could not deduce how strong the Celestial Spear was, but her intuition told her that she would definitely die under this spear!
The Deity Crystal was an extremely strong state. Under normal circumstances, it could not be broken. But once the strength exceeded a certain limit, as long as a single point was broken, her entire body would shatter!
Fei Yu and Kuai Jian's eyes flashed with a strange light. They didn't expect Luo Zheng to have such a trick up his sleeve.
Just as the Celestial Spear was about to hit the fox girl, a huge hand suddenly made a stroke in the direction of the Endless Labyrinth, and this stroke happened to be in the Hexagonal Star Array room where Luo Zheng was.
On the immortal scroll, this stroke was a black mark, but in the room, there was a wall, a wall between the fox girl and Luo Zheng.
The Celestial Spear, which contained a force of 1,800,000 godly catties, smashed into the wall.
Boom!
Along with the roaring sound, the entire Endless Labyrinth shook violently, the two Great Snakes that were blocking the way were also restless, they controlled a lot of power, naturally they were afraid of a stronger power.
Even though the wall was indestructible, it still caved in under such a powerful force.
Looking from the top of the immortal scroll, the newly drawn lines were even more distorted.
But the crying face was not worried that the wall would be smashed through, because all the walls in the Endless Labyrinth were a whole, unless the whole labyrinth was erased, it was impossible to destroy it.
This smash was blocked by a wall that suddenly appeared, Luo Zheng was startled, and his face darkened.
Regardless of how the wall appeared, at least Luo Zheng had no way to counter the fox girl.
The fox girl originally planned to close her eyes and wait for death, but when she saw the wall suddenly appear, she was stunned for a moment, then her eyebrows rose, and her small face fell into deep thought.
After a few breaths, the killing intent on the fox girl's face reappeared, and her figure flashed, she wanted to go around the wall to chase Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth, and his figure retreated.
The fox girl had just taken two steps, and with a whoosh, another wall appeared in front of her.
She still didn't want to give up, and her figure retreated, wanting to go around from the other side.
Whoosh!
After the third wall appeared, she was completely isolated.
The hexagonal star formation room was divided into an inner and outer room, and the fox girl couldn't enter it, so she had no choice but to enter the hexagonal star formation and turn into a flat painting.
The moment she turned into a painting, she stood up on the painting, looking at the face in the sky, the face that was constantly changing between crying and laughing.
"Lord Enforcer! Why did you stop me from killing him? "The fox girl asked angrily.
"Because you have already lost," the smiling face said in a deep voice, "If I hadn't acted, you would have been smashed by him."
"But shouldn't you have been helping me?" The fox girl asked again.
"Hahaha …" The smiling face continued to gloomily say, "I helped you because I needed you to do something that was inconvenient for me to do, not to help you slaughter the people in the passage. If it was only to slaughter, I could do it myself."
Hearing this, the fox girl was speechless for a moment.
Everything she had, everything they had, was given to them by this face, and this was indeed reasonable.
However, the fox girl's brain turned very quickly, and she suddenly reacted, "No, you wouldn't have been paying attention to me because you were worried about me!"
There was no doubt about the fox girl's strength, and it was almost impossible for her to encounter danger in the immortal scroll. The Enforcer wouldn't have been paying attention to her all the time and save her in a moment of crisis.
The only possibility was that the Enforcer had been paying attention to someone …
The answer was obvious. The Enforcer was worried about the safety of the human boy who was fighting with her!
The fox girl's guess was right. The Enforcer's eyes never moved away from Luo Zheng. He wanted to see how far Luo Zheng could go, but he didn't expect that Luo Zheng could kill the fox girl.
At the critical moment, he saved her and stopped the two from continuing to fight.
"Why? Is he special? "The fox girl asked.
"Yes," the smiling face replied affirmatively.
"Is it possible for him to enter the last room?" The fox girl's expression changed.
"At least he passed your test," the smiling face turned into a crying face, but the voice was unusually cheerful.
The fox girl was silent for a moment, thinking that the Enforcer's words were reasonable.
Putting aside the Enforcer's abnormal behavior of silently protecting him, that human boy did indeed defeat her and even "killed" her.
"I only know that he is the person we should be waiting for, but I'm not sure whether he can enter the last room," the crying face said faintly, "But he is the most likely, so according to the agreement, you should come forward."
"Understood, Enforcer."
The fox girl's body lay in the immortal scroll and returned to the room again.
Because of several walls, Luo Zheng and others couldn't leave.
They didn't understand what had happened at all, and they were worried about their next fate.
At this moment, they heard the sound of something being erased, and the walls quickly disappeared in front of them.

Chapter 3412
These Nirvana Realm beings paced back and forth in fear. They were so nervous that their nerves were numb.
They did not even think of escaping. Instead, they stared at the fox girl with indifferent eyes.
Kill them or torture them. Since they could not escape, they might as well die a quick death.
The fox girl glanced at Luo Zheng with a complicated gaze.
She had the home ground advantage, yet she was still defeated by this fella. Perhaps luck played a part in it, but a defeat was still a defeat. This meant that the enforcer had chosen the right person.
"You, come out." The fox girl coldly said.
Luo Zheng had already repaired the Chaotic Origin Spirit in his body and was on guard against the fox girl. When he heard that she wanted him to come out, he was startled momentarily. He frowned and asked, "What do you mean?"
"You have defeated me. I should tell you some things." The fox girl said.
"I'm not going." Luo Zheng rejected her at the first instance.
He did not know what happened to the fox girl. But the moment she transformed back into Hua 'er, the gap in strength between him and the fox girl would be even greater. He would be at her mercy.
The fox girl saw how vigilant Luo Zheng was. For a moment, she could not think of a way to persuade him. After pondering for a moment, she turned her gaze to those Nirvana Realm beings behind Luo Zheng, "You all can leave first."
Since Luo Zheng was not willing to leave, she could just clear this room.
"We are not going anywhere …"
"There are still huge snakes guarding outside. We are just seeking death if we go out!"
"…"
Seeing how resolute these people were, the fox girl understood that she was a huge threat to them.
Honestly speaking, she originally intended to let Luo Zheng go. After all, Luo Zheng was the only one who defeated her. But after recalling the words of the enforcer, she felt that there was no longer any meaning in slaughtering these people.
"You all can leave. I will bring the Great Snake Xie Qi away." The fox girl said.
"What!"
"She is really going to let us go?"
"She …"
Everyone could not believe their ears.
Seeing how these people were still looking at her with suspicion, the fox girl pursed her lips. Only then did she send a message to the Great Snake Xie Qi. The two Great Snakes then slowly retreated from the upper right corner of the Unbroken Maze.
Even though the two serpents had already retreated and disappeared, the crowd still didn't believe it. They were really scared …
The fox girl gritted her teeth and said, "I'll give you three breaths of time. If you don't leave, I'll kill you!"
As soon as he said that, the Nirvana Realm creatures rushed out of the room one after another. They stepped on the Hexagonal Star Formation and transformed into a painting before leaving in a hurry.
Before Fei Yu and Kuai Jian left, they both looked at Luo Zheng and wished him luck.
It seemed that the fox girl really didn't intend to kill anyone. But if she left Luo Zheng alone, who knew what would happen …
After they left, the fox girl asked, "You obtained a total of eighteen seals in Soul City and the Disgraced Bone Land?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng answered honestly.
Since the other party asked this question, he must have figured it out, and Luo Zheng had no need to hide it.
The fox girl smiled again. "What kind of bloodline do you want?"
Faced with such a question, Luo Zheng was somewhat speechless.
The Primeval Lord of Heaven had said that the most difficult part of the Immortal Painting was on the third page, which was the big whirlpool. Inside the big whirlpool was also the time to test oneself.
As mentioned before, those seals could indeed help Luo Zheng go further, but it didn't mean that the more seals he had, the further he would go. The fox girl asked him what kind of bloodline he wanted …
"I entered the Immortal Painting with all my strength. What kind of bloodline I get will depend on the heavens," Luo Zheng answered after thinking for a while.
Man proposes, God disposes. He had always been like this.
"I want you to go to the end." The fox girl's expression suddenly became very serious.
"The end?" Luo Zheng smiled. "There are so many seniors who entered the Immortal Painting. I'm not confident that I can go to the end."
Genryū almost controlled the Nirvana Realm. There were countless elites among them. It would be very difficult for Luo Zheng to surpass them all in one go. At least, Luo Zheng wasn't confident.
"I don't want you to surpass those good-for-nothings. I want you to go to the end. Do you know what the end means? Those guys you mentioned only got the origin bloodline. They're still far from it! "the fox girl said excitedly.
"The origin bloodline?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
"We've never heard of the origin bloodline. Maybe it's the fox girl's own definition," the Primeval Lord of Heaven said. "We usually divide the big whirlpool on the third page of the Immortal Painting into an inner circle and an outer circle.
It's best if you listen to what she has to say. "
In any case, the fox girl's lack of hostility was the best thing that could happen.
"The bloodlines in that world are divided into three levels. The weakest level is the ordinary origin bloodline. The sixteen outer circles of the big whirlpool are all origin bloodlines," the fox girl said. "The four inner circles are the origin bloodlines."
"It's similar to our division," the Primeval Lord of Heaven nodded. "The outer circle is the sixteen outer circles, and the inner circle is the four inner circles. The bloodlines obtained in the inner circle are much stronger than those in the outer circle."
Most Immortal realm experts obtained the bloodlines of the outer circle, which were ordinary origin bloodlines. For example, Chi You and Hou Yi. Only a few Immortal realm experts obtained the inner circle bloodlines.
"You mean to say that the origin bloodline I obtained isn't enough? Isn't that already very powerful? "Luo Zheng asked.
The origin bloodline was already comparable to the powerhouses of the 33 Heavens. Wasn't that enough?
After hearing Luo Zheng's words, the fox girl's eyes were filled with disappointment. "That's far from enough. If you came to the Immortal Painting just to obtain an origin bloodline, then I can end your life right now."
The fox girl raised her hand, her thumb and index finger intertwined. As long as these two fingers touched, Luo Zheng would die without a doubt.
She used the power of law unique to this passage, which was also a Theurgy given to her by the author. In theory, apart from the author, no one could contend against her.
Seeing the fox girl's somewhat violent expression, Luo Zheng secretly rolled his eyes. He had never seen such an excessive person. However, thinking of the fox girl's seemingly cute but violent personality, Luo Zheng could only express his attitude. "I'll do my best, but you said the origin bloodline?
The origin bloodline isn't enough. What bloodline is stronger than the origin bloodline? "
"Origin level," the fox girl said, stretching out five fingers. "There are five types of Origin level bloodlines in the room at the end. You have to get one of them. If you can't get it, don't come back. I will kill you."
The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth couldn't help but twitch when he saw how serious the fox girl was. He couldn't casually choose a bloodline now. If he couldn't get the so-called origin bloodline, he wouldn't be able to leave this place alive ….
At this time, the Primeval Lord of Heaven fell into deep thought.
As far as he knew, the origin bloodlines of the inner circle were already the most powerful bloodlines. As long as he obtained one of them, he would be qualified to establish a sect in the 32nd or 33rd Heavens. At the very least, he would be able to guarantee that his clan would stand tall.
Fall.

Chapter 3413
When they came out of the Hexagonal Star Array room, the fox girl signaled Luo Zheng to stand still. Then, she stretched out her white fingers and gently pressed on Luo Zheng's forehead.
Luo Zheng knew the power of this pair of hands. Just as he was about to step back, the fox girl's two fingers had already touched his forehead, and a wisp of black gas instantly went into his forehead.
Luo Zheng only felt a chill on his forehead, but the chill immediately disappeared without a trace.
"What did you do!" Luo Zheng frowned and said.
The fox girl shrugged her shoulders, "I just helped you, so that you can walk more smoothly in the big whirlpool."
Luo Zheng looked at the fox girl suspiciously for a few breaths, then stepped into the Hexagonal Star Array and turned into a flat painting.
The moment Luo Zheng turned into a painting, a strange feeling came over him.
Previously, when he turned into a painting, his point of view was fixed, and he could only look down at the scenery within a certain range. But at this moment, his point of view was slightly loosened.
What's going on …
Luo Zheng raised his head reflexively. His point of view suddenly looked straight ahead, and he could actually see the end of the immortal painting scroll. Only then did he realize that he was standing on the immortal painting scroll!
His current form was not his normal form. It was still that painting, but it had turned into a standing painting.
"This is the ability of the Insect Tapir!"
Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment, then reacted.
The fox girl and the Insect Tapir could both disappear temporarily, which meant that they had jumped out of the immortal painting scroll.
Now, he was stepping on the Unbroken Labyrinth, and the linear walls were as if they did not exist to him. He could directly cross them with just one step.
If someone looked at Luo Zheng in the Unbroken Labyrinth, they would not be able to see Luo Zheng's entire body, only the flat surface of his feet.
"No wonder the Insect Tapir is so strong. This ability is too shameless," Luo Zheng suddenly understood.
In his current form, it was too easy to kill other Nirvana Realm creatures. Those creatures were not on the same level as him. They would not even know who attacked them when he stepped on them.
Luo Zheng no longer followed the path of the labyrinth. Instead, he kept crossing the walls and went straight to the end of the endless labyrinth.
When he saw the gate at the end of the Endless Labyrinth, Luo Zheng lowered his body and returned to the painting, then went out through the gate.
After passing through the Endless Labyrinth was the third page of the Immortal Scroll, which was the giant whirlpool in the sky above Ping Yi Sky.
Right now, the Nirvana Realm beings in Ping Yi Heaven were all sighing in despair.
"It's the Black Calamity, they must have encountered the Black Calamity!"
"The Black Calamity this time is too powerful. Almost all of us were wiped out!"
"Only a few survived …"
Sometimes, the Immortal Painting would become extremely dangerous, and this danger was known as the Black Calamity.
Every time the Black Calamity appeared, it would lead to the annihilation of the Nirvana Realm beings.
However, the probability of the Black Calamity appearing was extremely low, and it had never appeared on the first page.
When there were only a few Nirvana Realm creatures left, they were convinced that they had encountered the Black Calamity. Those who had entered the Immortal Picture Scroll this time would probably be annihilated.
"At least there's still one person left. The Fated Kin still have hope," one of the Fated Kin elders said.
"Not a single person from the Swallow Victory Clan is left …"
"The Three-Eyed Kin has two survivors. It's a great fortune!"
Just as the Nirvana Realm beings were counting the remaining people, someone pointed at the Immortal Picture Scroll. "They're coming out. They're about to enter the whirlpool!"
After entering the third page, the Nirvana Realm beings could be clearly seen in the painting. Many of them had already appeared at the bottom of the giant whirlpool.
Within the Immortal Picture Scroll …
Fei Yu, Kuai Jian, and the other survivors stood to the side. They did not enter the whirlpool immediately. Instead, they silently observed it.
A type of black sand floated in the giant whirlpool. This sand was called the Void Sand.
Occasionally, a grain of Void Sand would float over and hit Fei Yu, Kuai Jian, and the others. The Void Sand would immediately disappear, and a sand-sized hole would be left on the surface of their bodies. This was accompanied by a heart-wrenching pain.
Anyone who had entered the Immortal Painting and been to the huge whirlpool on the third page had an instinctive fear of the Nihility Sand. Even those who had reached the Immortal Realm were no exception. Whenever they mentioned the Nihility Sand, their faces would turn pale.
There would be no small change.
Some of the Nirvana Realm beings originally had the potential to reach the Immortal Realm, but after being tortured by the Void Sand several times, they chose to give up.
Not only could the Void Sand swallow the flesh of the beings who entered it bit by bit, but the shocking pain was also not something that anyone could endure.
Just as they were watching, Luo Zheng came out of the Endless Labyrinth.
"Brother Luo Zheng, are you alright?" Fei Yu was pleasantly surprised when he saw Luo Zheng.
When the other Nirvana Realm beings saw Luo Zheng, they heaved a deep sigh of relief. They were glad that Luo Zheng was able to come out alive.
Although these Nirvana Realm beings had their own standpoints, Luo Zheng had saved their lives. At least here, they could stand on the same side.
"I'm fine," Luo Zheng replied. "Why didn't you enter the giant whirlpool?"
"We were waiting for you," Fei Yu smiled.
In fact, with so many problems on the first and second pages, these people were also afraid.
Although there were no curled beasts on the third page, nor the so-called Black Calamity, they were afraid that there would be another accident. If there was someone as powerful as Luo Zheng in front of them, it would give them a certain sense of security.
"If that's the case, then we can go in!" Luo Zheng said.
Finally, they had reached the last step. Luo Zheng was slightly excited.
He had just taken a few steps when a grain of Void Sand in the distance flew over and hit the back of Luo Zheng's hand.
"Da!"
When this grain of Void Sand hit Luo Zheng's body, it disappeared instantly. A trace of inescapable pain was transmitted, as if a needle had pierced Luo Zheng's Yang Soul!
Although the pain was extremely short, it was very sharp, as if it could not be eliminated or avoided.
"This is the Void Sand?"
Previously, Heavenly Venerable of Primordial Beginning had told Luo Zheng that this giant whirlpool was formed by a vast amount of Void Sand. Every grain of Void Sand could devour a corresponding material.
If enough Nihility Sand hit him, he would be completely dissolved by the Nihility Sand.
But to Luo Zheng who had the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, this was nothing …
Luo Zheng lightly caressed the spot where he was hit by the Void Sand. Under the protection of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, it did not leave behind a hole, "It doesn't seem to have any effect?"
"The Spirit of Chaotic Origin can really block off the Void Sand …" Heavenly Venerable of Primordial Beginning was slightly astonished. He then continued: "This is indeed good news to you. But don't be careless. The Void Sand can not only dissolve your fleshy body, it can also dissolve your soul.
It has two aspects. "
"Dissolve my soul." Luo Zheng frowned slightly.

Chapter 3414
In the Fourth Heaven, Luo Zheng had single-handedly destroyed the You Xiong Clan's God Temple.
But at that time, Luo Zheng's fleshy body had a huge advantage. After all, those Sun Souls were limited in strength and could not fight against him.
But this was the Thirtieth Heaven!
Even Paramita Lifeforms at the Thirtieth Heaven were not weak.
Furthermore, after the Sun Soul transformed and became a Sacred Soul, his strength had increased tremendously. He similarly had the capability to fight against the fleshy body!
Luo Zheng stood on top of the ice wall, attracting the attention of many.
Nobody had ever dared to behave atrociously in the Ice City. Luo Zheng standing here was a form of provocation!
"Where did this reckless human brat come from …"
On a platform not far away stood a few burly creatures. They were covered in silver scale armor, and their bodies were tall and sturdy. Golden hair fluttered on their heads.
That was the Scaled Lion Race, once the overlord civilization of the 337 worlds of Primal Chaos.
The Scaled Lion Race had a good relationship with the Primordial Spirit Civilization. After entering the Paramita Realm, they directly submitted to the Primordial Spirit Civilization. They were one of the most loyal Paramita Civilizations to the Primordial Spirit Civilization!
When these Scaled Lion Race members saw Luo Zheng invading, they naturally felt an intense hostility!
"Ao …"
Accompanied by a primitive roar, these Scaled Lion Race members abruptly leaped up. After jumping repeatedly on the ice buildings, they climbed up the ice wall and charged towards Luo Zheng!
These Scaled Lion Race members did not rely on their sacred techniques. Instead, they cultivated their unique combat instincts. With the help of their combat instincts, almost every strike of theirs was a fatal strike. At the same time, when dealing with the fatal attacks of their enemies, they would try their best to avoid or neutralize them!
If they were facing an opponent on the same level as them, the Scaled Lion Race members were practically undefeatable. But the moment the opponent was stronger than them, the boost of their combat instincts would be limited.
Luo Zheng's fleshy body had been strengthened to the limit by the Body Conquering Dipper Jade. He even had the support of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
With his current strength, other than those Five Elements Spirit Slaves, most Paramita Lifeforms were nothing in his eyes!
In the instant that the first Scaled Lion Tribesman charged in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng lowered his body slightly and punched upwards, smashing heavily into the Scaled Lion Tribesman's abdomen.
This Scaled Lion Tribesman's entire body was covered with extremely tough scales. Only his abdomen was a soft spot. The power of Luo Zheng's punch was transmitted through his abdomen, and the Scaled Lion Tribesman's entire skeleton was instantly broken. The Scaled Lion Tribesman's body withered beside Luo Zheng.
Just as he finished off the first Scaled Lion Tribesman, the second Scaled Lion Tribesman stretched out its sharp claws and slashed towards Luo Zheng's head!
Luo Zheng's hands were as fast as lightning. Even though he moved later, he caught the sharp claws with a backhand and spun his body!
Using the Scaled Lion Tribesman as a weapon, he began to spin!
"Bang bang bang bang …"
The few Scaled Lion Tribesmen at the back couldn't dodge in time and were directly smashed down the ice wall. Then, Luo Zheng threw the Scaled Lion Tribesman he was holding onto into the air, drawing a parabola in the air and smashing into a building in the distance.
"The commotion isn't big enough." Luo Zheng looked into the distance. Since they were here to cause trouble, the bigger the commotion, the better.
Just as Luo Zheng was pondering how to create a bigger commotion, a voice suddenly sounded in his ears. "The younger generation will surpass us in time, but … it ends here."
Hearing that voice, Luo Zheng was shocked.
Just as he turned around, a figure flashed behind Luo Zheng. It was a human Yang Soul. In his hand was a brush that was taller than a person. In the instant he flashed past, he drew a stroke on Luo Zheng's back!
Luo Zheng knew that the situation was bad and wanted to distance himself from this person.
However, although this person was only a Yang Soul, his movements were like that of a ghost. The entire Yang Soul was like a maggot that followed Luo Zheng's movements. At the same time, he drew a large stroke.
"Shua!"
The second stroke was drawn on Luo Zheng's chest!
The third stroke …
The fourth stroke …
The fifth stroke …
During this process, Luo Zheng used all of his skills. He dodged, counterattacked, and even soared into the air. However, the Yang Soul still entangled him and left a stroke on his body!
"The brush dust is gone, the stars of Xiushan move, seal!"
After the Yang Soul drew seven strokes on Luo Zheng's body, he suddenly spat out a sentence.
Luo Zheng felt as if the strokes on his body had come alive. They were like snakes moving on the surface of his body, connecting with each other and tightly entangling Luo Zheng!
The power in Luo Zheng's body was actually sealed. He smashed into the ice wall from mid-air.
The Yang Soul carried the ink brush and walked over slowly.
Although the Yang Soul's appearance couldn't be seen clearly, looking at his stooped body, he should be an old man.
This old man's name was Qi Ran. He was also a powerhouse in the Divine Farmer Clan who had been active in the 32 Levels of Heaven for a long time.
"Heh, heh, heh, the younger generation really will surpass us in time. With the strength of one person, you blocked the invasion of the Jinwu. Now, you've directly killed your way into the Yu Sheng-tian," Qi Ran said with a faint smile.
As he spoke, he carried the large brush and slowly walked closer.
The large brush was a unique magic weapon called the Dragon Sealing Brush.
When used together with Qi Ran's Soul Magic, it could seal all things in the world. Even those powerful existences in the Nirvana Realm would be easily sealed with just a few strokes!
Luo Zheng lay on the ground and stared at the old man indifferently.
He really didn't expect these old guys who had stepped into the Holy Soul Stage to be so powerful in the Nirvana Realm!
"But since you dared to break into the Ice City, you're courting death. Genryū will be very happy to hand you over to them," Qi Ran walked in front of Luo Zheng and reached out to lift Luo Zheng up.
But the moment Qi Ran lifted Luo Zheng, the brush strokes wrapped around Luo Zheng's body suddenly broke. The seal of the brush strokes was actually broken in an instant!
"How is this possible?"
Qi Ran was extremely shocked. At the same time, a strong sense of crisis rose in his heart and he quickly retreated.
BANG!
Although Qi Ran retreated extremely quickly, he was still hit by Luo Zheng's fist.
Qi Ran had stepped into the Holy Soul Stage for a long time, and the degree of his soul's stability was comparable to the degree of his physical body. But being hit by Luo Zheng's solid punch was not a good feeling!
When his entire soul was smashed away, Qi Ran decisively retreated from the Nirvana Realm!
PUFF!
In a palace in the mother world, Qi Ran suddenly opened his eyes and spat out a mouthful of blood. His entire face was as pale as a sheet of paper. His vision turned black, and he fainted directly!
Qi Ran didn't know that his brush sealing technique was just a child's play in the eyes of the Heavenly Lord of Origin.
When he had just sealed Luo Zheng, the Heavenly Lord of Origin had already begun to unseal Luo Zheng. At the same time, he reminded Luo Zheng to wait for this person to come closer to give him a surprise attack.
"This Holy Soul Stage cultivator's strength is really strong. It's really unexpected!" Luo Zheng shook his head.

Chapter 3415
In all of history, they had never heard of anyone obtaining eighteen seals. None of the previous people had obtained such a dense protective seal light.
Even Luo Zheng felt that the protective seal light was too dazzling. It took him a while to get used to it.
The others stared at Luo Zheng in a daze for a while, their eyes filled with envy.
With this level of protective seal light, he could easily enter the inner circle and even reach the first three circles, the first two circles …
Everyone had entered Soul City and the Calamity Bone Land. Why was there such a big difference?!
No wonder Genryū and the Wu Kong Clan were fighting over this kid. Was this the inside story?
On the other side of the plaza, there was another huge hexagon array.
Everyone gathered in front of the hexagon array after obtaining the protective seal light. Standing there, they could see the void sand floating on the ground.
"Are we going to charge out together? I can take the lead, "Luo Zheng said.
The void sand would dissipate when it hit Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng took the lead and blocked the front, the people behind could take advantage of it.
But as soon as Luo Zheng spoke, the people behind him had strange expressions.
"Brother Luo Zheng, you can't do that," Fei Yu shook his head.
"Why not?" Luo Zheng was puzzled.
"The more people gathered together, the more concentrated the void sand's attacks will be," Kuai Jian replied.
When the immortal painting was first designed, they had considered the problem of entering in groups. After all, if they found a strong person to block the front, the people behind would be able to take advantage of the chaos. It would be too easy to enter the inner circle.
Some people had tried this before, but when too many people entered, the void sand in the whirlpool would gather crazily and become even harder to resist.
Since then, everyone had learned to be obedient. They had to enter the great whirlpool one by one.
"But this protective seal light …"
Luo Zheng felt that the protective seal light was very strong, but maintaining such a strong state required a lot of energy. It could only be maintained for a limited time.
