"Brother Luo Zheng, don't worry. As long as we're in the square, the protective seal light won't weaken," Fei Yu assured.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything more. After all, it wasn't the first time for these people to enter the Immortal Scroll. Their experience was passed down through countless generations, so it shouldn't be wrong.
"Then I'll go first!"
The Fated Kin was the first to step forward.
He took a step forward and stepped onto the Hexagonal Star Array. Then, he transformed into a painting and dashed forward.
At the instant the Fated Kin rushed out, the Nirvana living beings in Ping Yi Sky gathered their gazes on him, and the Fated Kin, in particular, let out cheers.
The last remaining Fated Kin had eight seals, and his Kalpa Bone and Sacred Soul each had four seals. It was very balanced. The more balanced the physical body and soul were, the more balanced the protective seal light would be.
"Junior Brother Li Ming will definitely achieve a good result!"
"His goal is to enter the first seven laps. He will definitely make it."
"I believe in Li Ming's strength …"
The Fated Kin was running wildly in the Great Whirlpool.
He had just left the plaza when grains of Nothingness Sand flew toward him. His chest was hit by more than ten grains of Nothingness Sand at the same time, but the moment they touched the protective seal light, they were instantly dispelled by the protective seal light.
Of course, a portion of the protective seal light was also dispelled, but the amount of seal light dispelled by more than ten grains of Nothingness Sand was almost negligible.
With the protection of the protective seal light, the outermost ring of the Great Whirlpool did not hinder him at all. In less than the time it takes for a stick of incense to burn, he had already gone around it. Next, he went around the nineteenth ring, the eighteenth ring, the seventeenth ring …
The many living beings in Ping Yi Sky could see him very clearly. In comparison, Luo Zheng and the others could not see clearly. They could only see a golden dot of light dashing on the ground in the distance.
Not long after, the Fated Kin had already entered the first ten rings.
On the ground outside the first ten rings, there were a few Hexagonal Star Arrays scattered sparsely. Behind these Hexagonal Star Arrays were circular rooms.
If this Fated Kin wanted to obtain a bloodline, he could just choose a Hexagonal Star Array and enter it.
However, this Fated Kin called Li Ming had set his goal at the seventh ring. He had no intention of compromising.
After he entered the ninth ring, the vast expanse of Nothingness Sand attacked him.
The Nothingness Sand madly corroded his protective seal light. The golden protective seal light was dimming at a speed visible to the naked eye.
"Junior Brother Li Ming's protective seal light seems to be very weak!"
"It looks like it'll be very difficult for him to maintain it until he reaches the seventh ring."
"… …"
The Fated Kin were a race that was filled with suffering. Even though they were under Genryū's protection, they were weak and were not valued. They had always been oppressed by the other races that bullied them. Even now, the Clan Leader only had one Immortal Realm cultivator.
As Li Ming gradually grew stronger, he swore to himself that he would definitely become a powerful Immortal Realm cultivator and explore the Thirty-three Skies to revitalize the Fated Kin.
He had this ambition in his heart, which was why he was exceptionally ruthless.
"The eighth ring!"
After he entered the eighth ring, the protective seal light on the surface of his body was already very weak.
Logically speaking, the eighth ring should be his final destination, but he refused to do so. If he wanted to enter the seventh ring, he would enter the seventh ring. Every time he went deeper into the ring, his bloodline would become slightly stronger. He had to reach his limit.
If he could not even do this, how could he talk about revitalizing his race?
When he was halfway through the eighth ring, Li Ming's protective golden light had completely disappeared.
Once the protective golden light disappeared, the situation would become extremely difficult and dangerous.
"Junior Brother Li Ming, hurry up and choose a bloodline!"
"Your protective seal light is gone!"
The Fated Kin were all screaming loudly, but they were in Ping Yi Heaven. It was impossible for their voices to reach the Immortal Painting, and with Li Ming's current condition, even if he heard them, he would not stop moving.
The grains of Nothingness Sand hit his arms, chest, stomach, and legs, leaving behind countless sand-sized holes. The concentrated pain was like thousands of needles stabbing madly into his Yang Soul Body.
If his willpower was slightly weaker, the pain would have made him faint, but Li Ming still gritted his teeth and used his arms to protect his face as he continued forward.
When he neared the seventh ring, most of the arms he used to protect his face had already been eaten away by the Nothingness Sand. After the bones inside broke, they fell off from his shoulders.
His legs had also been eaten away until they were incomplete, and he could only limp forward.
The Fated Kin shouted in Ping Yi Heaven. They saw Li Ming's picture on the Immortal Painting becoming smaller and smaller. In the end, he was practically staggering forward on the ground.

Chapter 3416
After the light in the distance gradually disappeared, Fei Yu said thoughtfully, "That Fated Kin brother failed."
"Failed? How do you know? "Luo Zheng asked in surprise.
They observed from the plaza and could only see a dot of light spinning quickly in the distance before disappearing. Luo Zheng did not know how Fei Yu came to this conclusion.
"If he entered the room to fuse with his bloodline, there would be a flash of light. Now that the protective light has disappeared, there is no flash of light. This means that the Fated Kin brother did not enter the room," Fei Yu explained.
"He's trying too hard," Kuai Jian said as he shook his head.
Actually, the third whirlpool was relatively safe. As long as one's body was not completely corroded, one could choose a room nearby to obtain a bloodline and successfully reach the Immortal Realm.
However, some people were too greedy. They did not have the corresponding strength, but they still wanted to forcefully reach that step.
"Let me go!"
At that moment, the Jade Top Kin stepped forward. After he stepped into the Hexagonal Array, he quickly left the plaza and charged into the depths of the whirlpool.
After about half an hour, the protective light dimmed, and a flash of light shot into the sky. The Jade Top Kin had already chosen a room to enter. After fusing with his bloodline, an Immortal Realm cultivator was born.
"This Jade Top Kin is quite rational. From the distance, he should be within the ninth circle. This is similar to the strength of the seals he obtained," Fei Yu said as he nodded at Kuai Jian. It was now Kuai Jian's turn to enter the whirlpool.
Kuai Jian's seals were the same as the Jade Top Kin's — seven. He did not have high expectations. After reaching the ninth circle, he simply chose a room with a Hexagonal Array to enter.
Next up were a few other members of the Nirvana Realm. Perhaps it was because of the tragic fate of the Fated Kin, but these people's desire to gamble had completely disappeared. This time, they did not dare to grit their teeth and fight recklessly. Most of them took advantage of the fact that the protective seal light was still around to choose a six-pointed star.
Array room.
Fei Yu was the third last to enter the whirlpool. The protective light from the ten seals was enough to ensure that he could go further. In the end, he reached his goal and entered the sixth circle.
In Flat Sky, the Three-Eyed Kin members were filled with joy. After the Black Calamity, two of them had successfully reached the Immortal Realm. Fei Yu himself had reached his goal of entering the sixth circle.
There was light.
"The sixth circle should be the peak of the Immortal Scroll, right?" A Three-eyed Tribesman said proudly.
The disciples of the large clans would either die on the first page or the second page. There was simply no chance for them to compete.
But at this moment, another person began to dash out of the whirlpool. That person was very small and was from the Mastiff Race.
The Guis had an even smaller presence in Flat Sky. They were even weaker than the Fated Kin, so it was only natural that the outsiders did not think highly of them.
But when the Zhi people saw their own clansmen appear, their faces were filled with anticipation.
How many years had it been since the Gui Clan had produced such a genius?
If he could enter the inner circle and reach the Sempiternal realm, he would be able to raise the strength of his clan by a notch.
Under the indifferent gazes of the many Nirvana Realm races, the Gu Clan member actually entered the first four rounds in an unparalleled manner and obtained a bloodline in the fourth round.
The inner circle bloodline was the origin bloodline.
Since ancient times, very few people had obtained it. At the very least, out of the thousands of times the immortal picture scroll was opened, only one person could enter the inner circle!
So when the Gu Clan member soared into the sky, many Nirvana Realm races were in an uproar.
This unknown small clan actually produced a person from the inner circle. Indeed, this person could change the fate of the Gu Clan.
However, some of the Nirvana Realm races that were close to the Gu Clan did not look too good. Usually, there were many disputes between the few races. Naturally, they did not want to see such a person appear in the Gu Clan.
"Why isn't it Luo Zheng's turn yet?"
"It should be soon."
"He's the last one!"
Gan Gaohan and the others were in Flat Sky. They knew that every time the immortal picture scroll opened, it was like a grand event. But as they came from the Mother World, they were often absent.
This time, Luo Zheng came with 18 seals. They wondered what kind of expressions these fellows would have when they saw him.
On the square, after Luo Zheng saw the blazing light that soared into the sky in the distance, he stepped into the Hexagonal Star Array. Then, he turned into a flat pattern and moved forward along the whirlpool without hesitation.
Even after turning into a picture, the protective seal light was still very bright.
Any Nihility Sand that approached was blocked by the protective seal light. Luo Zheng moved forward very easily.
In the first 16 laps, Luo Zheng almost did not encounter any resistance.
After a while, he smoothly entered the first four laps, which were the inner circle.
Compared to the "miracle" created by the Gu Clan, Luo Zheng's performance also received a lot of attention. But the attitude of Genryū and Wu Kong Clan had already shown that Luo Zheng was not ordinary. Luo Zheng entering the inner circle was within the acceptable range.
After all, the person who was favored by Ah Huo and Tongtian could not even enter the inner circle. Wouldn't that be funny?
After entering the inner circle, Luo Zheng did not relax. Starting from the inner circle, the particles and density of the Nihility Sand increased a lot. The Nihility Sand was crazily consuming his protective seal light.
Although his protective seal light was very strong, it would not last long if it continued to be consumed like this.
However, the closer to the inner circle of the whirlpool, the shorter the distance.
Luo Zheng did not stop at all. He quickly moved forward along the path in the whirlpool.
The first four laps …
The first three laps …
The first two laps …
The creatures of the Nirvana Realm in Ping Yi Heaven remained silent. They watched Luo Zheng smoothly enter the top four, top three, and top two. Their expressions changed drastically.
There was a difference in strength between the experts of the Immortal Realm, and this difference was determined the moment they reached the Immortal Realm.
Those in the outer circle were ordinary Immortal Realm experts. Those who could enter the inner circle were qualified to become the clan leader of a big force. Of course, the biggest possibility was to be directly recruited into Genryū or Wu Kong Clan.
The creatures of the Nirvana Realm in the first three laps and two laps were the mainstays of the conquering 33 Heavens. They could even dominate a region in the future.
The situation of those in the inner circle was different. They were the strongest existences with the potential to compete for the entire Nirvana Realm!
Now, Luo Zheng had easily entered the first two laps and had no intention of stopping. It seemed that he intended to rush into the inner circle?
"No wonder the two big clans are competing like this …"
"So they already knew it!"
"So far, only eleven Supreme Bloodlines have appeared in the first circle. I wonder if that human kid will get the twelfth one?"
Some of the Nirvana Realm clans called the bloodlines in the inner circle "Supreme Bloodlines" and believed that they were the best.

Chapter 3417
Under the gaze of many Nirvana Realm creatures, Luo Zheng rushed into the innermost circle.
The Nothingness Sand in the innermost circle was dark purple. These Nothingness Sand were more corrosive than the Nothingness Sand in the outer circle. As if they had intelligence, they turned into bloodthirsty mosquitoes and swarmed toward Luo Zheng.
"Buzz …"
The originally extremely sturdy Protective Seal Light was swaying wildly, like a candle flame fluttering in the wind, about to be extinguished at any time.
If it weren't for the fox girl, Luo Zheng might have stopped here.
Not far from Luo Zheng's left, there was a hexagonal star formation. After entering the hexagonal star formation, one could safely reach the room behind it. There was a strange flat pattern drawn in the room. This pattern was probably related to obtaining the bloodline.
However, the fox girl's words were still ringing in his ears. Luo Zheng had no choice but to obey. If he wanted to pass through the innermost circle smoothly, he had to speed up!
"Rush over!"
However, just as Luo Zheng accelerated, the Nothingness Sand reacted even more violently.
The vast expanse of Void Sand seemed to have formed a solid monster as it rushed towards Luo Zheng to embrace him.
Countless Nothingness Sand and the Protective Seal Light canceled each other out, eroding Luo Zheng's shaky light.
"No, I can't go on like this." Luo Zheng's eyebrows were tightly knitted together.
Looking at the Nothingness Sand fluttering around the picture scroll, he suddenly had an idea. He suddenly looked up and his entire figure was pulled out of the immortal picture scroll, leaving only two feet standing on it.
"Eh?"
"What's happening?"
"That human kid only has two feet left? Could it be … "
"I'm afraid his entire body has been eroded by the Nothingness Sand, leaving only his feet!"
Many Nirvana Realm creatures who were observing Luo Zheng in the Pingyi Sky let out a sigh of pity.
The Three-Eyed Race also looked at the Divine Souls of the Heavenly Palace not far away with pity.
"What a pity. He should have fused the bloodline after entering the first circle."
"Sigh, I guess that kid is too greedy and wants to get a ranking. In fact, the ranking in the inner circle is not very important. He's still too young and doesn't know when to stop."
"It's a pity that this human is comparable to that Fated Kin. That Fated Kin was just an ordinary Immortal. Once this human becomes …"
Gan Gaohan, Ning Xuyuan, Li Bianxue, and the rest of the Heavenly Palace's experts stood motionless like statues. When Luo Zheng entered the inner circle, their hearts almost flew out of their chests.
This guy who had created miracles along the way was still continuing his miracles.
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng's footsteps would come to an abrupt halt after just a few steps …
Gan Gaohan, in particular, was stunned on the spot. He suddenly thought that if Luo Zheng died, it would bring a series of changes to the Heavenly Palace. The Heavenly Palace would probably have to move away from the Center God Province. What would happen to Donghuang and Old Ying in the end?
There was a huge change.
Whether he admitted it or not, this young man was already indispensable to the Heavenly Palace.
The pitiful and regretful voices that entered their ears were like needles pricking them, making it hard for them to breathe. For a moment, no one spoke. They just stared at the footprints.
"No, the protective seal light around Luo Zheng hasn't dissipated yet. He shouldn't have died, right?" Li Bianxue suddenly said.
Although the Heavenly Palace's people had never entered the Immortal Painting, they had a general understanding of it. The Void Sand had to first erode the protective seal light before it could begin to erode the physical body.
Just a moment ago, Luo Zheng was only left with a pair of "footprints". Everyone instinctively thought that Luo Zheng's body had been devoured, but after thinking about it, they realized that was not right. Although the protective seal light was weak, it was still there!
Li Bianxue was not the only one who realized this. Many people in Ping Yi Sky pointed out this problem.
"It moved! The footprints are moving! "Someone shouted loudly.
"They disappeared, then reappeared. This, this …"
"I know, this is the Insect Tapir's ability! This guy must have a way to escape from the Immortal Painting! "
They had long speculated about the Insect Tapir's ability to disappear and reappear. The Insect Tapir should have escaped from the Immortal Painting in the form of a "painting". That was why it could move in a discontinuous manner in the Immortal Painting.
Such an ability was naturally incredible. After all, the Insect Tapir could move in a higher world.
No one had thought that Luo Zheng could also do it. How on earth did this guy do it?

The flat soles of his feet.

Up and down like this, Luo Zheng seemed to be swimming. He jumped out of the water for a while, then dived back into the water for a while. The purple Void Sand followed closely behind Luo Zheng. Using this strange way of moving forward, the Void Sand couldn't touch him at all.
When it came into contact with Luo Zheng, the Body-protection Seal Light on the surface of his body was almost undamaged.
In a short while, Luo Zheng had passed through the Hexagonal Star Formation that the predecessors had entered before.

Chapter 3418
Looking at Hou Qu's sharp eyes, Luo Zheng gestured, "Use the method of deciphering Sanskrit to decipher it …"

.............. How do those large creatures pass through these bone caves? "Luo Zheng asked.

. and even Pang Hao's clansmen. Their fate would be even worse if they entered.

The spirit said.

Chapter 3431

Li Hu Province.
It was a small, remote province in the west of the Mother World.

These seven giant flying boats flew at a very low altitude in order to not attract attention. But because of their large size and extremely fast speed, they naturally produced strong wind pressure. Wherever they passed, the ground would be plowed.
"Senior Gan, aren't you being too careful by taking a detour?" A Greater One Mountain expert asked.
It wouldn't take long for them to enter Li Mountain. As long as they entered Li Mountain, it would be much safer.

Chapter 3432

But of course, the people of the Kurofune didn't expect that Shinno could borrow Gui Qing's ability. With Gui Qing's means of controlling a province, as long as it was in Li Hu Province, everything would be spied on by him.
"Okay, Sister Shen Tu," Xiao Lan nodded.
Xiao Lan's life was very pitiful. Her family had been extinct, and only one of them was left.
She had never been famous in the Kurofune, but the other members of the Kurofune took good care of her, especially Shen Tu.
So, Xiao Lan also treated Shen Tu as her own sister.
Her gray eyes flashed, and the invisible boat suddenly collapsed. Naturally, Shinno's strike also missed.
Space condensed into a single point, and in the next instant, Xiaolan had already controlled the invisible boat to appear in another location 50 kilometers away.
To an Immortal Realm expert, a distance of 100 miles was nothing.
But whether it was Xiao Lan or the others on the black ship, they were very confident in the Invisible Boat.
Shinno would not have discovered it so quickly.
However, when Shinno saw the boat disappear, his face sank, and the eyeballs on his forehead flashed with a green light.
Ghost Green was crawling up from the ground.
The force that twisted his neck just now came from his own body, so the force that twisted his neck could not be transferred to the boundary wall.
Just as he was feeling puzzled, he suddenly felt his eyes bulging out. His eyes seemed to have grown legs and were dragging his head around!
"Again!"
This time, Gui Qing had learned his lesson.
He simply jumped up and turned his body in the direction of his eyeballs.
After a full rotation, his eyeballs returned to normal.
As for what had happened, Gui Qing was still in a daze.
After Shinno found the invisible boat through Gui Qing's eyes, he teleported again and slashed at the invisible boat.
Xiaolan also looked like she had seen a ghost.
When she moved the invisible boat, she did not leave any clues. Even an Immortal Realm expert could not track her, but Shinno was chasing after her.
"Go again!"
Before Xiaolan's voice fell, the invisible boat collapsed and disappeared again.
But just as the invisible boat moved, Shinno caught up again, and he was getting closer and closer to the invisible boat.
The black ship's members finally could not remain calm.
"How did Shinno find us …"
"Look, he's here again!"
"Did he leave a clue on this red crystal?"
The black ship's members tried their best to find clues. They were extremely reluctant to fight against Shinno.
"Could it be Gui Qing who told him?" Shen Tu asked.
"Yes, it's possible …" Xiaolan nodded.
Many years ago, Gui Qing easily found the invisible boat with his own ability.
"That guy …" The bronze man was also a little conflicted.
In the past, Gui Qing had caused trouble for the black ship. He did not expect that after his rebirth, Gui Qing would make a comeback. This guy was really born to be a troublemaker.
"What do we do? Do we find a way to kill Gui Qing? "Bamboo Pole asked.
"We can't kill him. Even Fuxi couldn't kill him, so it's even more impossible for us," Shen Tu shook his head.
Seeing Shinno closing in step by step, Xiaolan did not even have time to speak. As soon as she moved to a certain location, she had to move away immediately. If she was a little slower, the invisible boat would be destroyed.
Under such a chase, Gui Qing suffered.
Every time Shinno checked, Gui Qing's eyes would bulge and drag him in a circle.
He had to cooperate, otherwise his neck would be broken …
Thus, many people saw Gui Qing cursing and walking in circles, but no one knew what he was going through.
…
Directly above Hu Province.
The ship's boss stood on the bow of the black ship in a cloak.
On the edge of his black bamboo hat, black blood flowed. This blood seemed to be heated by high temperature, causing fine bubbles to appear.
As for the black ship, its surface was scorched.
Although there was no fire, the ship had been charred without anyone noticing, becoming a veritable "black ship."
Opposite the ship's boss floated a huge shadow of Nuwa. This shadow had already spread out its arms to embrace the ship and the ship's boss.
The only reason the ship's boss did not come personally was because he was entangled by Nuwa.
"Empress Nuwa, Lie Shan has already made a move against us. With his strength, he will take Luo Zheng away sooner or later. I don't think you should bother me," the ship's boss said.

No matter who got Luo Zheng, Li Shan could not accept the result.

She was confident in her judgment.

"Once Huang Xi wins, it'll make sense," Nuwa said calmly.
If Fuxi defeated Ji Xuanyuan, he would be free to fight over Luo Zheng. It didn't matter if there were other variables, at least on the surface.

Chapter 3440
A small black shadow smashed into a mountain that was thousands of meters tall. The mountain that had stood for thousands of years crumbled from top to bottom like soft tofu.
Boom!
In the blink of an eye, the mountain had completely disappeared.
The rocks that fell out of the mountain shot everywhere like a rain of hidden weapons. Anything that was hit by the rocks was pierced through.
To the creatures that lived in this area, this was the end of the world.
Buzz!
Then, a huge Eight Trigrams seal appeared in the ruins of the collapsed mountain. The seal's surroundings were very blurry. It was formed by overlapping many Eight Trigrams seals.

Boom!

Intense rumbles overlapped. The original sound could no longer be heard clearly. Instead, it was a continuous, high-pitched sound.
The overlapping Eight Trigrams seals exploded at the same time!
Looking down from the sky, the ground caved in.
Three hundred meters, ten thousand meters, ten thousand meters, a million meters …
The mother world was a hollow egg world. Almost all creatures lived on the inner shell of this egg. The shell of this egg wasn't thick. The thickest part was only five or six million meters, while the thinnest part was only a few hundred thousand meters.
Ten thousand meters.
The exits of the mother world were all dug in weak areas.
Li Hu Province was also in a weak area. It was only a few hundred thousand meters from the surface to the outside of the chaotic world. Now, Fuxi had smashed through it!
In the chaos, the Xuanyuan Sword glowed with golden light and blocked in front of Ji Xuanyuan.
The Great Path in the golden light was the Ultimate Defense Path. It was also the path with the strongest defense.
Even with the protection of the Great Path, Ji Xuanyuan was still in a difficult situation.
Ever since he destroyed Jiuli, Ji Xuanyuan had been very confident. Even though Human Emperor Fuxi was once the strongest in the Immortal Realm, that was a thing of the past.
With Genryū's help, Ji Xuanyuan's strength had improved by leaps and bounds. Whether it was the use of his bloodline or the combination of the Paramita Tokens, he had reached a new level.
In comparison, Fuxi was just an unorthodox country bumpkin. All he relied on was his talent and his fierce pursuit in the Three Pure Heavens.

Fuxi himself did not have many tricks up his sleeves. He did not even rely on the Nirvana Realm Token. He was purely strong!

After the Lihu Province was penetrated, the Chaotic Qi continued to pour in through the hole and spread throughout the Lihu Province.
"Zzz —"
After the first wisp of Chaos Qi passed through the rocks, it came into contact with a small and tender blade of grass. This blade of grass instantly withered, turned black, and died …
To weak living beings, Chaotic Qi was a deadly poison.
Although the Mother World was vast, most of it could not withstand the Chaos Qi. For the natives in the Li Hu Province, more than 90% of them would die.

Even though he was more than a hundred feet away, his voice could still be accurately transmitted.

The Natal Weapon was originally refined from one's bloodline. Ji Xuanyuan's words made sense.

In Fuxi's eyes, there was nothing in this world that he could not break.

The Xuanyuan Sword emitted a golden light, and three Sacred Paths appeared. They were the Path of Illusion, Path of Illusion, and Path of Truth. It was also one of Ji Xuanyuan's strongest supernatural powers.

The three Sacred Paths intertwined and rushed toward Fuxi in the Li Hu Province like a dragon.

Fuxi raised his fist and said coldly as he saw the three Sacred Paths coming toward him.
He did not care what kind of Sacred Path Ji Xuanyuan was going to use. As long as the Sacred Paths came close, he would shatter them!
"Huff, huff, huff …"
A few Eight Trigrams appeared on the Sacred Paths. As Fuxi threw a few punches in the air, the Eight Trigrams quickly exploded.
The Sacred Paths that should have been shattered did not. The three Sacred Paths were like illusions and avoided Fuxi's fist.
The three Sacred Paths were both real and fake, and they were unpredictable.
"Strength is not only reflected in the fist." Ji Xuanyuan's voice floated over.
The three Sacred Paths began to split. Three became six, six became eighteen, and eighteen became three hundred and twenty-four. Soon, they formed a fine grid that surrounded Fuxi.
"Huff!"
In the next moment, Ji Xuanyuan appeared outside the grid.
The golden light on the surface of the Xuanyuan Sword had reached its peak. Ji Xuanyuan thrust the sword forward, and the dense net immediately unleashed a terrifying power. All the threads turned into golden sword rays and shrunk towards Fuxi in the center!
"Shatter!"
In that instant, it was as if hundreds and thousands of swords had struck Fuxi's body.
Ji Xuanyuan did not attack, but once he did, he attacked with all his might.
The blood in his body seemed to boil, and his eyes turned crimson gold. He was betting all of his strength on this attack. He was going to tear Fuxi to shreds!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
The golden sword light had completely shrunk around Fuxi's body.
All of the sword light exploded at the same time, and the golden light was so bright that even the sun and moon dimmed.
The powerful beings from the Heavenly Palace, You Xiong, and Shennong, who were still fighting in the distance, were also attracted by the sun on the horizon.
However, Ji Xuanyuan was not Di Jun, and the Sacred Path sword he cultivated was completely different from Fuxi's Nine Suns Annihilation Sword.
After a short while, the golden light had already dissipated …
What happened next made Ji Xuanyuan's eyelids twitch.

"Too weak!

Chapter 3441
High up in the sky, Nuwa's shadow remained motionless. Nuwa's tentacles wrapped around the black ship.

'Since you don't dodge, I'll kill you first.'
Nuwa slightly twisted her waist. Her golden dress fluttered, and the enormous Nuwa Shadow moved in a circle, easily forcing Min Hu to step back.
Her other hand made a pinching motion in the direction of the boatman.
Nuwa Shadow did the same thing with one hand. The energy beam wrapped the ferryman up like a tentacle.
The erosion of pure energy beams was terrifying, especially when it came from Nuwa's Shadow.
Those energy beams embedded themselves in the ferryman's body at a visible speed. Blood gushed out of his sunken body, but the blood was vaporized by the energy beams as soon as it appeared!
Min Hu, who had retreated, was stunned when he saw this.
Was the ferryman trying to kill himself?
Was he killed by Nuwa, just like that?
Or, did Nuwa use some trick that he couldn't see through to prevent the ferryman from dodging?
At the moment, waves of shock rose in Min Hu's heart. He even wanted to run away as soon as possible!

Chapter 3442
The entire Land of Ancient Flesh was located in the southernmost part of the Three Pure Ones.

Using fire to melt the Land of Ancient Flesh could only melt a small piece. After that, the Land of Ancient Flesh was no longer afraid of fire …
Thus …
All kinds of methods could only be used once in the Land of Ancient Flesh.

Chapter 3443

。

Chapter 3445

Based on this property, the ship master helped the copper man make the bolong pillar.

Chapter 3447

What appeared in front of them was actually Li Hu Province!
After going around in a circle, they were actually back?
"This is impossible!"
"We have been going west. It's impossible to go back to Li Hu Province!"
"…"
The members of the black ship couldn't understand this scene at all.
Standing on the side, the captain wrapped in a black bamboo hat pondered for a moment and said, "Someone has tampered with the space. Even if the invisible ship opens the way, it still can't be broken. We are trapped in it."
The captain was warned by Goddess Nuwa, and then he encountered such a thing. Naturally, he had his own thoughts and guesses.
"Tampered with the space? A space that spanned across several continents? Who has such ability? "The bronze man asked.
The bronze man was still carrying Luo Zheng on his shoulder. It seemed that it was difficult for him to put down this handy weapon.
"It may be difficult for ordinary Immortal Realm experts to do it," the captain said solemnly, "But what about Kong, who has mastered the Truth Divine Ability?"
Kong was originally a Chaos Ancient God who stood behind the You Xiong Clan. If You Xiong and the Shennong Clan wanted to make sure nothing went wrong, it was possible for them to ask a Chaos Ancient God to help.
Behind the Path of the End and the Path of Order, there were Chaos Ancient Gods, because these two paths had a place for them.
And this was the disadvantage of the black ship …

Chapter 3448
For a long time, the Kurofune had been in hiding and hadn't established its own super force because of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
The Primal Chaos Ancient Gods didn't like the Kurofune, so the Kurofune naturally couldn't establish a foundation in the Mother World.
These six natural giants in the Primal Chaos silently influenced the situation of the Mother World.
"How powerful is Void's control over space …" Xiao Lan's eyes revealed a look of surprise.
The ship's boss once told Xiao Lan that in terms of talent in space, she was probably the best in the Mother World.
But no matter how high her talent was, she couldn't compare with the Ancient Gods who had mastered the truth of space.
"I'm afraid we'll soon see how powerful they are," the ship's boss said with a dignified voice.
The Kurofune wasn't afraid of a super force level battle.
But when it came to the level of Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, the Kurofune's strength was difficult to deal with.
Fortunately, the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods rarely took action in the past, and the Kurofune was lucky enough to avoid them.
But this time, it was unavoidable.

"Of course," the ship's boss narrowed his eyes slightly, obviously already prepared.
…
"Hong!"
A huge hole broke open at the bottom of the Kurofune, and then Min Hu drilled out of it.
His face was full of anger. He wanted to fight against Nuwa together with the ship's boss, but he didn't expect to be tricked by the ship's boss!
It wasn't easy to break free from the Kurofune, so he naturally had the idea of finding trouble with it.
Min Hu spread out his Divine Sense and soon found another Kurofune.
Spatial fluctuations spread in front of him, and he took a step forward and arrived next to the Kurofune, "Ship's boss, don't blame me for this!"
"Hu …"
A terrifying fluctuation came from Min Hu's body, and a steady stream of Shen Jun's power formed a ring around him as he threw a heavy punch at the Kurofune.
At the same time Min Hu threw a punch at the Kurofune, a loud noise came from inside the Kurofune.
"Hong!"
The loud noise inside the Kurofune came from Shinno, who had transformed into a Faun and was rampaging through the cabin.
Because Nuwa didn't have a proper response, even if the Faun was stronger than Nuwa, it couldn't blast the Kurofune away.
Now, Min Hu and the Faun worked together to smash the Kurofune!
"Huala!"
Seeing that the black ship had turned into countless pieces, Min Hu's face revealed a ruthless expression as he threw another punch forward.
Min Hu didn't know that the members of the black ship and the ship's boss had left. He only thought that the person on the black ship was the ship's boss. He didn't expect that it would be Shinno who transformed into a Faun.
The Faun, who was already very irritable, finally left the black ship. When it saw someone attacking it, it naturally went up to them.
Shinno's will had a hard time influencing the Faun, so of course it wouldn't see Min Hu as its ally.
Boom!
Min Hu, who was good at melee combat, was at a disadvantage against the undying Faun.
Countless cracks appeared on Min Hu's cyan armor after the fist collided with him!
"You are …"
Min Hu was stunned for a moment before he realized that this was the strange body formed by Shen Nong's bloodline.
It was during this moment that the Faun flipped over, its sturdy hoof landing squarely on Min Hu's chest.
"Crack!"
The armor in front of Min Hu's chest was instantly shattered, and his entire body smashed onto the ground like a meteor.
"Brother Lie Shan …"
Min Hu was just about to beg for mercy when he saw the Faun's horns stabbing towards him.
Min Hu was scared half to death. It would be too tragic if he died at the hands of one of his own.
He didn't have time to say anything. As soon as he landed, he leapt into the air, transformed into a streak of light, and flew away.
Min Hu was extremely fast, but the Faun was even faster. It chased after him without knowing what was going on.
…
Ji Xuanyuan flew along the realm barrier.
Behind him, a series of Eight Trigrams appeared on the realm barrier.
Boom, boom, boom, boom …
It was as if an invisible beast was gnawing away at the realm barrier, creating a series of giant holes in it.
Ji Xuanyuan was slightly slower, and the Eight Trigrams landed on his body.
His fist followed the seal …
As soon as the Eight Trigrams landed on Ji Xuanyuan, a fist of light exploded on his body.
At his peak, Fuxi was terrifyingly powerful. Even a casual strike from him could pose a great threat to Immortal Realm cultivators.
He was actually forcing Ji Xuanyuan into a corner …
"Boom!"
The fist of light and the Eight Trigrams simultaneously exploded on Ji Xuanyuan's body, but there wasn't much of a sound.
However, the power of the explosion had already seeped into Fuxi's body, wreaking havoc within it.
Ji Xuanyuan's body slammed heavily into the realm barrier. At the same time, he spat out a mouthful of blood, and a look of defeat appeared on his face.
He had considered the possibility of losing to Fuxi, but he hadn't expected to lose so miserably!
"How did you become so strong so quickly …" Ji Xuanyuan looked at Fuxi with a complicated gaze.
Fuxi stood in the air, glancing at his own palm. A hint of a smile appeared on his face. "It's not that I'm fast, it's that you're too slow. You thought you were strong enough to rely on Genryū, but before you knew it, you were slacking off.
How much do you know? "
In the past, Fuxi's strength hadn't been that far off from Ji Xuanyuan and Shinno's. They were all on par, and Fuxi wasn't confident that he could defeat Ji Xuanyuan.
However, after reaching the Immortal Realm, Ji Xuanyuan, Shinno, and the others had always been under Genryū's protection.
The resources that Genryū had given them could indeed allow them to advance by leaps and bounds for a short period of time, but they were also restricted in the Thirty-three Skies.
If Genryū felt that they shouldn't enter a place, they couldn't explore it.
This protected them, but also restricted them …
Fuxi and Nuwa, however, were different. Every step they took in the Thirty-three Skies was an enormous risk.
Even the Elemental Spirit Civilization, which controlled the 'exit' of the Thirty-three Skies, hadn't been able to completely explore the Thirty-three Skies. There were still many unknown regions.
Fuxi couldn't remember how many times he had narrowly escaped death, but what followed was a tremendous amount of benefits!
Unknowingly, he had also surpassed other Immortal Realm experts.
Ji Xuanyuan looked at Fuxi with a complicated gaze …
Without a doubt, he was jealous of Fuxi. If he had known what would happen, perhaps he wouldn't have made the choice he had in the past.
However, the path he had chosen was destined to be one he couldn't turn back from.
He was stunned, and a strange smile appeared on his face. "Then do you really think you've won?"
Fuxi clenched his fist. "At least … I'll deal with you first."
An eight trigram seal appeared on Ji Xuanyuan's body. Then, Fuxi punched out.
Boom!
This mountain-splitting, earth-shattering punch exploded against Ji Xuanyuan's body.

Fuxi's eyes narrowed, and he raised his head to look at the horizon.
In all of the primordial chaos, there was only one person capable of using spatial divine abilities to such an extent. That person was' Space '.
Upon realizing that 'Space' was present as well, the black vortex in Fuxi's eyes seemed to ignite as it spun wildly.
There was still an enormous gap between the Immortal Realm experts and the Primordial Chaos Primordial Gods.

Chapter 3449

Eight trigrams and fist lights exploded in front of Ji Xuanyuan, but Ji Xuanyuan seemed to exist in a different world from those powers.

Swoosh …

Buzz!
As the Xuanyuan Sword hummed, a saint path with intense killing intent slashed at Fuxi.
Fuxi punched out in the face of this sword.
Whoosh …
Whether it was the Eight Trigrams or the fist lights, they crossed this saint path. It was like two different spaces that could not intersect.
Boom!

After spitting out a mouthful of blood, he stopped using Greater Teleportation and flew into the distance.

"No matter how strong you are, you are still just a Sempiternal realm cultivator. You can only dream of going against the truth."
Ji Xuanyuan continued to walk in the air with his sword in hand. The accumulated power of the Divine Path on the Xuanyuan Sword grew denser and denser.
If everything went smoothly, Ji Xuanyuan didn't want to be empty-handed.
Primal Chaos Ancient Gods were the foundation of the entire Primal Chaos. Once they got involved in worldly conflicts, the situation would become complicated.

However, there were always variables. If Sky directly joined the battle, it was very likely that everything would be sped up.
But no matter what, Sky had covered himself with a layer of spatial armor. He was already in an invincible position!
Swish!
Ji Xuanyuan slashed out with his sword again. Fuxi didn't dare to use Greater Teleportation. He could only rely on his speed to dodge.
He could dodge one slash, but two slashes, three slashes … there would always be a time when he would be struck.

"Stop here!"
A cold light flashed in Ji Xuanyuan's eyes. Three more Divine Paths gathered on the Xuanyuan Sword.
Right now, Fuxi was just a target that couldn't fight back. If he couldn't even deal with this Fuxi, then he, Ji Xuanyuan, would be completely worthless.
Buzz ….
Accompanied by the sound of the sword, countless sword images poured down on Fuxi with the support of the three Divine Paths.
But just as Ji Xuanyuan thought that Fuxi had no strength to fight back, Fuxi's aura suddenly changed.
A dense green light appeared in his eyes. It was as if an extremely powerful life force had been injected into his body. The ghastly wounds on his body instantly healed.
The green light only lasted for a moment. Then, all the green light turned red. Fuxi's aura also became extremely dangerous.
Whoosh!
An instant later, Fuxi pounced on Ji Xuanyuan.
Even though he had the protection of the spatial armor, Ji Xuanyuan's heart jumped.
He didn't stupidly swing his sword to counterattack. Instead, he instinctively retreated.
"Crack!"
Fuxi's attack caused the spatial armor on Ji Xuanyuan's body to explode. His left arm was torn off by Fuxi.
Ji Xuanyuan wisely chose to retreat. If he had still trusted the spatial armor and counterattacked, he probably would have been killed by Fuxi!
After retreating far away, Ji Xuanyuan's expression became unsightly again. He looked at the sky like Fuxi. The thing he was worried about had happened. Another Chaos Ancient God had joined in.
With the Li Hu Province as the center, the hundreds of provinces around it, from the Center God Province to the Li Mountain, all saw a giant scorpion appear.
Countless living beings raised their heads to look at the huge scorpion, and two completely different feelings emerged from the depths of their hearts. One was a deep attachment to it, as if looking at where they came from was their origin, and the other was a deep fear.
Fear, as if it was an existence that could destroy everything.
"Ji" was originally a Chaos Ancient God that controlled the truth of life. When the Mother World was still in its infancy, "Ji" could interfere with the process of life and even create living beings. Thus, it was not an exaggeration to say that it was the origin of all things in the current Chaos.
However, not only could it create, but it was also the ruler of all living beings. It could create and destroy.
After "Ji" appeared, another giant figure appeared on the other side. It was a giant one-eyed monster. Apart from the giant eye, there was also a strange hunched body. It was Ji Xuanyuan.
It was "Void" who was covered in a spatial armor.
"You still don't understand?"
Void's voice echoed throughout the hundreds of provinces.
The Chaos Ancient Gods had their own standpoint.
Purely from the standpoint of the Chaos Ancient Gods, the Dao of Order was clearly beneficial to them. This was why "Void" was willing to help Ji Xuanyuan.
"Void" wasn't willing to fight "Ji". He and "Ji" came from the previous Chaos Epoch. Although they belonged to different races, the two races had always been on good terms in the previous Chaos Epoch.

Chapter 3450
Ji was silent in the sky for a while. A green light flashed through its huge eyes.
Hum!
In the invisible boat, a small light spot floated out of Luo Zheng's body. It was Ji's sub-soul 9527.

9527 was just a weak sub-soul, but the presence it released was exactly the same as Ji.
Whoosh!
The green light spot drew a thin line in the sky and shot into the sky, hitting Ji's head.
In Ji's mind, the original owner had already woken up. It was surrounded by many brain masters, like the emperor of humans.
It was like the king in the mind world, waiting for 9527.
9527 stood in front of the original owner and did a very "human-like" action. It bowed to the original owner for no reason.
"You are me, why do you have to do this?" The original owner thought for a while before saying, "Is it because you've been away for so long that you've started to be obsessed with your own consciousness?"
To some extent, a sub-soul was also an independent entity.
9527 had been in contact with Luo Zheng for a long time, and slowly developed its own personality.
It even resisted returning to the original owner's side. It wanted to be a special 9527, and not a sub-soul of the original owner.
"Yes, original owner," 9527 answered sincerely, "I … want to leave you."
As soon as 9527's "consciousness" was sent out, the brain masters around the original owner instantly exploded.
A mere sub-soul that had its own consciousness dared to ask to leave the original owner's consciousness?
In the mind world, this was definitely a rebellious action. Or rather, the many brain masters and sub-souls couldn't have this consciousness at all!
"You've made a great contribution, and you've also successfully helped Luo Zheng reach the Sempiternal realm. Release yourself first. Once I understand what happened during this period of time, I'll allow you to leave," said the Original Lord.
9527 didn't say anything and opened itself up.
Countless scenes were interpreted by the original owner in an instant. From Luo Zheng's journey to the original world to Luo Zheng's encounter in the other side, the original owner knew everything.
At first, when the original owner saw how 9527 helped Luo Zheng, the original owner was very pleased.
Luo Zheng's performance was amazing, but 9527 was still very important to Luo Zheng.
But as Luo Zheng ventured deeper into the other shore, the effects of 9527 grew weaker and weaker. In the end, it became almost negligible. Then, the blue jade spirit that appeared represented an entire civilization. Even the Primeval Lord of Heaven was a super powerhouse of the 33 Heavens …
This kid encountered all strange things along the way, far beyond the original owner's imagination.
When Luo Zheng directly replaced the Elemental Spirit Clan to control Yu Shengtian, I was even more shocked. This guy is really going to turn the sky upside down after entering the Nirvana Realm!
But when the original owner started to understand the memories of Luo Zheng entering the immortal picture scroll, the original owner was even more shocked.
Although 9527 kept silent and didn't disturb Luo Zheng, it could still roughly understand the communication between Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi.
For example, the center of the picture scroll, the Four Spirits Gate, and the Heavenly Lord Yuanshi's guesses were all transferred to the original owner through 9527.
The communication between the original owner and the original owner was actually very fast. The whole process didn't even take half a breath. Ji's huge eyes gently closed and slowly opened again. There was a hint of complicated emotions in his eyes.
Luo Zheng's performance was completely beyond his expectations, even beyond his imagination …
If Ji was still hesitant and wavering before, at this moment, he had no more worries.
"Of course, I understand," Ji's voice sounded in the air. The voice was easily transmitted to the ears of all the living beings in hundreds of states, but it was very peaceful, "Let go of Luo Zheng. The other unrelated people can leave, and we'll pretend that nothing happened today."
Hearing Ji's choice, Fuxi and Nuwa instantly heaved a sigh of relief. Ji Xuanyuan and the others also had a layer of coldness on their faces.
On the other side of the sky, Kong said, "You still don't understand. We are this world's …"
Kong was still trying to convince Ji, but Ji directly interrupted him, "Say no more. I've seen more than you understand. I won't believe their flowery words anymore."
Hum!
An azure light flashed across the huge eye in the sky.
Its aura quickly grew stronger …
"You, must you fight me?" Kong asked again.
"I only have one condition. Let go of Luo Zheng," Ji answered decisively.
From 9527's memories, Ji already understood the importance of Luo Zheng. He was so important that even if he had to give up his life, he had to let him stay.
Hum hum hum hum …
Kong's single eye started to vibrate crazily.
The concentrated power of space vibrated crazily in his eye.
He had the highest space authority in the chaos. Now that he couldn't reach an agreement with Ji, he planned to exile Ji.
Whoosh …
Ji knew how powerful Kong was. He naturally wouldn't allow Kong to exile him so easily.
Ji didn't delete the space. If he was casually exiled to a corner of the chaos, Ji would need time to return to his home world.
Clang clang clang clang …
Ji's huge body charged forward. Transparent space walls appeared in front of him.
Each space wall was as strong as a world wall. However, in front of Ji, they were as fragile as glass. They almost shattered with a single hit …
Although the space walls were shattered one by one, they still slowed down Ji's speed.
When Ji charged in front of Kong, the power of space in Kong's huge eye had finished. A thick blue light shone on Ji's body.
The blue light disappeared. Ji also disappeared.
"Hehehe …"

Then Kong's body started to shrink. From a body so huge that one couldn't look up, it shrank to the size of a normal person. At the same time, his entire body started to change. In the end, he turned into a human. It looked like a mountain.
He said that this was the complete "mountain".
In the next step, Kong already appeared beside Ji Xuanyuan. He reached out and pulled in the air.
All the space in Li Hu Province started to dislocate …
However, the key people were all pulled in front of him.
Shennong who turned into a faggot, Min Hu, the people from the black ship, and Nuwa …
They didn't feel the space ripple and weren't prepared. The surrounding scenery changed and they were pulled over.
Kong's gaze passed through the Unseen Boat and landed on Luo Zheng. He looked like he saw an important treasure. He pulled in the air and was about to move Luo Zheng over from the Unseen Boat.
However, Luo Zheng didn't move an inch despite its tug.

Chapter 3451
The boatman, Shen Tu, and the others also looked at the bronze man in surprise.

At this time, Shen Tu, Xiao Lan, and many other members of the Black Ship also joined in, joining forces to prevent the space from shrinking, but even with all the members of the Black Ship taking action, the Invisible Ship was still shrinking at its original speed.

Shen Tu turned to look at the Boat Captain. It would be too late if they didn't descend now.
Just as Shen Tu was looking around, a golden circle of light suddenly appeared behind the ship's captain, and under this golden circle stood a man in a black robe. This man had a pair of deep eyes, the depths of which seemed to be filled with stories.
However, the faint stubble on his face revealed an unruly aura.
"Need help?"
* Ka! *

As the man exerted force, the unbreakable space barrier of the Unseen Boat shattered into millions of pieces with a bang.

..
. Kong didn't think that the people of the Black Boat could escape. At least, they couldn't leave the Unseen Boat.
After dealing with the Black Boat, only the two from Li Shan were left.
Since he had made a move, he had to clean it up.
But just as he moved over, he felt a huge movement behind him.
"Kong" turned his head. When he saw the black-robed man, his cold eyes revealed a trace of seriousness.
In the Chaotic World, even the strongest Immortal Realm experts couldn't fight against the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods who controlled the truth. So, whether it was Fuxi or the Black Boat, Kong didn't put them in his eyes. At least, Kong felt that these Immortal Realm experts were no match for him.
But the man in black in front of him made him instinctively vigilant. This kind of vigilance only appeared when facing other Primal Chaos Ancient Gods. He didn't expect it to appear when facing a human, which made him a little confused.
Fuxi and Nuwa, who were pulled over by Kong, quietly looked at each other.
Of course, they knew that the man in black came from the Chaotic World and must be a member of the Wu Kong Clan. But the Wu Kong Clan had always been mysterious, so they couldn't confirm the identity of the man in black.
In fact, not long ago, the man in black had shown his face in the Thirty-second Layer of Heaven. If Gan Gao Han and the others were here, they would definitely recognize him.
But if the Wu Kong Clan could fight to the death with Kong, Fuxi and the others would be happy to see it.
"Who are you?" Kong asked.
The man in black sniffed the air, revealing the intoxicated expression of all Descenders. "I've been waiting for this moment for a long time. Even after thousands of Primal Chaos Epochs, the smell of this world hasn't changed."
The man in black didn't answer the question, and Kong's expression became even colder. He lowered his voice and asked, "Tell me, who are you?"
Saying so, Kong suddenly slashed at the man in black, and a crescent-shaped spatial crack flew straight at the man in black. A spatial crack created by a Primal Chaos Ancient God was extremely fatal.
Facing this spatial crack, the man in black smiled faintly and stretched out his hand to block the path of the crack.
"Hiss …"
When the man in black's hand collided with the spatial crack, there was a grinding sound that made one's teeth ache, and the spatial crack quickly disappeared.
Then he revealed a warm smile. "Who I am isn't important. What's important is that you have to hand that person over to me."
The man in black pointed at Luo Zheng on Kong's shoulder.
"You're dreaming." Kong replied.
If not for the fact that Luo Zheng could absorb all energy and couldn't be transported away, Kong would have hidden Luo Zheng in some corner of the Primal Chaos Epochs.
So what if this man in black was strong?

Chapter 3452
The black-clothed man's light brown eyes were devoid of any emotion.
To the onlookers, he looked like a taut bow, ready to release surging power at any moment.
But his emotions were like the surface of a still lake, calm as if he was taking a stroll in a courtyard. There was an extremely terrifying emotion trapped in his mind.
No one could see through him, not even "Kong".
"Pleading is useless, so we can only solve this with our fists," the black-clothed man said in a flat tone.
It was as if declaring war on a Primal Chaos Ancient God was a simple matter.

Inside this Space Cage, even if Luo Zheng woke up, he would not be able to escape, and external forces would not be able to break it. At the very least, the Immortal Realm cultivators present would find it difficult to do so.
But as soon as Luo Zheng touched the bottom of the Space Cage, a shocking scene occurred. Luo Zheng directly absorbed the bottom of the Space Cage, and Luo Zheng's body rapidly fell through the hole in the Space Cage.
Everyone present wore a speechless look upon their faces while Shen Tu secretly laughed.
Luo Zheng's body was too strange, causing "Kong" to misjudge it.
As far as he was concerned, all pure energy would be absorbed by Luo Zheng, except for the copper man's "Bolong Attraction". Perhaps it was a coincidence, or perhaps it was because the Bolong Attraction's energy was not high enough.
In fact, Shen Tu's guess was very close to the truth.
Luo Zheng did not absorb all energy, but according to the diagram in the square, the Bolong Attraction's energy was not high enough.
On the other hand, "Kong"'s energy was obviously enough, so every time "Kong" released his Space Force, Luo Zheng's body would immediately "eat" it.
After eating it a few times, another beam of light shot up from the Space-Time Quadrant of the diagram …
Seeing that Luo Zheng is falling, the man in black doesn't have the intention to grab him.

Li Shan was powerful not only because he had the support of the Primal Chaos Ancient God, but also because Fuxi and Nuwa were strong opponents.

Just as Ji Xuanyuan was thinking, a dazzling blue light suddenly bloomed in the sky. The blue light was even more dazzling than the sun. It was like a huge blue flare, so dazzling that everyone couldn't open their eyes.

"Kong" rarely fought head-on. Most of the time, he only needed to banish his opponent. Just like how "Mountain" had banished Luo Zheng in the space of Millet Mountain.
Previously, when "Ji" wasn't paying attention, he was also banished by "Kong".
However, facing this man in black, "Kong" had the urge to kill him!
"Buzz …"
Kong waved the long sword in his hand, and the space in front of him split apart like a piece of cloth, creating a huge space crack.
This space crack was like the mouth of a strange fish, trying to swallow the man in black.
The corner of the man in black's eyes twitched slightly. His body tilted slightly, and a wisp of green light as thick as a pinky shot into the sky, shooting tens of millions of feet away.
"Buzz!"
A three-foot-long thin sword appeared in his hand. It was his usual weapon, the Qingping Sword.
"Qingping Sword, Absolute!"
The moment the man in black slashed out with the Qingping Sword, he disappeared.
He and the Qingping Sword had turned into green light that filled the sky, shining towards "Kong".
The space crack that "Kong" slashed out disappeared the moment it met the green light. All the power of space seemed to be unable to withstand a single blow.
"Kong" didn't care too much that his attack was so easily dispelled.
He only looked up at the sky, wanting to find the man in black.
As long as one was deep in space, no one could avoid "Kong"'s detection. This was "Kong"'s talent. He didn't search for the other's aura, nor did he scan with his divine sense. Instead, he simply sensed everything that existed in space. This "Kong"
Ability was similar to Gui Qing's.
However, the man in black had completely disappeared under the green light? This didn't make sense.
"The truth of space is the highest law in the Chaos. As the Child of Truth, you can't completely control it. You only have a part of it …"
The man in black's voice suddenly came from behind "Kong".
"Kong" suddenly turned around and slashed out with the spatial saber in his hand in the direction of the voice.
Others might have misjudged the direction of the voice, but he, "Kong", would never be wrong!
Three slashes in a row …
The azure-blue spatial slash was fast enough to split everything in its path, including space itself.
Large patches of spatial cracks swept down as the spatial slash swept past. The sky was like a book, and these spatial cracks were like pieces of paper torn from the book. Under the powerful power of fear, space itself revealed a magical characteristic.
。

The man in black's voice sounded again. Just as "Kong" raised his spatial saber, he realized that he couldn't determine the source of the voice. It seemed to be transmitted from deep within his mind. Why was this happening?

"Hua hua hua …"

Chapter 3453

This guy was really not ordinary …

In these thousands of space cubes, there were countless flickering figures. These flickering figures were the black-clothed man.

The Sanqing Heaven was a magical place. It had all kinds of strange and strange abilities.

Chapter 3454

The control of the Ji Deleted Space.

Buzz!

Chapter 3455

Chapter 3456
