"Pu!"
A dot of green light went from front to back, piercing through Sky.
Confusion appeared in Kong's eyes. He stared at Tongtian and asked, "Only I can do those space distortions. Why did you …"
In fact, many people could connect two points in space, but it was just to create a circular space channel. The same was true for the spatial distortion of "Void".
However, most of the temporary tunnels created by people were unstable. For example, Tongtian's casual sword had the strength of hundreds of thousands of Godly Jun. Only Sky could distort it.
However, Kong just realized that this black-clothed man could do it too. This made Kong fall into confusion again. Ever since he fought with this guy, many things had happened that Kong could not understand.
"Why can I do it too?" Tongtian said as he slowly pulled out his sword. The Qingping Sword was slowly pulled out from the two distorted spaces. It looked very strange. "Because the truth divine power is not unique to you. Or rather, through some special means.
I can also grasp the same means. Moreover, I can learn more from you … "
Kong's eyes narrowed slightly.
If the two of them were just testing each other, now was finally the time to lay all their cards on the table.
Tongtian's words were very clear …
He could deprive a Chaos Ancient God of his abilities!
Whether he could do so was still a question mark. However, he had indeed used the same space distortion divine power as Kong.
"Then let's see if you have the ability!"
Kong's voice became deep and ferocious. His face was still changing shape. His pair of human eyes began to distort, merging into a single eye. His teeth became sharp, and his head began to expand wildly. He was going to return to his original appearance.
Fuxi, the ship captain, Ji Xuanyuan, and the other Immortal experts knew that the two almighties were going to use their strongest forms. Kong would return to his original form, while Tongtian would use his Rage God Body.
The scene that they expected did not happen.
The Prime of Tongtian just stood in the air and stared at Sky with a mocking expression.
Although Kong's body began to change, his body quickly shrank back. It was as if there was a layer of invisible shackles on the surface of his body, firmly trapping him in his original form.
"Your human form is quite good. There's no need to transform into that ugly, one-eyed monster."
The Grandmaster of Heaven gently flicked the Qingping Sword, and three thin threads that were wrapped around Kong's body quietly appeared. The white threads quickly expanded and turned into three white jade chains. The other end of the chains was connected to a yellow sword in the Grandmaster of Heaven's hand.
This lock was extremely exquisite, and there were strange Sanskrit characters on both sides. This was Tongtian's other magic treasure, the Heart-Piercing Lock.
Tongtian had previously hinted that he only needed to stab Kong three times with his sword to kill him. These three stabs were enough to send the Heart-Piercing Lock into Kong's body. At this moment, Kong's entire body was sealed by the Heart-Piercing Lock. All of his divine powers were sealed.
He was unable to use the Rage God's power.
Kong appeared to be motionless, but in reality, there was an intense struggle going on inside his body. He absolutely would not allow himself to be sealed by a mere Heart-Piercing Lock. He could not accept that a mighty Chaos Ancient God would fail in such a simple task.
Tongtian did not care about Kong's actions. He pulled the white jade chains and slowly descended.
Since he had already trapped Kong, it was now the turn of the imposters below.

Chapter 3457
After Tongtian landed, he glanced at Nuwa, Fuxi, Ji Xuanyuan, and the others.
There was an indescribable hatred in his eyes.
Although these people were on different sides, and they were just clones without memories, when he glanced at their faces, an inexplicable hatred welled up in his heart.
Fuxi, Nuwa, Ji Xuanyuan, and Shinno, who was slowly recovering from his Faun form, could all feel this hatred.
Min Hu, who was standing next to him, was secretly relieved.
This man in black was full of resentment toward Ji Xuanyuan, Fuxi, and the others, but he was expressionless toward him. Perhaps …
While Min Hu was still rejoicing, Tongtian suddenly turned to look at him and said indifferently, "Those who have nothing to do with this can die first."
Swoosh!
The Heart-piercing Shackle in his hand shook slightly, and another thin chain came out of it and went straight for Min Hu. However, this chain was not white jade in color, but black in color. Its grade was obviously lower than the white jade chain that trapped Sky, but it could be used to kill Sky.
However, it was more than enough to trap Min Hu.
When Min Hu heard Tongtian's words, his expression changed. His body leaned backward, and he was sent flying at high speed.
The space in front of the black chain distorted, and in the next moment, it appeared in front of Min Hu.
Puff!
The black chain pierced through the armor that Min Hu was so proud of, and it went straight into his chest.
The moment the black chain entered his body, the Disillusionment Vanguard armor on Min Hu's body collapsed instantly. At the same time, Min Hu's vitality was rapidly draining away.
In the blink of an eye, Min Hu's vitality was completely gone.
An Immortal Realm expert had died in the hands of Tongtian just like that.
Tongtian Sect Leader suddenly swung the chain. Min Hu's body was lifted up and flew toward the black ship.
"I'll let you deal with the body," Tongtian said.
When Shen Tu, who was on the black ship, saw Min Hu, he almost drooled.
The ghouls she created were generally weak, so Shen Tu often had to modify his own body.
Now, Tongtian had rewarded an Immortal Realm expert. More importantly, Min Hu was still alive, and there were many opportunities to modify him. In Shen Tu's eyes, Min Hu was a rare treasure that could only be found by chance.
"Many thanks, Sir Religion Master," Shen Tu happily caught Min Hu, his big eyes intentionally or unintentionally glancing towards Fuxi.
His meaning was clear, Shen Tu wanted to obtain the corpses of these Immortal Realm masters.
If Shen Tu could really refine these Immortal Realm cultivators into Corpse Ghosts, she would be able to roam the Mother World unhindered.
After seeing Min Hu's fate, Ji Xuanyuan and Shennong's faces were extremely gloomy, while Fuxi and Nuwa were accumulating energy in their bodies.
The two groups of people who had been fighting each other not long ago had no choice but to join forces to fight against this person!
But this black clothed man's strength was too strange …
Not only did he have the Space Truth Divine Ability that was impossible to guard against, but he also had the Heart-Piercing Lock that could lock up almost everything.
Fuxi and Nuwa secretly exchanged a glance. Nuwa had already taken out a Ring of Descent.
After obtaining the Golden Chaotic Origin Spirit from Luo Zheng, Nuwa had naturally researched on the usage of the Ring of Descent.
At first, Fuxi had been eager to give the opportunity to Trigram Hound to obtain the Ring of Descent.
Trigram Hound was indeed powerful. He was Fuxi's close friend in the Three Clear Sky. In terms of strength, he was slightly weaker than Fuxi, but much stronger than most Immortal Realm experts.
Yet after obtaining the "Ring of Descent" after much difficulty, Li Shan was left with another Immortal Realm expert? This was not what Nuwa wished to see.
Hence, Nuwa gave a "Ring of Descent" to the "Barbed Crocodile Clan" in the Shang Clear Sky.
This Barbed Crocodile Clan was an extremely powerful Paramita Clan in the Three Clear Sky. But this clan was extremely xenophobic and did not care about worldly affairs. Even Genryū did not dare to provoke them, much less enter their territory.
A long time ago, Nuwa had once saved a young Barbed Crocodile, earning the friendship of the Barbed Crocodile Clan.
The Barbed Crocodile Clan Head agreed to Nuwa's request and was willing to help Nuwa once during crucial moments to repay her kindness.
Unless it was absolutely necessary, Nuwa would not choose to descend.
But Nuwa did not expect that after watching the tigers fight, "Kong" had surrendered, and the lives of these Immortal Realm experts were in danger …
As for Fuxi, his eyes were still blazing with fighting spirit.
In Fuxi's opinion, apart from the Heart-piercing Lock and a few minor Theurgies in the Truth of Space, this guy had nothing to fear. If he really went all out, he might still have a chance.
Just as Fuxi and Nuwa were about to attack, the forehead of Ji Xuanyuan, who was carrying Luo Zheng, suddenly split open. At the same time, a hearty laugh sounded.
"Tongtian, why are you in such a hurry to descend?" It was Genryū Ah-Huo's voice.
Tongtian was not surprised to hear this voice. He and Genryū had been fighting for hundreds of chaos epochs. It would be strange if the Meta-Spirit Clan did not appear.
"As far as I know, you don't seem to be able to descend?" Tongtian smiled.
"Because they are too stupid," Ah-Huo said.
"It doesn't matter whether they are stupid or not." Tongtian shrugged. "If you don't have any other tricks up your sleeve, you can shut up. I have already won this battle."
With that, Tongtian raised the Qingping Sword and was about to stab the eyeball on Ji Xuanyuan's forehead.
But at that moment, Ji Xuanyuan's expression changed. He suddenly revealed an almost charming smile, and a girlish voice came from his mouth. "Although we missed the Ring of Descent, it doesn't mean we don't have any other options.
Law Descend! "
"Feng!"
Ji Xuanyuan's hair instantly burst into flames. His eyeball emitted a golden light, emitting an aura similar to the Ring of Descent.
His appearance, demeanor, and aura skyrocketed at this moment. He had become a living example of "Ah-Huo" in the Paramita Realm.
Before Tongtian could react, Ah-Huo had already grabbed Luo Zheng, stepped forward, and threw Luo Zheng at Tongtian.
"Buzz!"
A blue light flashed in Tongtian's eyes, and he used Space Deflection to dodge the attack.
"Oh no!"
"Sect Master …"
"That Luo Zheng …"
When the people on the black ship saw this scene, they all reminded him, but it was obviously too late.
The moment Luo Zheng came into contact with the distorted space, he had already devoured the Truth Energy that existed in the space. The space returned to normal. Luo Zheng naturally slammed into Tongtian.
"Puff!"
The Prime of Tongtian was sent flying.
Ah-Huo glanced at Luo Zheng in her hand and was very satisfied with this temporary weapon.
"Fire, come!"
"Feng!"
Ah-Huo wanted to ignite a fire on this handy "weapon". For a moment, she forgot the characteristics of Luo Zheng's body. The flame only lit up for a moment before it was directly absorbed by Luo Zheng.
She just made a face that she didn't care. She stepped out and came to the other side of the space. Once again, she swung Luo Zheng.
"Bang!"

Chapter 3458
The ground of Li Hu Province looked like a rag. As the two of them fought, the rag began to roll, wrinkle, and tear apart.
The creatures living on the rag fell like weeds. They did not know what was happening, but they felt the sky collapsing and the earth sinking. Even a small number of people who knew what was happening had no ability to fight back in a battle of this level.
Even the boundary wall, which was so strong that it could only be broken by the full force of an Immortal Realm expert, was weak under the collision of the two of them.
Whoosh!
After the ground sank to the south of Li Hu Province, the entire ground was torn apart. A crack that was millions of miles long was torn open. Through this crack, one could see the scene in the Chaos.
The negative pressure caused by the crack attracted the Chaos clouds not far away. The Chaos clouds swarmed toward the crack.
If the creatures in Li Hu Province could only wait for death to come, the people of the Heavenly Palace still had a chance of survival.
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace who escaped were at least in the Nirvana Realm. The Chaos Qi was not a threat to them.
However, under the shock waves from the battle between Ah Huo and the Sect Leader of Tongtian, they would be severely injured if not killed.
"Everyone, run to the west!"
"If you're lost, go to Li Mountain!"
"Go to the west and cross the boundary wall!"
The originally indestructible boundary wall was now full of holes. It could no longer stop them from escaping.
Li Bianxue, Gan Gaohan, and the others quickly organized the disciples of the Heavenly Palace to cross the boundary wall.
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace were not the only ones who escaped. Even the disciples of the You Xiong, Shinno, and Minyue clans returned to their flying boats.
A battle of this level had completely disrupted their previous plan. However, as long as they ran to Li Mountain, they might still have a chance of survival.
Ji Xuanyuan's body was occupied by Ah Huo. Shinno had just recovered from the state of a faun. Min Hu was dead. They had no time to care about the lives of these Saint Soul Realm experts.
Whoosh …
Another crack was torn open on the ground. Thousands of huge pieces of soil were thrown into the sky. Each piece of soil was the size of hundreds of huge mountains.
A two-winged earth dragon was hiding in one of the pieces of soil. When it sensed the movement outside, it quickly escaped from the soil and realized that it had appeared high in the sky.
Even though the Double-Winged Earth Dragon's cultivation had reached the True God Realm, its intelligence was low. While it was still pondering over the reason, a wisp of flame flickered in its direction.
The flame itself was still dozens of miles away from it, but the heat emitted by the flame was transmitted over from that distance, instantly roasting it from the inside out.
The Sect Leader of Tongtian felt that Sky was a hindrance, so he stuffed Sky into another space magic treasure of his, the Fishing Drum. Both of them showed incredible flexibility in the series of battles.
Although Ah Huo had fused with Ji Xuanyuan's body through the eyeball, his strength was not affected at all. He was not at a disadvantage when facing the Grandmaster of Heaven!
"Tongtian, ah no, Lord Hierarch! This is the second time Ah Huo has fought with you, right? Ah Huo feels that Lord Sect Master's strength hasn't improved! "Ah Huo said with a light smile.
The Tongtian Sect Leader's face suddenly became calm, but his heart was secretly shocked.
In terms of real strength, the Sect Leader of Tongtian and Mu Ling were almost the same. The several times they fought had already proved this point.
However, although Ah Huo was the third most powerful person in the Genryū, his strength was quite far from Mu Ling's. At least at that time, the Sect Leader of Tongtian did not take him seriously.
It had only been a few chaos eras, but he had grown to such a level?
"Whoosh!"
Just as the Sect Leader of Tongtian was pondering, Ah Huo swung Luo Zheng in front of him again.
It had to be said that Luo Zheng in this state was indeed a very perfect weapon.
The Sect Leader of Tongtian had several trump cards that were restrained by Luo Zheng. In Ah Huo's hands, Luo Zheng was a special weapon that could absorb all kinds of energy. The point was that he could not be damaged!
Now that the Sect Leader of Tongtian was in a passive situation, Luo Zheng had some credit.
After Fuxi and Nuwa regained their freedom, they had already flown to the southwest of Hu Province. The two of them did not leave.
"I thought I was the strongest in the chaos, but two monsters appeared so quickly …" Fuxi looked at the Sect Leader of Tongtian and Ah Huo in the sky and said resentfully.
"They are old monsters who have lived for countless chaos eras. Of course you can't compare to them." Nuwa's eyes were full of calmness.
The Wu Kong Clan and the Meta-Spirit Clan were determined to get Luo Zheng, and so was Nuwa. Now that they were not strong enough, they could only use their wits to get them.
Fortunately, the Sect Leader of Tongtian and Ah Huo had not decided the winner, so they still had a chance.
On the black ship …
The boatman did not observe the battle above.
If the Sect Leader of Tongtian was defeated, the black ship would have no meaning to exist. He only needed to wait for the result.
In comparison, he had more meaningful things to do.
"Buzz!"
Two more Rings of Descent were activated.
The other two members of the Wu Kong Clan appeared in front of the boatman.
One of them was the Sect Leader of Tongtian's disciple, Fairy Yunxiao, and the other was Evil Deities.
Evil Deities, who descended again, glanced at the members of the black ship and only snorted.
"Boy, what do you mean?" The bronze man's face immediately became gloomy.
The people of the black ship were not kind people.
Evil Deities was able to grow so fast and get rid of Luo Zheng to enter the other side. It was all thanks to the black ship.
But this guy had only risen for a few days, and he was already arrogant.
"Don't bother with him. He was like this when he was weak," Shen Tu said and smiled at Evil Deities.
Evil Deities then smiled at Shen Tu as if they were old friends who had not seen each other for a long time.
He was able to stay on the black ship because of Shen Tu, and Shen Tu showed a considerable degree of patience.
"Where is the evil incarnation?" Fairy Yunxiao asked.
"Come with me, Fairy," the boatman said and nodded to Xiao Lan.
Xiao Lan gently spread her hands, and an invisible space ship enveloped everyone. The next moment, they appeared on the west side of Li Hu Province, where Li Shan and the You Xiong Clan first fought.
When Shinno was chasing the missing ship, he had consumed too much of Gui Qing's strength. Now, Gui Qing was very weak.
Fortunately, he was bound to Li Hu Province. As long as Li Hu Province was not completely destroyed, he would not die.
"Buzz!"
The missing ship descended.
The people of the black ship, Evil Deities, and Fairy Yunxiao appeared in front of Gui Qing.
Gui Qing raised his head and his gaze fell on Shen Tu and Evil Deities.
He felt a different emotion from Shen Tu, and this emotion seemed to be related to him.
And Evil Deities, who had an arrogant face and proudly puffed out his chest, looked at him as if he was looking at himself.
"What do you want?"
Seeing Hei Chuan and the others coming aggressively, Gui Qing knew that they were up to no good.
However, Gui Qing had no sense of belonging to the You Xiong Clan. In principle, he was like a drop of oil floating in the water. It was impossible for him to join any force.
If he was forced to join the black ship …
"This is the incarnation of the evil will of the mother world. You have to swallow him," Shen Tu pointed at Gui Qing and said.

Chapter 3459
Just as Gui Qing was about to struggle, Fairy Yun Xiao waved her horsetail whisk at him.
When the horsetail whisk touched Gui Qing's face, Gui Qing felt his body stiffen.
He wanted to say something, but his mouth remained open. He could not say a word.
"How? Eat him? "Evil Deities looked at Gui Qing and laughed.
Although Evil Deities could not eat human flesh directly, he was very satisfied with Gui Qing.
Gui Qing's aura was almost exactly the same as his. This guy was very suitable to be a part of him.
"Don't you have the ability to devour souls?" Fairy Yun Xiao looked at him strangely.
Shen Tu nodded. "Just absorb his soul."
Gui Qing's body was secondary. The most important thing was to fuse with Gui Qing's soul.
Then, Fairy Yun Xiao stretched out her hand toward Gui Qing's forehead. A faint green light was released from her palm.
Gui Qing could not resist. He only felt a terrifying force coming from Fairy Yun Xiao's hand. His eyes rolled back, and his body trembled involuntarily. Soon, Gui Qing's soul was dragged out of his body.
Usually, a certain form of soul was the same as the body. There were very few exceptions.
However, Gui Qing's soul was very unique. It looked like a cat with its fur standing on end. His body was filled with soul spikes. He struggled wildly in Shen Tu's hand.
Under Fairy Yun Xiao's control, his physical body could not break free, let alone his soul.
Slap!
Fairy Yun Xiao dragged Gui Qing's soul and slapped it into Evil Deities' mind. Gui Qing's soul was stuffed into Evil Deities' mind.
Gui Qing's soul was not weak. At least, he could fight against Evil Deities.
However, Fairy Yun Xiao did not plan to help. She let Evil Deities deal with Gui Qing in his mind …
"Will it be dangerous?" Shen Tu asked.
"So what if it's dangerous?" Fairy Yun Xiao shrugged, looking indifferent.
Seeing Fairy Yun Xiao's indifferent expression, Shen Tu calmed down.
Actually, the Wu Kong Clan's attitude toward Evil Deities was similar to Gui Qing. They were both incarnations of evil and were destined to not be loyal partners of the Wu Kong Clan.
In the end, the Wu Kong Clan needed this identity of theirs, but it made no difference who was in charge.
However, facing Gui Qing, who he had a dispute with, Shen Tu's mentality was naturally very complicated.
"Moreover, we have already given so many Evil Deities. If we can't even deal with Gui Qing, who has just revived, we might as well support him," Fairy Yun Xiao said.
Suddenly, a pained expression appeared on Evil Deities' face. Their facial muscles began to twitch, making all sorts of strange expressions.
After a few breaths, Evil Deities closed their eyes and calmed down.
After a while, the Evil Deities finally opened their eyes. Their pupils were as deep as an ancient well, so deep that one couldn't see the bottom.
"You won?" Shen Tu asked.
Shen Tu still felt that these were Evil Deities, but the eyes of these Evil Deities were filled with many inexplicable meanings.
Evil Deities stretched his body and looked at Shen Tu with a strange look in his eyes, "I unearthed some strange memories."
"What strange memories?" Shen Tu asked, puzzled.
Evil Deities suddenly reached out and wiped Shen Tu's face. He pulled her straight black hair in front of his face and sniffed it. He frowned and said, "It's still a strong corpse stench. Why does that guy think it's fragrant?"
"You, why do you have memories of the past?" Shen Tu frowned. His porcelain-white face had a hint of redness.
The other Black Ship members also understood.
Evil Deities' action was what Gui Qing used to do to Shen Tu. However, Evil Deities obviously didn't like the corpse stench on Shen Tu like Gui Qing did.
Evil Deities shrugged, "I found some dusty memory fragments when I fused with his soul. I guess this guy doesn't even know about the existence of these memory fragments."
After the incarnation of the evil will reappeared, the memories of the previous life should have disappeared. This was almost the same view in the Mother World, because the flames of memory had completely disappeared.
But now it seemed that Gui Qing's memories of the previous life had been preserved through some method.
"That's not important. What's important is how much influence you have on the Mother World," Fairy Yun Xiao next to him asked.
Controlling the Mother World was equivalent to controlling the core of the Chaos. Many Immortal Realm powerhouses thought so.
But controlling the largest Land of Origin in the Chaos was easier said than done!
What's more, no incarnation had ever appeared in the Land of Origin. In theory, it was impossible to control.
However, many Land of Origin gave birth to the incarnation of the evil will. For example, the Land of Origin in Luo Zheng's body was so small, but it still gave birth to the Evil Deities.
Whether it was the Black Ship or the You Xiong Clan, the purpose of cultivating the incarnation of the evil will was the same. They wanted to control the Mother World through them.
"My influence on the Mother World …"
Evil Deities narrowed his eyes and looked at the World Wall not far away.
After he swallowed Gui Qing's soul, he felt that the tattered World Wall had a connection with him.
Evil Deities stretched out his hands towards the World Wall …
The translucent, amber-colored boundary wall suddenly started flowing like melted honey.
Under the control of Evil Deities, the World Wall that stretched for hundreds of miles quickly collapsed and flowed …
At this moment, the faces of Shen Tu and the others from the Black Ship changed. Even Fairy Yun Xiao was surprised.
When Gui Qing was born, he was also carefully cultivated by the Black Ship.
At that time, Gui Qing told the people of the Black Ship that he felt there were a total of five stages to improve himself.
When Gui Qing reached the second stage, he could already merge himself with the World Wall and open and close the World Wall of the Mother World …
He said that when he reached the third stage, he could control the World Wall at will. At that time, his strength would increase by another level. Even an Immortal Realm powerhouse would find it difficult to be his enemy.
Unfortunately, Gui Qing had already rebelled when he was about to step into the third stage. In the end, he was killed by the joint forces.
The second Gui Qing was cultivated by Ji Xuanyuan. He was still in the early stage of the second stage. In the end, he was directly swallowed by Evil Deities.
Logically speaking, even if Evil Deities merged, they could only reach the second stage at most. But now, Evil Deities' performance exceeded the expectations of the Black Ship.
Whoosh!
At this moment, a loud whistling sound came from the sky.
The Grandmaster of Heaven was once again smashed down by Ah Huo from the sky, and he happened to fall in the direction of the World Wall.
When Evil Deities saw this scene, a black light flashed in its eyes, and it stretched out its hand.
The World Wall that was originally flowing like liquid suddenly began to condense and turn into a long spear!
A section of the World Wall that was hundreds of miles wide condensed into a nine-foot-nine long spear. Its strength was almost unimaginable!
After Ah Huo smashed the Grandmaster of Heaven down, his figure flashed and he chased after him.
With Ah Huo's instantaneous teleportation speed, even Fairy Yun Xiao would find it difficult to catch him, let alone ordinary Immortal Realm powerhouses.

Chapter 3460
Ah Huo didn't expect anyone else to attack.
In Ah Huo's eyes, other than Tongtian and the Chaos Ancient Gods, no one else could fight.
After teleporting, she was ready to pounce to the left. That was where the Prime of Tongtian had fallen.
Who knew that as soon as she got out of the spatial passage, a long spear would come at her!
"Boom!"
The long spear was extremely sturdy, and the power it contained was not weak.
Under such a bombardment, Ah Huo was directly smashed back into the spatial passage.
The spatial passage couldn't withstand Ah Huo's impact and was directly shattered by her. The area where she was sent flying was immediately covered with spiderweb-like spatial cracks.
Evil Deities' hand made a circle in the air, and the long spear formed by the boundary wall also flew a few rounds in the air.
"Whoosh …"
After the long spear adjusted its direction, it shot toward Ah Huo again.
Ah Huo was caught off guard. When she reacted and looked at the long spear again, her face was blooming with a fierce light.
"Whoosh!"
She swung her hand backward, and Luo Zheng returned and smashed the long spear hard.
Ah Huo was surprised that the long spear didn't shatter, but that was all. When she was fully focused on defense, the long spear didn't pose any threat to her.
However, the moment Ah Huo smashed the long spear to the side, Tongtian had already appeared behind Ah Huo.
"Buzz!"
A dot of green light bloomed, and a thin chain was faintly contained within the green light. It was the Heart-Piercing Lock.
As long as three Heart-Piercing Locks were planted, the battle would be over.
However, Ah Huo's speed and strength far exceeded Tongtian's expectations. After that round of battle, Tongtian was completely at a disadvantage.
Just as he was about to be defeated, the long spear suddenly appeared to help him.
Tongtian's sword had already pierced Ah Huo's body. Just as he was about to plant the first chain, Ah Huo's body suddenly underwent a drastic change.
Her eyes, ears, mouth, and nose spewed out flames, and most of her body turned into flames. The flames wrapped around Luo Zheng and quickly moved to the side.
This scarlet flame was Ah Huo's true form.
Genryū lifeforms had no concept of lifespan. From the moment they were born, they would constantly grow stronger, and the process of growing stronger was often through devouring each other.
For Ah Huo to grow to this point, it was unknown how many fire spirits of the same race she had devoured. She herself was the amalgamation of various forms of fire energy.
Huo also sensed the power of the Sect Leader's sword and wanted to transform into his true form to avoid it.
However, she had forgotten that she was carrying Luo Zheng's energy black hole.
"Hua!"
All the flames that came into contact with Luo Zheng were rapidly absorbed by Luo Zheng!
Ah Huo sensed that her body was being absorbed by Luo Zheng. Her face darkened in the flames, but thinking that the amount of energy Luo Zheng could absorb might be limited, she endured it.
But in the next moment, when she felt Luo Zheng's crazy absorption speed, she finally turned pale with fright. If she continued to be in contact with Luo Zheng, she was afraid that her whole body would be absorbed by Luo Zheng …
[What's wrong with this kid …]
After descending into Ji Xuanyuan's body, Ah Huo suppressed part of her energy.
Even an Immortal Realm expert couldn't withstand the outburst of her energy.
However, Luo Zheng absorbed it directly. This was too exaggerated and unreasonable …
In desperation, Ah Huo could only give up on Luo Zheng. At this time, Luo Zheng had already absorbed more than a third of Ah Huo's body.
In the Eternal Godly Court, more than thirty light pillars suddenly rose from the diagram surrounding the Holy Grail!
The various types of fire energy were mostly destructive, and the light pillars that rose were all in the destruction quadrant. Only a few of them were in the creation quadrant and the life quadrant.
The four bronze statues were already numb to the light pillars that rose from time to time. The kid in the cup was clearly different from any other lifeform they had seen before.
But now, more than thirty light pillars had suddenly appeared, shocking them once again.
"This guy is a monster …"
"Is he trying to create a miracle?"
"Even the pillar man hasn't reached this step!"
After a series of shocks, they became more and more expectant of Luo Zheng.
…
"Whoosh …"
After dodging the Sect Leader's sword, Ah Huo condensed again. As soon as she took shape, Ah Huo's figure flashed, and she grabbed at the falling Luo Zheng.
But how could the Sect Leader let Ah Huo succeed so easily? The Qingping Sword trembled slightly, and a green light shot straight at Ah Huo like a poisonous snake.
Ah Huo was about to grab Luo Zheng again, but the green light was right between her and Luo Zheng.
Ah Huo clenched her teeth and had to withdraw her hand. Then, she used Space Diversion again. This time, she appeared on the other side of Luo Zheng.
However, the Sect Leader did the same. The Qingping Sword trembled again, and a green light blocked Ah Huo's momentum again.
While the two of them were fighting, Luo Zheng was falling at a rapid speed.
The moment Luo Zheng was about to hit the ground, a figure poked his head out of the spatial fluctuation. A pair of hands suddenly appeared, grabbed Luo Zheng, and dragged him into the spatial tunnel.
The Sect Leader of Tongtian and Huo were stunned when they saw this.
"Fuxi!"
The Sect Leader's face turned gloomy again.
Because of Ah Huo, he didn't have time to take the lives of these two imposters. It could be considered as giving them a chance to live.
He didn't expect that not only did they not escape, but they also attacked when the two tigers were fighting!
How bold of them!

Among the four origin bloodlines, the Infinite Multiplication, the Myriad Dao Life and Death, and the Infinite Time and Space were all traceable. He could roughly make out some outlines. However, the meaning of the "Ultimate Cycle" was unclear. It was completely shrouded in mist, and not even the slightest outline could be seen.
Now that he had the Origin Divine Blood as his backing, Luo Zheng was curious and naturally wanted to comprehend it.
Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He was standing on the bronze mountain, right in the middle of the Self-Ouroboros.
"Whoosh …"
An invisible fluctuation transmitted from the Self-Ouroboros. The statue of the Self-Ouroboros began to move. The snake's head began to swallow its own tail.
Although the snake's head was swallowing its own body, its tail was also constantly growing. In the end, swallowing itself like this was an endless cycle.
When the Self-Ouroboros began to rotate, Luo Zheng also felt that the Origin Divine Blood in his body was responding.
A strange picture surrounded Luo Zheng. A constantly cycling world appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Everything in that world grew and multiplied endlessly. In the end, it perished, and then a new world was born.
"Is this a Chaotic World?"
"Or is it similar to a Chaotic World?"
Just as Luo Zheng was puzzled, several more cycling worlds appeared in front of Luo Zheng.

Chapter 3470
"There's more than one chaotic world," Luo Zheng murmured.
Some key information was revealed in this picture.
The world was constantly being rebuilt, destroyed, rebuilt, destroyed …
Under Luo Zheng's careful observation, he quickly discovered something amiss.
Whether these worlds were rebuilt or destroyed, the internal energy was constant. But when each world was destroyed, there would be an extra part of energy, and this part of energy was not small.
If there were ten units of energy in a world, then every time a world was built to be destroyed, there would be three more units!
It might not sound like much, but if one thought about it carefully, if the chaotic world had an extra one-third of energy from its birth to its destruction, what kind of terrifying number would it be?
However, the extra energy didn't go into the new world. Instead, it disappeared without a trace.
"Where did this part of energy go?"
Luo Zheng frowned as he observed these pictures.
Under such careful observation, Luo Zheng found a clue.
The moment these worlds were destroyed, there would be a violent energy fluctuation. At the moment of the energy fluctuation, there would be a strong energy escaping from the bottom. It seemed to be sucked away by some means!
Because this was a virtual picture, Luo Zheng could only make a limited judgment.
However, Luo Zheng already had a new guess in his mind. He asked the Heavenly Lord of the Origin, "Senior Heavenly Lord of the Origin, what do you think is the meaning of the chaotic world?"
When everything in the world existed, there was a most primitive meaning.
The chaotic world could not be an exception. Obviously, it needed a reasonable explanation.
"Was it the Moment Civilization that wanted to expand its power?" The Heavenly Lord of the Origin had already noticed that Luo Zheng seemed to have come to some conclusion, so he just asked tentatively.
"If it was just to expand its power, after building the chaotic world, it could have bred its own people here. There would be no place for the Elemental Spirit Civilization in the chaotic world, and there would not be the emergence of all kinds of dominator civilizations later," Luo Zheng said.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin nodded. In fact, Luo Zheng's conclusion had appeared a long time ago, but there were differing opinions. Some people said that the Moment Civilization was the sower of civilization. Because it was too powerful, it only gave without asking for anything in return.
There were also people who said that the Chaos World was a world that was accidentally born. It was also the only unique world that could constantly be reborn from destruction. There were all kinds of hypotheses.
However, Luo Zheng obviously had a different understanding of the Ultimate Cycle, so the Heavenly Lord of the Origin did not interrupt Luo Zheng.
"I'm afraid that the Moment Civilization or the Four Spirit Gates built Chaos in order to absorb the energy of the Chaos World. Moreover, they must have built more than one Chaos World. That way, they could continuously obtain a huge amount of energy," Luo Zheng said as he looked at the scene in the distance.
Yuanshi Tianzun frowned and asked, "But the energy in the Chaos should be constant. Absorbing a part of it will reduce a part of it. Since the Chaos Era, the total amount of Chaos should not have decreased …"
"What I see is a decrease. Every chaotic era will increase the energy by a third, and when it is destroyed, it will decrease by a third. This is the only way to achieve a balance," Luo Zheng explained.
Hearing Luo Zheng's explanation, the Heavenly Lord of the Origin's body suddenly shook and his eyes widened. At the same time, he transmitted Luo Zheng's voice to the other heroes. Then came Goddess Nuwa's laughter, which contained a trace of complacency.
After hearing Goddess Nuwa's voice, Luo Zheng showed a puzzled look. "Goddess Nuwa, what are you laughing at?"
"You can ask the Heavenly Lord of the Origin," Goddess Nuwa replied.
The Heavenly Lord of the Origin sighed and immediately said, "The wisest person in the Human Tribe should be Goddess Nuwa. She is also extremely sensitive to energy …"
Goddess Nuwa had once invented a very unique measuring method to measure the total energy of the entire Chaos.
Everything in the Chaos, from the Land of Origin to the various small and large worlds to the Chaos Clouds, was composed of matter, and matter could also be regarded as a kind of energy.
Goddess Nuwa used a unique method to calculate the total energy of the Chaos and judged that the total amount of Chaos was actually increasing continuously!
Goddess Nuwa's conjecture was regarded as nonsense, and at that time, many Immortal Realm masters sneered at it.
Because every time a Chaos was destroyed, there were Sons of Truth who could survive to the next Chaos. They could directly observe the most original energy of the Chaos — the fusion energy.
And the fusion energy of the Chaos was almost constant in every Chaos, neither more nor less. This conclusion naturally proved that Goddess Nuwa's conjecture was false.
The conclusion of the Sons of Truth was different from that of Goddess Nuwa's measurement. Goddess Nuwa herself was also puzzled. According to her measurement method at that time, the increase of energy after the end of the Chaos Era was one-third!
Who would have thought that a part of the energy of the Chaos would be taken away at the end of the Chaos?
After the Heavenly Lord of the Origin's words, Luo Zheng also said with admiration, "Goddess Nuwa is really smart. I really admire you for coming to the same conclusion just by measuring."
Goddess Nuwa said sarcastically, "It's a pity that some people didn't believe it at that time, but chose to believe in those brainless Sons of Truth!"
In Goddess Nuwa's eyes, the Sons of Truth were just lucky to be chosen by the Chaos as the next guardian of the world. These Sons of Truth were useless except for controlling a part of the truth.
"It's my fault. I apologize to Goddess Nuwa," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin said with a helpless smile.
Goddess Nuwa ignored the Heavenly Lord of the Origin and said directly to Luo Zheng, "If this is the case, then the Momentary Civilization doesn't want the Chaos to complete the Way of the End."
Once the Chaos defeated the "needle" and completed the Way of the End, the Chaos would no longer be destroyed, and the extra energy would only flow inside the Chaos. In this way, the entire Chaos would contain more and more energy, and it would also continue to expand and expand.
"In other words, the needle was arranged by the Momentary Civilization," Goddess Nuwa murmured again.
When she came to this conclusion, Luo Zheng and the Heavenly Lord of the Origin's eyes flashed.
The destroyer was the creator …
Rebirth in destruction, growth after rebirth, and destruction after growth …
The "Ultimate Cycle" in front of them was probably not some kind of magical power that could be used, but a kind of education method engraved in the blood, telling the descendants of the Momentary Civilization how to survive.
"But these are still just your conjectures, Goddess Nuwa," the Heavenly Lord of the Origin said.
Goddess Nuwa blinked and didn't speak. Every step of her thought was logical, but without direct evidence, it could only be an illusory conjecture.

Chapter 3471
Within the Xuan Liang world …
Some of the dots of light were fixed in place, while others were constantly jumping around.
Some complicated things could be expressed with dots of light, but they were often very simple.
For example, a person would only have thirty to forty dots of light …
But some simple things would require countless dots of light to express themselves. For example, a rope could be made up of three to four hundred dots of light.
Luo Nian had originally wanted to compile these patterns so that Jiang Ziya and the others could learn more quickly and adapt to the Xuan Liang world.
After trying for a while, Luo Nian decided to give up.
No matter how omniscient and omnipotent Jiang Ziya seemed, once he entered the Xuan Liang world, he was like a cat that had fallen into water.
Even though Jiang Ziya was incredibly intelligent, he was like a three year old in the face of the complex dots of light.
After crossing the grasslands, Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya had discovered another world that was similar to the Chaos Realm. Jiang Ziya felt like he had discovered a new continent, and he became even more passionate about the Xuan Liang world.
Since there was a second world, there would definitely be a third, a fourth …
There were living creatures in these worlds, and there were clues.

Although Jiang Ziya was powerful, it was still difficult to deal with strange creatures in the Xuan Liang world.
But since he was already here, Luo Nian didn't want to back down. He was also very interested in this strange yet familiar world.
Luo Nian climbed up the mountain path for quite a distance. Just as he was about to go around the mountainside, he suddenly saw a pair of bright red eyes not far away. Surprisingly, it was a face that looked like an ape's.
The other party had clearly noticed Luo Nian, and with a whoosh, it jumped out from a crack in the mountain.
This creature was tall and had long arms. It held a large club in its hand, and it clearly didn't have good intentions. Even worse, there wasn't just one ape. More than ten apes jumped out from the crack in the mountain!
The leader of the gibbons leapt up, brandishing his club as he swung it at Luo Nian.
In the Xuan Liang World, even these creatures without intelligence were very ordinary. They were at least stronger than Luo Nian!

When Jiang Ziya felt Luo Nian's slap, his heart skipped a beat. This meant that Luo Nian was in trouble!
In this state, he was no different from a blind man.
He couldn't use any great divine abilities, but he still had a powerful corporeal body and strength.
In front of him, a large mass of strange light spots that could not be distinguished was swarming towards him. Jiang Ziya simply stretched out his hands and slashed forward.
Jiang Ziya had practiced this action countless times!
This time, it finally came in handy!
How could the burly long-armed ape put Jiang Ziya in its eyes?
While the long-armed ape was swinging its wooden stick at Jiang Ziya, it heard a muffled sound. However, Jiang Ziya's body didn't move at all. How could the long-armed ape know that its full strength was nothing but a mayfly trying to shake a tree?
"Crack!"
Jiang Ziya's palms landed on the gibbon's shoulders. Under the horrifying force, the gibbon watched in horror as its bones were shattered. Its entire body was crushed by the huge force …
The gibbon's miserable state also shocked Luo Nian.
Jiang Ziya could not distinguish the strength of his opponent in the Xuan Liang World. He did not know how to control his strength and directly slapped the gibbon into a pile of minced meat.
The bloody scene shocked the gibbon behind. Although these gibbon had low intelligence, they were not fools. Obviously, the two "humans" in front of them were not people they could provoke. They let out strange cries and crawled into the mountainside.
In the crevice.
After scaring the monkeys away, Luo Nian pulled Jiang Ziya and followed them. They also climbed into the crack along the mountainside.
Not long after they entered the crevice, there was a big cave in front of them.
Luo Nian's eyes suddenly brightened. Was this the third "world"?
With this thought, Luo Nian would not retreat. Without hesitation, he took Jiang Ziya and crawled into the cave.
The cave was not only very deep, but also very spacious. A considerable part of the cave was occupied by the gibbons. Apparently, these gibbons were very afraid of Jiang Ziya. When Luo Nian and Jiang Ziya passed by, the gibbons curled up together and shivered.
They were trembling.
Luo Nian ignored them and continued to walk into the depths of the cave.
When the gibbon saw Luo Nian moving forward, their eyes were full of expectation and pity.
Although they had low intelligence, they knew that they could not go into the depths of the cave.
All the gibbon that entered the cave did not come back. That was the "abyss". If these two humans went in, they would die!
After walking for hundreds of feet, the space in front of them suddenly changed significantly.
The scattered light spots quickly gathered in one direction. The two people quickly turned from light spots into human figures.
"What attacked you just now? Where is this … "
In front of them was another huge space. However, compared to the poisonous fog world, this place was even more spacious. In other words, it was a piece of nothingness!
Jiang Ziya took Luo Nian and kept on moving. Not to mention living creatures, even a piece of soil or a lump of matter did not exist. This was a completely empty world!
The two of them tried to enter the Nirvana Realm with the True Meaning of Dao, but it did not work. The Nirvana Realm did not exist …
"Looks like this world is even worse." Jiang Ziya's face became serious.

The Chaotic World would probably go the same way.

This kind of insignificance was not only in terms of size, but also in terms of momentum and meaning.

Chapter 3473

The four Bloodlines of the Origin Divine Blood are also transformed from the Origin Divine Blood."

Chapter 3474
When the fox girl heard the slightly panicked voice of the enforcer, she also looked at Luo Zheng with a puzzled look.

. divine pressure.

They were formed.

！

Luo Zheng had really obtained the origin divine blood …

Chapter 3476
The writer had never experienced Luo Zheng's journey, so he did not know why Luo Zheng asked such a question.

They are pioneers and destroyers … "the writer replied.
The Four Great Holy Spirits were naturally humans, monkeys, snakes, and birds. In a larger sense, it was difficult to define whether the Four Spirit Gates were good or bad.
"The Ksana Civilization you mentioned should be the founders of the Chaotic World," the writer continued. "The Ksana Civilization does belong to the Four Spirit Gates, but they are only a branch of the Four Spirit Gates!"

The Fishing Drum's Nine Laws: lock, buckle, chop, release, change, clear, suppress, release, twist.
This rhythm was the Eighth Law, the Law of Enlightenment. It was to release the living beings trapped in space!
What the hell …
He was the only one who could use such a technique …
"Sky" could use his own understanding of the truth of space to control it so easily?
"Buzz …"
The Tongtian Sect Leader looked as if he had seen a ghost as he watched "Sky" come out of the Fishing Drum with ease.
As they looked at each other, the two top experts could see the unusual shock on each other's faces.
"Sky" was shocked by Luo Zheng's technique. While the Tongtian Sect Leader was shocked that "Sky" could come out, he was also confused.
What's with "Sky"'s expression?
Why did he also have a confused look on his face?
Did he not come out by himself?
Just as the Tongtian Sect Leader was confused, the Fishing Drum sounded again.
"Dong dong dong, dong dong …"
It was still the eighth law, the release law.
Soon, another figure came out of the Fishing Drum. It was Luo Zheng.
When the Tongtian Sect Leader saw Luo Zheng, some of his doubts were solved. Luo Zheng had woken up!
It seemed that it was Luo Zheng who had released "Sky".
However, he was even more confused. How could Luo Zheng unlock the Heart-Piercing Lock and control the Fishing Drum?
"Luo Zheng?"
"He has woken up?"
Fuxi and Nuwa's expressions were not much better than the Tongtian Sect Leader's.
Luo Zheng's awakening meant that he had left the Immortal Painting and obtained a bloodline. He was also a member of the Immortal Realm.
However, as he had just reached the Immortal Realm, it was difficult for him to use the power of his bloodline. Even if he had woken up now, he would not be able to fight against the Tongtian Sect Leader.
However, Fuxi and Nuwa could not understand why Luo Zheng and "Sky" were able to escape from the Fishing Drum.
"Sky, I've released you. You can make your move now," Luo Zheng said to "Sky".
Hearing this, Fuxi and Nuwa could not help but look at each other, their eyes full of shock.
"Sky was released by Luo Zheng?"
"What on earth happened to Luo Zheng?"

As soon as he finished speaking.
The fluctuation of the Truth of Space surged out of his body.
The bloodline power of a Chaos Ancient God was extremely terrifying. The Tongtian Sect Leader had easily captured "Sky" before because he was unprepared. This was also the reason why "Sky" was angry.
Now that "Sky" had gained his freedom, he felt that he would not repeat the same mistake.
He wanted to kill the Tongtian Sect Leader, and he wanted to kill Luo Zheng too!
"Buzz!"
A huge triangular space suddenly appeared within a radius of ten thousand miles. This triangular space was extremely stable and completely formed a world of its own.

Chapter 3481
One side of this huge triangle glowed with a blue light.

The six Chaos Ancient Gods were not just for show. They relied on the Chaos World to display their Truth Divine Ability, and their power could not be underestimated.

Next was Fuxi and Tongtian. They stabilized themselves in the air almost at the same time.
The slowest was Luo Zheng …
In fact, the moment the spatial laws changed, Luo Zheng's bloodline circulated once. Through the analysis of the Origin God Blood, Luo Zheng understood the essence of this triangular space.
He had obtained the means to break this triangular space, but he still needed to adapt to the spatial laws himself.
Therefore, Luo Zheng fell for more than a hundred meters before he could barely adapt.

The spatial laws changed again!
Everyone started to fall again and adapt to the spatial laws again …
However, just as they adapted, the orange light disappeared and the purple light lit up.
"Kong" had created four sets of spatial laws. The changes in these four sets of spatial laws were not big, but they could change quickly.
When the lights on the four sides of the triangle started to change, the spatial laws were constantly changing.

Nuwa did not know how Luo Zheng had such a heaven-defying ability, but it was most likely related to his bloodline. However, what kind of bloodline could be so heaven-defying?

…
In the space tunnel, Fuxi, Nuwa, and Luo Zheng were flying at high speed.

The opposite truth?
Fuxi was also amused by his own guess, but his intuition was right.

Luo Zheng entered the deepest part of the immortal scroll and successfully came back. The bloodline power he obtained was not ordinary. At least, it was stronger than Mu Ling's.

Chapter 3482
This kid is really …

Feng Ge stood behind Concubine Yan, looking up at the five people in the sky. She frowned, and her tired eyes revealed confusion. She didn't expect the situation to change so quickly.

Chapter 3484
In the huge triangular space, frightening energy flowed crazily.
The black flames seemed to have come from the craziest hell, almost occupying the entire triangular space.
The two huge beings fought in the space like caged beasts!

The endless black flames released from the Six Soul Banner incessantly burned the space, causing many cracks to appear in the surrounding space.
When these cracks expanded to a certain extent, the entire space would collapse.
When the Tongtian Sect Leader regained his freedom, Kong would be no match for him.

As he spoke, Kong's body shook violently, and the cracks in the triangular space quickly recovered!
He could not take down the Tongtian Sect Leader in this space, which meant that he had already lost. The man in front of him was an existence above the Chaos Ancient Gods!
However, Kong had the ability to drag this battle out!

This round of battle allowed Kong to clearly see the gap between him and the Grandmaster of Heaven. He did not want to let the tiger return to the mountain.
The Grandmaster of Heaven said seriously, "Before I destroy Li Mountain, I won't attack you."
"Why should I believe him?" Kong shook his head.
The Prime of Tongtian thought for a while, then said, "Use the Twin Snakes to make an oath."
The Binding Contract Twin Snakes was one of the Nirvana Realm tokens of the ship captain. After making an oath, it would become a great constraint. If the oath was broken, one would have to pay a heavy price.
Kong had naturally heard of the Binding Contract Twin Snakes. His single eye flashed with a noncommittal look. "If you can summon the Twin Snakes, I'll trust you this time."
"I can't, but someone else can," Tongtian said.
Not long after he finished speaking, several black ships emerged from the distant space and stopped at one side of the triangular space.
The boatman in a black cloak walked out of the black ship and lightly knocked on the spatial wall.
Kong hesitated for a while before letting the boatman in.
After using the Binding Contract Twin Snakes to make an oath, the battle between Kong and the Tongtian Sect Leader finally came to an end. The huge triangular space was also removed.
"Sir Sect Leader, are we going to Li Mountain now?" the boatman asked.
"Of course." The Tongtian Sect Leader was a swift and decisive person. Naturally, he would not give Li Shan any chance to catch his breath. He turned to look at "Kong" and said, "Are you coming with me to Li Shan?"
Kong only snorted. A square space enveloped him, and his huge body disappeared in front of the Tongtian Sect Leader and the people on the black ship.
The Tongtian Sect Leader and Kong's contract only stated that they would not attack before Li Mountain was destroyed. After Li Mountain was destroyed, the contract would become null and void. At that time, both sides would not have any scruples. They would probably still fight over Luo Zheng.
Since the Tongtian Sect Leader was in such a hurry to attack Li Mountain, Kong could naturally act as if it had nothing to do with him.
The boatman looked at the place where Kong disappeared and reminded, "Sir Sect Leader, I'm afraid Kong won't leave so easily."
"Of course he won't leave." The Tongtian Sect Leader shrugged, and his gaze became deep. "And he can't leave."
The boatman did not know the meaning of the Tongtian Sect Leader's words. He only nodded and brought the Tongtian Sect Leader onto the black ship.
On another black ship, Evil Deities had been sitting cross-legged. He had fallen into a strange state.
Even if he closed his eyes, he could still see the continents that were pieced together in the distance.
Every time he entered a continent, an invisible tentacle would extend toward him and try to establish a connection with him.
After this connection was established, Evil Deities would be connected to the continent.
Every detail of the continent would appear in his eyes, and the damage he suffered could also be transferred to the boundary wall of the continent.
This was a very practical ability, but Evil Deities was not satisfied with this.
Above Evil Deities' head, there was an even wider space. It was the entire mother world.
He could feel the tentacle that represented the entire mother world, but that tentacle was faintly discernible, as if it was avoiding him.
Touching this tentacle was the target of Evil Deities.
…
Two hours after Nuwa and the others returned to Li Mountain, the entire continent was covered in milky white clouds.
It was as if one was looking down from the highest point of the mother world, one could see the largest mountain in the mother world being quickly covered in 'white snow'.
This layer of clouds spread out like a waterfall, forming a magnificent spectacle.
However, the people of the various mountains and sects in Li Mountain did not feel gratified. Instead, they were deeply worried.
That was the Fog Shield Formation of Great Cloud Mountain, the most powerful formation in Li Mountain. It had not been activated for many years, which meant that Li Mountain had encountered a strong enemy.
Not long ago, when Li Mountain was continuously provoked by the You Xiong Clan, some news spread like wildfire. The temporary peace in the mother world was probably over.

Twenty pages of stairs …
Thirty …

Only when their clansmen had fallen somewhere would they be able to rush over and take action.
Although the flames within the black snakes were fierce, Grandmaster Tongtian had split them into three hundred portions. Even Saint Soul experts could resist them if they were careful.
However, while the black snakes were killed, their clansmen suffered heavy casualties.
After five minutes, everyone on Li Mountain was in a frenzy.
Nuwa did some calculations. Seven hundred clansmen had died in this wave of snakes. Many of them were elites …
Although there were many clansmen on Li Mountain, the ones who died were her people. One could imagine how she felt.
Luo Yan quickly sensed Li Mountain's panic.
After all, the Mountain Lords and Gate Masters had contributed nearly half of the energy. They had gone to capture the black snakes, so the energy that the energy axis could convert was naturally unstable.
Fortunately, Luo Yan was very familiar with it. After constant adjustments, he could barely suppress the crazily growing blue pillars.
When the Mountain Lords and Gate Masters returned to their original positions, the energy supplied to the energy axis stabilized.
Luo Zheng, who was at Deep Mist Peak, also saw the commotion. However, Nuwa had said before the battle that they should listen to her commands. Luo Zheng did not act rashly.
Phoenix, Gan Gaohan, and the others only injected energy from another peak. They did not participate in the battle directly.

According to Nuwa's plan, as long as they could lure a Primal Chaos Ancient God into the Li Mountain Pit and kill him, the plan would be considered a success. But now, it seemed impossible.
Just as Nuwa and Fuxi were thinking of a solution, the Grandmaster of Heaven waved the Six Souls Banner again. Hundreds of black snakes that he created sneaked into Li Mountain again.
After the time it takes for an incense stick to burn …

The number of core disciples was always limited. How could they withstand such a massacre?
Nuwa's worried face suddenly turned ruthless. She turned to look at Fuxi and said, "We can kill our way out if we give our bodies to our true body."
After entering Li Mountain, Nuwa had a detailed discussion with her "main body".
Nuwa quickly accepted her identity. She could feel the personality and aura of the "original body", which was exactly the same as her. It was just that the original body had lived longer and was more knowledgeable.
She didn't mind having her 'true self' take over her body. Besides, her true self was trapped in the 33 Heavens. Right now, her 'true self' was only an energy soul. It couldn't truly control her.

Now that they saw the citizens of Li Mountain being slowly depleted by the Grandmaster of Heaven and could not come up with a solution, they could only change their previous strategy and take the initiative to attack.
The safest method was to let the original body temporarily take over the body.
"There's no need." Fuxi shook his head.
"Huang Xi." Nuwa stared at her husband with a pitiful look in her eyes.
Of course, Nuwa was well aware of Fuxi's arrogance.

With Ji Xuanyuan's strength and Genryū's backing, he was still not Fuxi's match.

Even if that someone else was his true self, he found it hard to accept.

…
The Grandmaster of Heaven was very satisfied with his offensive methods.
Many times, a little trick could often have a good effect.
The people on Li Mountain looked like they were going to die. Naturally, he would not rush in recklessly.
Forcing them out bit by bit and waiting for them to tire themselves out was the best choice.

The layer of white clouds that originally enveloped Li Mountain quickly dissipated, revealing a huge, verdant, and vast mountain range!

Chapter 3499

Chapter 3500

At this moment, a huge figure suddenly appeared in the sky above Li Mountain. It was a creature covered in white fur.

Chapter 3501

Chapter 3502
Extracting the energy from such a huge amount of Deity crystals was an extremely long process.
