The blue energy that flowed out of the Deity crystals flowed towards the center like thousands of small streams. When they gathered at the center, they formed an azure energy river!
The energy river was very gentle. Ordinary people wouldn't be harmed if they swam in it. The surging energy wouldn't harm anyone, much less kill a Primal Chaos Ancient God.
However, when the energy river surged over, the three Sage Soul experts of the Rat Race took action together.
Three identical tokens of the Nirvana Realm appeared above their heads.
These three Paramita Tokens came from the 33 Heavens. However, because they were auxiliary Paramita Tokens, their functions were more functional. They weren't of much help in combat, so very few people fused with them.
However, when these Paramita Tokens interacted with each other, they could greatly compress the energy river!
After the surging energy river was compressed, it once again turned into crystals.
Originally, Deity crystals were a type of ore that contained energy. Now, these crystals were pure energy crystals.
The energy contained within these energy crystals was equivalent to 70 million Deity crystals.
Nuwa had once tried to compress all the Deity crystals to this level. This way, it would be much more convenient to store them. They wouldn't need to occupy such a large space within Li Mountain.
However, after being compressed to such an extent, they were in an extremely unstable state. They couldn't be stored at all!
The river surged as extremely compressed energy crystals floated in front of Rat King Sheng Tian.
Similarly, a Paramita Token appeared above Rat King Sheng Tian's head. This was a living Paramita Token. It was the size of a palm and looked like a black cat.
In reality, this was an Ancestral Chi. It was also a mystical Paramita lifeform of the 33 Heavens.
Although it looked like a ferocious black cat, it was actually a type of energy body. Most of the time, it would take the form of an energy ball. Only when it was hunting would it transform into a black cat.
The Ancestral Chi had a very mystical characteristic. Other Paramita Tokens or Paramita lifeforms could only devour a limited amount of Deity crystals.
The Paramita Tokens of the 33 Heavens could easily absorb millions or tens of millions of Deity crystals. However, absorbing more than 100 million Deity crystals was extremely difficult. Most of them couldn't do it …
But the Ancestor Puppet was different. It could absorb almost limitless divine crystals, and the more divine crystals it absorbed, the stronger it became.
Many people had placed their hopes of obtaining the complete Path of the End on it after discovering this characteristic. Fuxi had also been very enthusiastic about subduing it at first.
Later on, he discovered that the Primogenitor Chi's attack wasn't that strong. It had its own world in its stomach, and the divine crystal it swallowed could only produce destructive power in the world in its stomach. It was impossible for the Primogenitor Chi to swallow the needle.
Fuxi was greatly disappointed, but Nuwa had an idea and gave it to Heavenly Rat King. Together with Heavenly Rat King, she set up such a huge array formation so that it could be put to good use at the crucial moment!
This time, during the war with the Primal Chaos Elder Gods, Nuwa had summoned the Victorious Heavenly Rat King to take charge of the Divine Crystal Formation!
"Heaven Enlightenment, Earth Enlightenment!" Heaven Victorious Rat King instructed in a low voice.
The two Ear Mice beside the Victorious Sky Rat King nodded in acknowledgment and simultaneously took out their Nirvana Token.
Heaven Enlightenment and Earth Enlightenment were the Victorious Sky Rat King's left and right arms. Their role in the Divine Crystal Formation was to trap the opponent. Even if it was only for a short while, it was enough!
"Leng" was still relentlessly bombarding the golden shield.
During the process, Leng could also feel the energy flow within Li Mountain.
He thought that the energy flow was used to stabilize the golden barrier, so it didn't arouse any alarm!
Boom, boom, boom, boom …
The golden barrier shook more and more violently.
The disciples and clansmen of Li Mountain were frantically replenishing divine crystals and injecting energy into the barrier.
Even so, they still couldn't keep up with the consumption of the Misty Mountain Barrier Array.
Every time the huge fist landed in front of Luo Yan, Luo Yan's face turned a shade paler.
But as the controller of the array, she couldn't escape at all.
Under such circumstances, she would die if she tried to escape!
"Senior Heavenly Rat King!"
Luo Zheng anxiously shouted at the Jade Platform.
"Ready!" Heavenly Rat King replied. "Earth Enlightenment, Heaven Enlightenment, do it!"
Kacha!
A large crack suddenly appeared on the mountain wall not far away from the Jade Platform. Heaven Enlightenment had already taken out his Paramita Token. It was a small piece of gold known as the Dazzling Gold. It was a one-time use Paramita Token.
The instant the Dazzling Gold was thrown out, it began to burn rapidly and emit a golden light.
This golden light wasn't blinding. Looking at it with the naked eye, one would find it somewhat beautiful and gentle.
However, the instant the golden light bloomed, it would create an irresistible consciousness storm. No matter how powerful an existence was, they would be affected by this consciousness storm.
When Luo Yan saw the golden light, her mind went blank.
Apart from her, everyone else on Li Mountain was affected by the golden light …
Leng was the same. Even though his soul was far stronger than most Immortal Realm cultivators, he was still unable to resist this consciousness storm.
The only exception was probably Luo Zheng.
He could clearly feel the bloodline in his body ripple. Before he could figure out what had happened, he saw Leng pause in midair.
For experts of this level, even half a breath's delay could be fatal.
Heavenly Rat King and his subordinates had practiced this tactic countless times. The instant Leng paused, a stone fragment hit Leng's body like a hidden weapon.
After the small stone fragment stuck to Leng's body, it emitted circles of black spirals. Meanwhile, the other stone fragment was in Earth Enlightenment's hand. The surface of the stone fragment was also emitting circles of white spirals.
When Earth Enlightenment saw that the black spiral stone fragment had successfully attached itself to Leng's body, he threw the white spiral stone fragment at Heavenly Rat King.
The Ancestor Chi on Heavenly Rat King's head leaped up and swallowed the white spiral stone fragment.
"Exchange!"
At this moment, Earth Enlightenment activated the abilities of the two stone fragments.
These two stone fragments, one black and one white, were known as the Dual Stone Fragments. They could exchange the positions of the objects they were attached to!
After Earth Enlightenment activated this ability, Leng, who was still in a daze outside Li Mountain, was swapped with the black spiral stone fragment into the Ancestor Chi's stomach.
The white spiral stone fragment that was swallowed by the Ancestor Chi appeared in the sky outside Li Mountain and rapidly fell down.
Luo Zheng, who was not affected by the Heaven Dazzling Gold, stared dumbfoundedly at Leng's disappearance. One must know that the Dual Stone Fragments' exchange of positions did not even cause spatial fluctuations. This forced exchange of positions was extremely powerful!
After the Ancestor Chi swallowed Leng, it meowed at the Victorious Sky Rat King and lay on the Rat King's shoulder.

Chapter 3503
"Leng" did not expect Li Shan to have such a plan. Without any resistance, he was pulled into the Ancestor Puppet's stomach.
In Li Shan, only Nuwa and Fuxi were worthy of "Leng"'s attention. The rest were just some small fries.
He did not expect that these "small fries" were not relying on Li Shan but were looking for an opportunity to kill him!
"Phew …"
In front of "Leng's" eyes, countless huge energy storms were spinning at high speed.
When he felt the terrifying energy, the contempt in his eyes disappeared. His expression became unprecedentedly serious.
"Where is this? Is there such a place in the mother world? "
There were many dangerous places in the mother world. However, these dangers were targeted at the true gods and the Nirvana Realm.
For beings like "Leng", there was no place in the Chaos that he could not go. There were also no dangerous places that could threaten his life.
However, today, there was one that appeared out of nowhere …
"Leng's" first reaction was to think that this was an illusion and that everything was an illusion. However, after a simple analysis, he realized that the roaring energy in this world was real!
"Weng!"
A strong spatial wave was released from "Leng"'s body. "Leng" wanted to leave this world by force.
However, the Ancestor Puppet's stomach was a space of its own. Spatial supernatural powers were ineffective here!
"Oh no …"
Realizing that something was wrong, "Leng" saw an energy storm coming toward him.
"Leng's" ice armor could withstand even Luo Yan's attack. This was because any energy that came into contact with the ice armor would be affected by the extremely low temperature of the ice armor.
The stronger the energy, the higher the temperature. When the temperature dropped, the energy itself would become extremely inactive.
In theory, his ice armor was the nemesis of these energy storms!
However, the intensity of these energy storms was still far beyond "Leng's" expectations. At first, the energy storms were blocked by the ice armor. However, when the energy storms scraped against the ice armor, they left thin lines.
At first, there were dozens of lines. Then, there were hundreds and thousands of lines. The number of lines increased rapidly!
When the number of lines accumulated on the ice armor reached hundreds of thousands, the entire armor shattered!
"Condense!"
Leng was already prepared. He condensed the ice armor again.
The shattered ice armor looked brand new and condensed on the surface of his body.
The first energy storm had also mostly dissipated, and they could be considered to have withstood it.
But what followed was a second, a third, and a fourth energy storm …
There were hundreds and thousands of energy storms in this world. At the edge of the world, the pure energy was like a thick wall, and more energy storms were being generated from the wall.
Leng gritted his teeth as he observed this incredible world. He felt like he was going crazy.
Even if it was a Nirvana Realm Token, it was impossible for it to explode with such power. How did Li Shan do it?
"Hua!"
The ice armor shattered once again.
Although the armor could be condensed at will, it would still consume the Power of Truth.
Now, the Power of Truth of 'Cold' was getting weaker and weaker. Every time he condensed the ice armor, it would become thinner and thinner. It was getting weaker and weaker!
The energy storm raged inside the Progenitor Puppet's stomach, but there was no movement outside.
It curled up on the Heaven Winning Rat King's shoulder, motionless as if it was in a deep sleep.
The Heavenly Rat King placed a finger on the Primogenitor Chi's head. Through this method, the Heavenly Rat King could observe the situation inside the Primogenitor Chi's stomach.
"King, how is it?" Earth Enlightenment asked. After spending so much effort, these rats were naturally curious.
"It shouldn't be able to hold on," the Heavenly Rat King said.
Although the Great Godly Crystal Array was designed to be able to kill a Primordial God, it was still very powerful.
However, after killing such a powerful existence, the Heavenly Rat King still felt somewhat nervous. That was an existence that controlled the truth.
Heaven Enlightenment and Earth Enlightenment nodded and waited silently at the side.
At the top of Li Shang, Luo Zheng and Nuwa also heard the Heavenly Rat King's voice. The siblings heaved a sigh of relief.
The Primordial God was finally restrained …
In comparison, Luo Zheng's state of mind was much calmer than the Heavenly Rat King's.
Although Luo Zheng had lived much shorter than the Heavenly Rat King, the things he came into contact with were not on the same level as the Heavenly Rat King.
Over countless divine epochs, many Scions of Truth had died. On the level of the Xuanliang World, the Scions of Truth were nothing.
However, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan still paid close attention to the situation inside Li Mountain. Before confirming that the Primordial God had died, everything could change. Naturally, they had to be on guard.
After about five minutes, the Heavenly Rat King frowned slightly. The sleeping Primordial Chi also suddenly opened its eyes.
"Hmm?"
The Primordial Chi felt that the world in its body was somewhat unusual.
Just as it was about to let the energy in its body become more intense, its stomach suddenly swelled, becoming a ball that was more than 60 meters wide.
Its rapidly expanding body even knocked the Heavenly Rat King, Heaven Enlightenment, and Earth Enlightenment out!
"Hu … hu …"
Before the Heavenly Rat King could stabilize himself, the Primordial Chi's body rapidly swelled again, from 60 meters to 300 meters wide!
The space inside Li Mountain was only 500 meters tall, and the Primordial Chi's body occupied more than half of it. The Primordial Chi still had an innocent expression on its face as it let out a low whimper.
"King!"
"Why is this happening?"
Heaven Enlightenment and Earth Enlightenment also knew that something had happened.
The Heavenly Rat King also shook his head, looking like he was out of his wits.
The Primordial Chi's cold intent spread out as its body swelled again. This time, it swelled to 50,000 meters!
"Boom …"
The cave inside Li Mountain exploded, and the cracks on the outside spread out in all directions.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng, who was on the Jade Stage, flew up, grabbed Luo Yan and said, "Let's go!"
"But …"
Luo Yan was controlling the Cloudy Mountain Fog Shroud Array, and she could not leave without permission.
"If we don't go, we will lose our lives!"
Luo Zheng's tone was very resolute and did not allow any resistance.
Luo Yan hesitated for a moment before pointing to a mountain peak in the distance. "Let's go there first!"
That was the Bird's Eye Peak, and the second array core of the Cloudy Mountain Fog Shroud Array was there. She brought the golden robe array to the Bird's Eye Peak, and she could still control the entire Cloudy Mountain Fog Shroud Array.
In a moment of desperation, Luo Zheng could only agree to her request!
Just as he brought his sister away, the Primordial Chi's body swelled again. This time, it swelled from 50,000 meters to 300,000 meters.
The Primordial Chi did not have the Rage God Body, and its body could not hold on any longer. Its round belly directly exploded.
Leng's huge body came out of it, and it once again returned to being a million meters tall monster. The entire center of Li Mountain was overturned by it!
Not only that, but a strange phenomenon appeared on Leng's body.
All the rocks and trees near its body seemed to become weightless, and were fixed in the space around its body.
After a while, these rocks and trees turned into countless white dots of light, and then disappeared completely.
Luo Yan was still looking at this strange scene with a surprised expression. Luo Zheng's heart was like a mirror. He had seen the same scene in Yu Sheng-tian, and that was Leng Prison.

Chapter 3504
Any matter that fell into the Cold Prison would take on a completely different form. Energy was no exception.
No matter how strong the energy in the Primogenitor Chi's stomach was, it could not cross the Cold Prison and cause damage to Cold.
Nuwa and Rat King Sheng Tian did not expect such a situation to occur.
They did not know about the existence of the Cold Prison and had never seen Cold use such an ability.
If it was Void or Ruin, they would have died in the Primogenitor Chi's stomach. Yet, Cold was the first to attack.
Cold looked down and cast its gaze on a piece of rock inside Li Mountain. "Rat King Sheng, is this your method of colluding with Li Mountain?"
Rat King Sheng stood on the rock and looked at Void with a gloomy gaze.
He had once claimed to be the richest existence in his mother world. However, the massive amount of God crystals did not bring about any changes to the Rat Race. That was why Rat King Sheng wanted to use these God crystals.
The entire God Crystal array was set up flawlessly with Nuwa and Fuxi's help. In fact, it was progressing very smoothly.
However, Cold still managed to break through this dangerous situation …
When such terrifying energy approached Cold, it turned into white light and disappeared. Rat King Sheng could not imagine how powerful this ability was.
"If I did not comprehend the Cold Prison, I might really die here today. What a pity …"
"Leng" extended a finger toward the Victorious Heavenly Rat King on the gravel. Circles of white aura swirled around the tip of his finger. Clearly, he was going to execute the Victorious Heavenly Rat King.
Whoosh!
The cold energy condensed into a white arrowhead and shot straight at Rat King Sheng.
Rat King Sheng was completely disheartened. He did not plan to escape.
"King!"
"Run!"
Tian Wu, Di Wu, and the other three Rat Kings rushed over.
These five Rat Kings were Rat King Sheng's trusted subordinates. They were absolutely loyal to Rat King Sheng. How could they bear to see him die?
"Divine Picture Shield!"
A purple round shield appeared above Tian Wu.
The Dazzling Gold was a Paramita token that Tian Wu fused with the God Crystal array. This Divine Picture Shield was Tian Wu's natal Paramita token.
As a defensive Paramita token of Trayastrimsa Heaven, any attack that touched the Divine Picture Shield would lose a third of its original power.
Even if it was an attack from a peak Immortal realm expert, Tian Wu was confident that he could withstand it.
"Boom!"
The white arrowhead didn't stop after coming into contact with the Heavenly Diagram Shield, and it drilled into Tian Wu's chest. Tian Wu's entire body instantly froze, turning into an ice sculpture.
Behind Tian Wu, Di Wu threw a Dual Tracking Stone Piece at the white arrowhead. When one of the stone pieces touched the arrowhead, Di Wu switched the direction of the two stone pieces.
After the white arrowhead changed its direction, it shot into the rock in another direction.
That circle of rocks instantly became hard and brittle. With just a slight vibration, it turned into a pile of ice shards.
Seeing that Tian Wu had fallen, the Victorious Sky Rat King, who was stunned on the spot, finally reacted. Its face was filled with grief. It suddenly took out its other two Nirvana Tokens, which were two shining sharp knives.
Just the reflection of the blade's light had left countless deep cuts on the surface of the nearby rocks!
Heaven Victorious Rat King knew that it would be a dead end if it faced Leng, but it had no reason to escape. It could only face him head-on!
"Useless scoundrel!"
"Leng" clearly did not take the Heaven Winning Rat King seriously. He raised his hand, intending to kill him.
At this moment, several golden sabers of light whizzed over from afar, heading straight for Leng.
"Whoosh … Whoosh …"
The moment the golden sabers of light fell into the Cold Prison, they turned into countless white light spots and disappeared.
Luo Yan was on Bird's Eye View Peak, using all his strength to glare at "Leng".
Luo Zheng knew the secret of the Cold Prison. He also knew that the golden sabers of light released by the energy shaft were useless against "Leng". If he wanted to break through the Cold Prison, the energy shaft was far from enough.
However, Luo Yan suddenly attacked, and Luo Zheng did not have time to stop him.
"Leng's" attention was originally on the Victorious Sky Rat King, but now Luo Yan had successfully attracted his attention.
"Little human girl, your courage is commendable …"
"Leng's" gorilla-like face revealed an ugly smile. Then, his huge body flashed, and he was already heading toward Luo Yan.
"Hum!"
As usual, Luo Yan released a golden shield.
After the Eared Mice's torment, the Misty Mountain Formation's energy had been replenished. The golden shield was also very solid.
However, this time, Leng only extended a finger. When his finger was still some distance away from the golden shield, the shield immediately turned into white light spots and dissipated.
Then, he once again flashed a terrifying smile at Luo Yan, then threw a punch at Luo Yan.
He knew that his fist would not touch Luo Yan. Before he could touch Luo Yan, the Cold Prison would transform him into the lowest form of energy and make him disappear.
Strictly speaking, he did not disappear. Instead, he transformed into a different form under extremely low temperatures. This form was very similar to the form outside the Primal Chaos Realm.
"Big brother …."
At this moment, Luo Yan realized something and instinctively called out.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body suddenly swelled. He had activated his Rage God's Body.
In truth, Luo Zheng did not want to fight a Primal Chaos God. Before he figured out the Origin God's Blood, his strength was comparable to that of an ordinary Immortal.
But now, he had to bite the bullet ….
Apart from using his own strength, he could only place his hopes on the Origin God's Blood.
"I hope you … I hope you understand your … situation!"
Luo Zheng's body rapidly swelled to a height of more than four hundred thousand feet. Like a huge mountain, he blocked Luo Yan's path.
Compared to Leng, who was more than a million feet tall, Luo Zheng was still a head shorter.
When Leng's fist came crashing down, he saw Luo Zheng transform into a huge monster. The smile on his ferocious face became even more intense.
This is the brat that Ruin mentioned, right?
I heard he wants to capture him alive?
He's courting death. Don't blame me ….
Even Leng could not control the Cold Prison with ease. Anything that came into contact with the Cold Prison would turn into countless light spots. Leng could not save himself. The moment Luo Zheng came into contact with the Cold Prison, his fist should have turned into light spots and scattered.
However, what happened next was beyond Leng's expectations.
"Boom!"
With a muffled sound, Luo Zheng's fist collided with Leng's fist.
Under the powerful recoil, Leng actually retreated a distance.
Leng's strength was naturally much greater than Luo Zheng's. However, he did not use any strength because there was no need to.
Now, he stared at Luo Zheng with an incomprehensible gaze, as if Luo Zheng was some kind of magical creature.
"You, you … why …."
It was because he understood the nature of the Cold Prison that Leng was so shocked that no one else was.

Chapter 3505
Even in Yu Sheng-tian, the Cold Prison was an absolute forbidden area. Luo Zheng did not dare to trespass.
Only a heaven-defying existence like Bofan dared to stay in the Cold Prison.
The Cold Prison was terrifying not only because of the extremely low temperature, but also because the creatures of the Chaotic World seemed to disappear immediately after turning into light spots.
To the creatures of the Xuanliang World, the Cold Prison was not that scary. Even the weaker creatures of the Xuanliang World would only be frozen temporarily when they fell into the Cold Prison. When they left the Cold Prison, their frozen state would be removed and they would be able to regenerate.
Lively as a dragon.
Luo Zheng's attack just now was a gamble. Although the Origin God's Blood could restrain all kinds of energy, the Cold Prison was very special. Luo Zheng himself could also be dissolved in it.
The result was not surprising. The moment he came into contact with the Cold Prison, the temperature of the Cold Prison began to rise rapidly.
"You know this is the Cold Prison, and you can even break it?" After holding back for a while, Cold was finally able to speak a complete sentence.
"There are many Cold Prisons in the upper levels of Yu Sheng-tian." Luo Zheng smiled. As for whether he could break the Cold Prison, there was no need to answer.
Cold carefully looked at Luo Zheng, the human whose life it was worried about. "It seems that I worried too much. I'm afraid it will take a lot of effort to take you down!"
"Whoosh …"
Cold's huge body leaned forward slightly and directly pounced on Luo Zheng.
After all, it was an old monster that had lived for countless years. How sharp were its eyes?
Luo Zheng did indeed have an incredible method to break through its Cold Prison. Even Cold was convinced by his ability to do so.
However, it also noticed that Luo Zheng was not very strong. With its physical advantage, Cold was confident that it could take this kid down!
Just as Cold rushed in front of Luo Zheng, the red light in Luo Yan's eyes flashed. Golden blades of light slashed towards Cold's head.
"Leng", who had suffered a loss once, casually slapped out a huge ice sheet, which directly covered Luo Yan's head. At the same time, he grabbed Luo Zheng, spun him in mid-air, and threw him fiercely toward Li Shan.
"Boom …"
Luo Zheng's four hundred thousand zhang tall body smashed through a mountain peak, and his body was embedded in the mountain.
Mount Li's originally sturdy and massive mountain seemed to have turned into soft mud, unable to withstand the destruction of the two massive creatures.
"Hu!"
A white light flickered in Leng's hand, and a slender icicle was condensed. It pierced towards Luo Zheng's head.
Luo Zheng had no time to dodge. He reached out and grabbed the icicle.
As the Origin Divine Blood in Luo Zheng's body fluctuated, the icicle melted into a puddle of water as if it was being roasted.
"Leng" was stunned again. He frowned and stopped using the Power of Truth. Instead, he swung his fists at Luo Zheng!
"Boom boom boom boom boom …"
A huge tremor reverberated throughout Mount Li.
Many people in Mount Li saw this scene but the Mountain Lord, Sect Master, disciples, and clansmen of Mount Li were unable to participate in such a battle. On the other hand, they had to hold their posts and provide Luo Yan with an endless supply of energy!
"Ka!"
The ice covering Luo Yan's head was finally shattered by Luo Yan. When she saw her brother being pressed to the ground, her eyes turned red.
"Weng …"
The energy axis began to vibrate violently.
The energy absorbed through the Cloudy Mountain Fog Barrier Array was being released by Luo Yan to a limited extent.
Even with the help of the Tianzhi Civilization, the energy axis could be converted into a large amount of energy. However, there was still a limit.
The energy that Luo Yan was releasing had clearly exceeded this limit!
"Hua hua hua …"
A huge golden light blade flew towards "Leng" at high speed. When "Leng" felt the power of this golden light blade, he immediately used Cold Prison again.
Cold Prison was useless against Luo Zheng, but it completely countered Luo Yan's energy attack.
There was no sound, no light, no vibration …
When these huge golden light blades entered Cold Prison, they turned into millions of white light spots before dissipating.
Luo Yan's chest heaved up and down. Her face had an unnatural dark red color. This sort of overdraft was extremely damaging to her. For a short period, she could not even use her energy axis. She could only watch as "Leng" punched Luo Zheng.
Previously, when Luo Zheng communicated with the Origin God Blood, the Origin God Blood had strengthened him once.
But this strengthening was limited. At the very least, it could not defend against the attacks of "Leng".
After the Chaotic Origin Spirit became fatigued, Luo Zheng's body began to suffer. "Leng" was not only able to control the temperature, his body was also top-notch amongst the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
At this moment, if Luo Zheng used the Tool of Civilization, the World Inheritance, he might still be able to fight back. Heavenly Sage Primordial Beginning had already suggested this.
This suggestion was rejected by Luo Zheng. Even if he activated the World Inheritance, he might not be a match for "Leng". After all, "Leng" specialized in dealing with energy attacks.
He wanted to make use of this opportunity to force the Origin God Blood!
"Hong hong hong hong hong …"
The way "Leng" attacked was like an enormous gorilla. His thick and solid hands slammed down on Luo Zheng's body, causing Luo Zheng's sternum to break into many pieces and collapse rapidly.
Under the constant attacks, "Leng" felt strange.
Even though he felt that Luo Zheng was weak, he should not have given up struggling …
This brat seemed to have given up resisting under his body …
What was going on?
Just as "Leng" could not figure out what was going on, something even stranger happened. Luo Zheng actually started talking to himself.
"Aren't you afraid of falling into the hands of others?"
"If I were to be defeated, your situation would definitely be worse than now!"
"I believe they will definitely not let you off. You are squandering a rare opportunity to feed them!"
Judging from the information obtained from the executor and the four statues, Luo Zheng was the only existence who had fused with the complete Origin God Blood and could bring it away. No one had ever done so before.
When Luo Zheng communicated with the Origin God Blood, "Leng" was still bewildered.
Even though his fists did not stop, the astonishment in his eyes was getting denser.
Who was he persuading …
Persuading an existence that could deal with him?
At this moment, Luo Zheng's chest suddenly split open …
There was no blood flowing out, only broken ribs spreading out to both sides.
The texture of the broken ribs rapidly changed, like two rows of blades sticking up toward "Leng".
The sudden change made "Leng" stop his fists. He felt an instinctive threat!
If he did not get rid of this brat now, he was afraid that something else would happen!
"Die!"
This time, he used almost all his strength to smash down on Luo Zheng's head.
However, Luo Zheng's face also rapidly changed. His eyes, mouth, and nose quickly sank, and his face took on a horrified expression.
When "Leng's" fist hit Luo Zheng's face, it actually sank into Luo Zheng's head along with his sunken face!

Chapter 3506
Luo Zheng's head "bit" Leng's hand and refused to let go.
"Leng" raised his arm abruptly, and Luo Zheng's body was lifted out of the pit.
"Let go!"
Leng swung his arm in the opposite direction and smashed Luo Zheng's body into Mount Li again.
But Luo Zheng still refused to let go, and the way he did it was so strange that even Leng, who had seen a lot of things, felt a little scared.
Lift up, swing down.
Lift up again, swing down again.
In a few breaths' time, Luo Zheng had smashed a huge pit near the top of Mount Li.
But Leng still couldn't get rid of Luo Zheng's face no matter how hard he tried.
Luo Yan was speechless when she saw her brother being abused by Leng.
Her brother seemed to be able to withstand Leng's attacks, but he couldn't keep being beaten like this.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
When Luo Zheng hit Mount Li, a huge rock about ten feet wide flew to one side and hit the Heavenly Rat King who had just climbed out of Mount Li.
"Pa!"
The Heavenly Rat King's tail whipped gently at a speed invisible to the naked eye, and the huge rock was split in half. The cut surface was as smooth as a knife.
The other Eared Rats followed the Heavenly Rat King. When they saw the devastating battle in front of them, their expressions became very strange.
Luo Zheng was an old friend of the Eared Rats. Everyone present, including the Heavenly Rat King, knew about his various miraculous abilities.
They never thought that Luo Zheng could directly face the Ancient God of Chaos after using the Immortal Painting Scroll!
"Luo Zheng's current state is very strange. He seems to be seriously injured, but he can still hold on to Leng," said one of the Eared Rats.
The Heavenly Rat King nodded and stared faintly at Luo Zheng with his soybean-sized eyes. "His body has become very strange. He doesn't seem to be injured, but his body actively changed into this state …"
"The bloodline affects the body?" Another Eared Rat was thoughtful. It was nothing new for the Immortal Realm to obtain a bloodline that could change the body.
Winning Sky Rat King continued, "That's not the point. Luo Zheng easily resolved Leng's attacks several times. Leng doesn't even dare to use his bloodline power now. That's the most amazing thing!"
The Primal Chaos Ancient Gods relied on their Bloodline Power, which was a Truth Divine Ability. This was the foundation of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
However, when the Power of Truth was ineffective, he could only rely on his physical body.
"Boom boom boom …"
After Leng's repeated attacks, he finally couldn't hold on any longer. He grabbed at the air with his other hand, and a huge ice blade formed under the white mist.
It thought that Luo Zheng had lost his eyes, nose, and mouth, and could only rely on this strange body to hold on. It was impossible for him to dissolve its Power of Truth again.
Just as the ice blade slashed toward Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng was still like a dead man. He only knew how to "bite" Leng's hand tightly and did not react to Leng's other hand.
However, just as the ice blade was about to touch Luo Zheng, one of Luo Zheng's hands seemed to have eyes and accurately pressed on the sharp edge of the ice blade.
"Sizzle …"
The sharp ice blade did not even have time to cut Luo Zheng's hand before it melted completely.
"…"
Leng felt like he was going crazy.
He couldn't shake him off or smash him to death. His divine ability was completely restrained by this guy.
Leng had never encountered such a situation before.
A junior that he didn't even take seriously had become so troublesome. This was something that Leng did not expect.
"I don't believe it!"
Leng seemed to be determined. He dragged Luo Zheng to a mountain peak at the side.
That mountain peak was like a sharp sword. This was the Sword Blade Peak of Li Mountain. The Mountain Lord, Xu Hao, was stationed there.
While Xu Hao led the mountain disciples to guard the peak, he was also silently observing the battle between Luo Zheng and Leng. Seeing Leng bring Luo Zheng over, Xu Hao sensibly gave an order.
"All disciples evacuate the Sword Blade Peak!"
The surrounding mountains of Li Mountain were too close to the center of Li Mountain.
During the battle just now, the disciples on the mountain peak naturally felt the power. Although all of them tried their best to release their energy, it was impossible to say that they were not afraid.
After all, if they were slightly affected by a battle at the level of a Chaos Ancient God, they would be reduced to ashes. Who would be willing to die?
Hence, after Xu Hao gave the order, all the disciples used Transference to disperse in all directions.
A moment ago, there were tens of thousands of people on the Sword Blade Peak. In the next moment, it was empty. The efficiency of the evacuation was indeed terrifying.
Leng rushed to the Sword Blade Peak and stopped in time. He extended his hand and slapped the Sword Blade Wind.
"Sizzle …"
White frost spread along with Leng's palm. In the blink of an eye, the Sword Blade Peak was frozen and became indestructible.
Not to mention the Mountain Master, even if Fuxi and Nuwa were here personally, they would not be able to destroy it.
After freezing the Sword Blade Peak, Leng grabbed Luo Zheng and flew up into the sky!
Heaven Winning Rat King, Luo Yan, Xu Hao, and the others saw Leng's actions and immediately understood his intentions. Leng wanted to drag Luo Zheng down from the sky and nail Luo Zheng to the Sword Blade Peak.
It was not that Leng could not defeat Luo Zheng now. He just did not have a good way to end Luo Zheng's life.
If Luo Zheng fell at high speed and did not have the chance to eliminate the Power of Truth, the Sword Blade Peak would be able to pierce through Luo Zheng.
However, just as Leng aggressively grabbed Luo Zheng and dashed up, Luo Zheng's arm suddenly extended and slapped the Sword Blade Peak. At the same time, he injected a green energy into the Sword Blade Peak.
"Hua …"
The frozen Sword Blade Peak melted in an instant. The surface of the Sword Blade Peak became lush and verdant, turning into a fairyland.
Leng was stunned in mid-air. The expression on his gorilla face was indescribable.
Xu Hao, who was hiding in the distance, did not know whether to laugh or cry when he saw this scene. This Luo Zheng …
"Brother …"
Luo Yan's eyes were also wide open. She felt that what she was seeing was not a battle, but a farce.
Forget about her brother …
Ancient God Leng, whose voice resounded throughout the Primal Chaos, seemed to be the initiator of this farce.
Leng stayed in mid-air for five to six breaths of time. Then, he braced himself and approached the Sword Blade Peak. He slapped the Sword Blade Peak again and injected the Power of Truth.
However, even after a long time, the Sword Blade Peak did not freeze.
Leng lowered his head and saw Luo Zheng's slender and boneless hand quietly sticking to the Sword Blade Peak again …
"Hahahahaha …"
Xu Hao, who was in the distance, could not help but laugh.
On the other side, Heaven Winning Rat King and the other Eared Rats were also laughing.
Even Luo Yan temporarily forgot that her brother was in danger. Her eyes turned into cute crescents.
"Roar!"
